oṃ
ṛgvedasaṃhitā
śrīmatsāyaṇācāryaviracitabhāṣyasametā
atha ṣaṣṭhaṃ maṇḍalam ||
bhāradvāje ṣaṣṭhe maṇḍale ṣaḍanuvākāḥ |
tatra prathame'nuvāke pañcadaśa sūktāni |
tatra ‘tvaṃ hyagne’ iti trayodaśarcaṃ prathamaṃ sūktam |
‘tvaṃ hyagne sasonini bārhaspatyo bharadvājaḥ ṣaṣṭhaṃ maṇḍalapaśyat’ ityanu- krāntatvāt maṇḍaladraṣṭā sa eva bharadvāja ṛṣiḥ |
anādeśaparibhāṣayā triṣṭup |
maṇḍalādiparibhāṣayā agnirdevatā |
pañcāḥ haviṣi avadīyamāne maitrāvaruṇenānuvacanīyamidaṃ manotāsūktam |
sūtritaṃ ca— ‘tvaṃ hyagne prathama ityanvāha’ (āśva. śrau. 3. 6) iti |
prātaranuvāka āgneye kratau traiṣṭubhe chandasi etadādisūktapaṭkaṃ dvitīyavarjam |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘tvaṃ hyagne prathama iti paṣṇāṃ dvitīyamuddharet’ (āśva. śrau. 4. 13) iti |
ādyā upākaraṇe niyuktā |
sūtraṃ tu pūrvamantra uktam ||
tvaṃ hyagne prathamo manotā dhiyo abharvo dasma hotā |
tvaṃ sīṃ vṛṣannakṛṇorduṣṭarītu saho viśvasmai sahase sahadhyai ||
1 ||
tvam | hi | agne | prathamaḥ | manotā | asyāḥ | dhiyaḥ | abharvaḥ | dasma | hotā | tvam | sīm | vṛṣan | akṛṇoḥ | duṣṭarītu | sahaḥ | viśvasmai | sahase | sahadhyai || 1 ||
he agne prathamaḥ devānāṃ madhye prathamaḥ pratamaḥ prakṛṣṭatamaḥ pūrvabhāvī vā tvaṃ manotā hi devānāṃ mano yatrotaṃ saṃbaddhaṃ bhavati tādṛśo bhavasi |
hiśabdaḥ ‘agniḥ sarvā manotānāṃ manotāḥ saṃgacchante’ (ai. brā. 2. 10) ityādibrāhmaṇaprasiddhidyotanārthaḥ |
he dasma darśanīya asyāḥ dhiyaḥ asya karmaṇaḥ hotā devānāmāhvātā abhavaḥ bhavasi |
he vṛṣan kāmānāṃ varṣitaḥ tvaṃ sīṃ sarvataḥ duṣṭarītu duhistyamahisyaṃ sahaḥ balam akṛṇoḥ akaroḥ |
kimartham |
viśvasmai sahase sarvasyāpi balavataḥ śatroḥ sahadhyai abhibhavāya ||
1. jha-ta-na-bha-ra-paśī |
adhā hātā nyasīdo yajīyāniḻaspada iṣayabhīḍayaḥ san |
taṃ tvā naraḥ prathamaṃ daivyanto maho rāye citayanto anu gmana ||
2 ||
adhe | hotā | ni | asīdaḥ | yajīyān | iḻaḥ | pade | iṣayan | īḍayaḥ | san | tam | tvā | naraḥ | prathamam | deva'yantaḥ | mahaḥ | rāye | citayantaḥ | anu | gmana ||2||
^adha adhunā he agne ^yajīyān atiśayena yaṣṭā tvaṃ ^hotā homaniṣpādakaḥ san ^iḻaḥ bhūmyāḥ vedilakṣaṇāyāḥ ^pade sthāne ^nyasīdaḥ niṣaṇṇavānasi |
kiṃ kurvan |
^iṣayan paśupuroḍāśādilakṣaṇamanna- micchan ^īḍayaḥ stutyaḥ ^san |
^taṃ tādṛśaṃ ^tvā tvāṃ ^prathamam itaradevatābhyaḥ pūrvaṃ ^devayantaḥ devaṃ svāmātmāna icchantaḥ ^naraḥ netāro manuṣyā ṛtvijyamānāḥ ^mahaḥ mahate ^rāye dhanāya ^citayantaḥ jānantaḥ stutyā jñāpayanto vā ^anu ^gmana anvagacchan pūrvam ||
vṛteva yantaṃ bahubhirvamyaistve rayiṃ jāgṛvāṃso anu gmana |
ruśantamimaṃ darśataṃ bṛhantaṃ vapāvantaṃ viśvahā dīdivāṃsam ||
3 ||
vṛtā'iva | yantam | bahu'bhiḥ | vamyaiḥ | tve iti | rayim | jāgṛ'vāṃsaḥ | anu | gmana | ruśantam | agnim | darśam | bṛhantam | vapā'vantam | viśvahā | dīdi'vāṃsam || 3 ||
^vṛteva mārgeṇa |
iveti saṃpratyarthe |
lokadvayamadhyagatena mārgeṇa ^bahubhiḥ anekaiḥ ^vasavyaiḥ vasubhiḥ |
^svārthikastaddhitapratyayaḥ |
^yajamānānāṃ nivāsayogyairvā sārdhaṃ ^yantaṃ gacchantaṃ tvām ^anu ^gmana anugacchanti |
saṃbhajante yajamānāḥ |
kīdṛśāste |
^tve tvayi deve ^rayiṃ dhanaṃ ^jāgṛvāṃsaḥ prayacchanta ityarthaḥ |
kīdṛśaṃ tvāmityucyate |
^ruśantaṃ dīptavarṇam ^agnim ahanādiguṇaviśiṣṭaṃ ^darśataṃ darśanīyaṃ ^bṛhantaṃ mahāntaṃ ^vapāvantaṃ ^viśvahā sarveṣu kāleṣu ^dīdivāṃsaṃ dīpyamānam ||
padaṃ devasya narmamā vyantaḥ śravasyavaḥ śrava āpurmṛktam |
nāmāni ciddhadhire yajñiyāni bhadrāyāṃ te raṇayanta saṃdṛśau ||
4 ||
padam | devasya | narmamā | vyantaḥ | śravasyavaḥ | śravaḥ | āpun | amṛktam | nāmāni | cit | dadhire | yajñiyāni | bhadrāyām | te | raṇayanta | sam'dṛśau || 4 ||
^devasya dyotamānasyāgneḥ ^padam āhavanīyasthānaṃ ^namasā haviṣā stotreṇa vā ^vyantaḥ gacchantaḥ ^śravasyavaḥ annamicchantaḥ pūrve yajamānāḥ ^śravaḥ sarvatra śrūyamāṇamannam ^amṛktam anyairaparibādhyam ^āpun āmuvanti |
he agne ^te tava ^bhadrāyāṃ stutyāyāṃ ^saṃdṛśau nimittasyāṃ ^raṇayanta ramayanti |
1. kha-āvahanīya sthānam; ga-śa-āvahanīyādisthānam |
2. kha-gha-jha-ta-na-bha-ya-ra-^raṇayanta ramayanti' nāsti |
^yajñiyāni yajñayogyāni ^nāmāni namanīyāni vaiśvānaro jātavedā ityādīni ^dadhire dhārayanti |
athavā namanasādhanāni stotrāṇi dadhire ||
tvāṃ vardhanti kṣitayaḥ pṛthivyāṃ tvāṃ rāya ubhayāso janānām |
tvaṃ trātā taraṇe cetyo bhūḥ pitā mātā sadanminmānuṣāṇām ||
5 ||
tvām | vardhanti | kṣitayaḥ | pṛthivyām | tvām | rāyaḥ | ubhayāsaḥ | janānām | tvam | trātā | taraṇe | cetyaḥ | bhūḥ | pitā | mātā | sadan | ita | mānuṣāṇām || 5 ||
he agne ^tvāṃ ^kṣitayaḥ manuṣyā ṛtvijaḥ ^pṛthivyāṃ vedilakṣaṇāyāṃ ^vardhanti vardhayanti |
^ubhayāsaḥ ubhayavidhāni paśvapaśurūpāṇi ^janānāṃ yajamānānāṃ sambandhīni ^rāyaḥ dhanāni ṛtvijaḥ adhvardhvādayaḥ ^tvāṃ svāmuddiśya vardhanti |
athavā ubhayavidhāni dhanāni gavādīni janānāṃ putrādīnām arthāya prāptuṃ tvāṃ vardhanti |
he ^taraṇe du
he vagne vtaṃ tādṛśaṃ mahānubhāva vnavyaṃ stutyaṃ vtvā tvāṃ vsudhyaḥ śobhanabuddhayaḥ vsumnāyavaḥ sukhamicchantaḥ vdevyantaḥ devaṃ tvāmicchantaḥ vvayam vīmahe yācāmahe |
stuma ityarthaḥ |
he vagne vtvaṃ vdīdyataḥ dīpyamānaḥ san |
kena |
vbṛhatā mahatā vrocanena rocamānena tejasā dīdyataḥ vviśaḥ prajāḥ stotṛnsmān vdivaḥ svargam vanayaḥ agamayaḥ |
roṃcanenādityena divamagamaya iti vā sambandhaḥ |
ādityamārgeṇetyarthaḥ ||
viśāṃ kaviṃ viśapatiṃ śaśvatīnāṃ nitośanaṃ vṛṣabhaṃ carṣaṇīnām |
pretīṣaṇimiṣayantaṃ pāvakaṃ rājantamugniṃ yajataṃ rayīṇām ||
8 ||
viśām | kavim | viśapatim | śaśvatīnām | nitośanam | vṛṣabham | carṣaṇīnām | pretīṣaṇim | iṣayantam | pāvakam | rājantam | agnim | yajatam | rayīṇām || 8 ||
vśaśvatīnāṃ nityānāṃ vviśām ṛtvigyajamānalakṣaṇānāṃ vviśapatiṃ svāminaṃ vkaviṃ krāntadarśinaṃ vnitośanaṃ śatrūṇāṃ hiṃsakaṃ vvṛṣabhaṃ kāmānāṃ varṣakaṃ vcarṣaṇīnāṃ stotṛṇāṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ vpretīṣaṇiṃ prāpta- gamanam viṣayantam annaṃ kurvantaṃ vpāvakaṃ śodhakaṃ vrājantaṃ dīpyamānaṃ dīpayantaṃ vā vrayīṇāṃ lābhāya vyajataṃ yaṣṭavyam vagniṃ stuma iti śeṣaḥ ||
mo agne īje śaśame ca marto yasta ānaṭ samidhā havyadātim |
ya āhutiṃ pari vedā namoṃbhirviśvetta vāmā dadhate tvotaḥ ||
9 ||
maḥ | agne | īje | śaśame | ca | marttaḥ | yaḥ | te | ānaṭ | sam | idhā | havyadātim | yaḥ | āhutim | pari | veda | namaḥ'bhiḥ | viśvā | ita | saḥ | vāmā | dadhate | tvā'ūtaḥ ||9||
he vagne tvāṃ vsaḥ vmartaḥ ma yajamānaḥ vīje yajate |
vśaśame vca stauti |
'śaśamānaḥ' ( ni. 3. 14. 22 ) iti stutikamesu pāṭhādidamapi stutikarma |
vyaḥ yajamānaḥ vte tava vsamidhā saha vhavyadātiṃ haviṣā dānam vānaṭ karoti |
kiṃca vyaḥ yajamānaḥ vnamoṃbhiḥ stutibhiḥ vāhutim ājyādilakṣaṇāṃ vpari vveda parito vetti |
dadātītyarthaḥ |
vsaḥ yajamānaḥ vtvotaḥ tvayā rakṣitaḥ san vviśvet sarvāṇi vvāmā vanīyāni dhanāni vdadhate dhārayati ||
asmā u te mahi mahe vidhema namoṃbhirime samidhota havyaiḥ |
vedīṃ sūno sahaso gīrbhistairā te bhadrāyāṃ sumatau yatema ||
10 ||
asmai | ū~ iti | te | mahi | mahe | vidhema | namaḥ'bhiḥ | agne | sam | idhā | uta | havyaiḥ | vedīm | sūno | iti | sahasaḥ | gīḥ'bhiḥ | ā | te | bhadrāyām | su'matau | yatema || 10 ||
ma. 6 a. 1. sū. 1 ] caturtho'ṣṭaka: 5
he ᵥagne ᵥmahe mahate ᵥasmai ᵥte tubhyaṃ ᵥmahi mahat atyadhikaṃ ᵥvidhema paricarema|
kairityadhyate|
ᵥnamobhiḥ namaskāraiḥ ᵥsamidhā vā ᵥuta api ca ᵥhavyaiḥ havirbhiḥ|
he ᵥsahasaḥ ᵥsūno ᵥvedī vedyāṃ ᵥgībhiḥ stotraiḥ ᵥukthaiḥ śastraiśca vidhemeti saṃbandhaḥ|
kiṃca ᵥte tava ᵥbhadrāyāṃ stutyāyāṃ ᵥsumatī śobhanānugrahabuddhau ᵥā ᵥyatema āgacchema|
bhavemetyarthaḥ||
ā yastanathu rodasī vi bhāsā śrobhiśna śravasya starutraḥ|
bṛhadbhirajaiḥ sthavirebhirasme revadbhiragne vitaraṃ vi bhāhi ||
11 ||
ā | yaḥ | ta॒na॒thu | roda̭sī iti | vi | bhāsā̭ | śrava̭
purūṇya̭gre puruthā tvā॒yā va̭sūni rāja॒nsutā̭ te aśyāma | purūṇi̭ hi tve puruvā॒ra sa̭ntya॒gre va̭su vi॒dhatḙ rāja॒ntve || 13 || purūṇi̭ | a॒gre | purṷthā | tvā॒yā | va॒sūni̭ | rāja̭n | sutā̭ | te | a॒śyāma̭ | purūṇi̭ | hi | tve | i॒ti̭ | purṷ'vā॒ra | sa॒nti॒ | a॒gre | va॒su | vi॒dhatḙ | rāja̭n | tve | i॒ti̭ ||13||
he agne rājan te tava purūṇi bahūni puruthā gavāśvādi rūpeṇa bahuprakārāṇi vasūni dhanāni vasutā vasutāyai vasu mattvāya aśyāma vyāpnuyāṃ bhuñjīya vā |
atra bharatasvāmī vasutāta ityekapadaṃ saptamyantaṃ cakāra bhaṭṭabhāskaramiśrā''pyekapadaṃ saṃbuddhayantaṃ cakāra |
he puruvāra bahubhirviraṇīya agne rājan rājamāne tve tvayi purūṇi bahūni vasu vasūni tve tvāṃ vidhate paricarate mahyaṃ dātavyāni santi ||
36 ||
vedārthasya prakāśena tamo hārdaṃ nivārayan |
pumarthāṃścaturo deyād vidyātīrthamaheśvaraḥ ||
iti śrīmadrājādhirājaparameśvaravaidikamārgapravartakaśrīvīrabukkabhūpālasāmrājyadhuraṃdhareṇa sāyaṇācāryeṇa viracite mādhavīye vedārthaprakāśe ṛksaṃhitābhāṣye caturthāṣṭake caturtho'dhyāyaḥ samāptaḥ ||
śrīgaṇeśāya namaḥ ||
yasya niḥśvasitaṃ vedā yo vedebhyo'khilaṃ jagat |
nirmame tamahaṃ vande vidyātīrthamaheśvaram ||
atha pañcamādhyāya ārabhyate |
pañcamaṇḍalasya ṣaḍanuvākātmakasya prathame'nuvāke pañcadaśa sūktāni |
tatra tvaṃ hi ityekādaśarcaṃ dvitīyaṃ sūktaṃ bharadvājasyāpyagneyam |
anyā ṣaṭpañcāśadakṣarā śakvarī ādito daśānuṣṭubhaḥ |
anukramyate hi—‘tvaṃ hyekādaśānuṣṭubhaṃ śakryantam’ iti |
prātaranuvāka āpriye ṛtau ānuṣṭubhe chandasīdaṃ sūktamuttamāvarjaṃ viniyuktam |
sūyate hi—‘tvaṃ hi kṣaitavadagmā yo hotājaniṣṭa’ (āśva. śrau. 4. 13) iti |
āśvinaśastre caitat śaṃsanīyaṃ prātaranuvākanyāyenyatidiṣṭatvāt |
agnirmāṃ dvirātraṃ dvitīye'hanīdameva sūktamājyaśastram |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘tvaṃ hi kṣaitavaditi cājyam’ (āśva. śrau. 10. 2) iti ||
tvaṃ hi kṣaitava̭śoḍgre mi॒tro na pa̭ryase | tvaṃ vi̭icarṣaṇe śra॒vo va̭so pu॒ṣṭiṃ na pṷṣyasi || 1 || tvam | hi | kṣaita̭'vata | ya॒śaḥ | a॒gne | mi॒traḥ | na | pa॒rya॒se | tvam | vi॒'carṣa̭ṇe | śra॒vaḥ | va॒so | pu॒ṣṭim | na | pu॒ṣyasi || 1 ||
he agne tvaṃ hi tvaṃ khalu kṣaitavat kṣitiḥ kṣayo'pacayaḥ |
tatsambandhi kṣaitaṃ śuṣkaṃ kāṣṭham |
tadyuktaṃ yaśaḥ annaṃ havirlakṣaṇaṃ paryase abhitastvaṃ gacchasi |
tatra dṛṣṭāntaḥ |
mitro na aharabhimānī 1. kha-abhi ta pasi; jha-na-bha-atipatasi |
devo mitraḥ | sa hya | yadvā yaśa iti gṛhanāma | kṣetavat | kṣetaṃ nivāsakaṃ havirlakṣaṇamannam | tadyuktaṃ yajamānagṛhaṃ mitrabhūtaḥ puruṣa ivābhipatasi | yadvā patyatiraiśvaryakarmā | īdṛśamatra patyase īśiṣe | ataḥ kāraṇāt he vicarṣaṇe viśeṣeṇa sarvasya draṣṭaḥ vaso vāsakāpre tvaṃ śravaḥ śravaṇīyamannaṃ yajamānagṛhasthaṃ na | ayaṃ naśabdaścārthe | annakāryabhūtāṃ puṣṭiṃ ca puṣyasi vardhayasi ||
tvāṃ hi ṣmā carṣaṇīdhṛto yajñebhirgarbhīrīḻate |
tvāṃ vājī yātyavṛko rajastūrviśvacarṣaṇiḥ ||
2 ||
tvām | hi | sma | carṣaṇīdhṛtaḥ | yajñebhiḥ | gīḥbhiḥ | īḻate | tvām | vājī | yāti | avṛkaḥ | rajaḥstūrḥ | viśvacarṣaṇiḥ || 2 ||
he agne tvāṃ hi sma svāmeva khalu carṣaṇīdhṛtaḥ manuṣyāḥ yajñebhiḥ yajñasādhanairhavirbhiḥ gīḥbhiḥ stutibhiḥ īḻate stuvanti |
api ca vājī |
vājo gamanam |
tadvān sūryaḥ tvāṃ yāti gacchati |
praviśatītyarthaḥ |
tathā ca śrūyate—‘agniṃ vāvādityaḥ sāyaṃ praviśati tasmādamirdūrānnaṃ dṛśyate’ (tai. brā. 2. 1. 29) iti |
vājiśabdaśca sūryavācakatvena kvacit śrūyate—‘abhirvāyuḥ sūryaste vai vājinaḥ’ (tai. brā. 1. 6. 3. 9) iti vāyuḥ sasirādityo vājī (tai. brā. 1. 7. 8) iti ca |
kīdṛśo vājī |
avṛkaḥ hiṃsakarahitaḥ rajastūrḥ |
udakamatra raja ucyate |
tasya vṛṣṭilakṣaṇasya prerayitā |
yadvā |
rajasāṃ lokānāṃ taritā gantā viśvacarṣaṇiḥ sarvasya draṣṭā ||
sajoṣāstvā divo naro yajñasya ketumindhate |
yaddu sya mānuṣo janaḥ sumnāyurjuhe adhvare ||
3 ||
sa'joṣāḥ | tvā | divaḥ | naraḥ | yajñasya | ketum | indhate | yat | hu | sya | mānuṣaḥ | janaḥ | sumnāyuḥ | juhve | adhvare || 3 ||
he agne sajoṣaḥ sajopasaḥ saha prīyamāṇāḥ divaḥ stuteḥ naraḥ netāra ṛtvijaḥ yajñasya ketuṃ prajñāpakaṃ tvā tvām indhate samiddhaṃ kurvanti |
kadeti ceducyate |
yaddu yadā khalu syaḥ saḥ mānuṣaḥ manorapatyabhūtaḥ janaḥ jāto yajamāno hotā vā sumnāyuḥ sumnaṃ sukhamātmana icchan adhvare hiṃsāpratyavāyarahite yajñe juhve tvāmāhvayati tadetyarthaḥ ||
ṛdhastai sudānave dhiyā martāḥ śaśamate |
ūtī ṣa bṛhato divo dviṣo aṃho na tarati ||
4 ||

ṛdhata |
sa yajamāna ṛdhuyān he agne sudānave śobhanadānāya tubhyam ||
vadhyarthe caturthī ||
tava sambandhanā dhiyā karmaṇā yaḥ marteḥ maraṇadharmā yajamānaḥ śaśamate śāmyati |
yadvā |
śamatiḥ stutikarmā |
tvadarthaṃ karmaṇā yuktaḥ san stauti |
saḥ yajamānaḥ bṛhataḥ mahataḥ divaḥ dīsasya tava sambandhinyā ūtī ūrjyā rakṣayā dvipaḥ dveṣṭṛn aṃho na āhananaśīlaṃ pāpamiva tarati atikrāmati ||
samidhā yasta āhutiṃ niśitiṃ martyo naśat | vayāvantaṃ sa puṣyati kṣayamagne śatāyuṣam || 5 || samidhā | yaḥ | te | āhutim | niśitam | martyaḥ | naśat | vayāvantam | saḥ | puṣyati | kṣayam | agne | śataāyuṣam || 5 ||
he agne samidhā samindhanahetubhūtendhanena saha te tvadīyāṃ niśitaṃ niśitāṃ tanukṛtāṃ mantrasaṃskṛtām āhutiṃ yaḥ martyaḥ manuṣyaḥ naśat vyāpnuyāt |
naśatirvyāptikarmā |
saḥ manuṣyo yajamānaḥ vayāvantam |
vayāḥ śākhāḥ putrapautrādilakṣaṇāḥ |
tayuktaṃ śatāyuṣaṃ bahuvidhāṃ cirakāla- jīvanaṃ vā kṣayaṃ gṛhaṃ puṣyati poṣayati vardhayati |

1 ||
tveṣastaiṃ dhūma ṛṇvāti divi pañcuka ātataḥ | saro na hi dhutā tvaṃ kṛpā pāvaka rocase || 6 || tveṣaḥ | te | dhūmaḥ | ṛṇvāti | divi | san | śukraḥ | ātataḥ | sūraḥ | na | hi | dhutā | tvam | kṛpā | pāvaka | rocase || 6 ||
he agne tveṣaḥ dīsasya te tava śukraḥ nirmalaḥ śubhravarṇo vā dhūmaḥ divi antarikṣe ātataḥ vistīrṇaḥ san ṛṇvāti meghātmanā pariṇato gacchati |
api ca he pāvaka śodhakāgne sūraḥ na sūrya iva kṛpā abhimukhīkaraṇasamarthayā stutyā stūyamānaḥ tvaṃ dhutā dīptyā rocase hi dīpyase khalu ||
adhā hi viśvebhyaḥsi priyo no atithiḥ | raṇvaḥ purīva jūryaḥ sunurna trayayāryaḥ || 7 || adha | hi | viśvebhyaḥ | īrdhyaḥ | asi | priyaḥ | naḥ | atithiḥ | raṇvaḥ | purīva | jūryaḥ | sunuḥ | na | trayayāryaḥ || 7 ||
adha api ca viśva prajāsu he agne tvam īrdhyaḥ stutyaḥ ali bhavasi | hi yasmādarthe | yasmādevaṃ tasmāt naḥ asmākam atithiḥ | luptopamametat | atithiriva priyaḥ asi | yadvā |
1. gha-ta-sambandhinyā | 2. ta-martyaḥ | 3. na-śāmyatiḥ | 4. ga-ta-vyāptasya | 5. kha-pāpaṃ barati; bha-ya-pāpaṃ ca tarati | 6. ta-tanukṛtvā saṃskṛtām |
havirvahanāya satangāmī bhūtvāsmākaṃ priyo'si |
tathā ᵥpurīva nagayāṃ ᵥjūrya: jīrṇo vṛddho hitopadeṣṭā rājeva ᵥraṇva: ramaṇīyo gantavyo vā bhavasi |
tathā ᵥsūnurna putra iva ᵥśrayayāya: yajamānai: trātavya: pālayitavyo bhavasi |
āyateretadṛpam |
yadvaitatsūnorviśeṣaṇam |
vidyātapa:karmalakṣaṇaṃ ayaṃ yātīti śrayayāya: |
yāterauṇādika āyapratyaya: |
spṛhayāya iti yathā |
athavā śrayayāyyo janmatrayaṃ prāpta: |
janmatrayaṃ smaryate—'mānurapre'dhijananaṃ dvitīyaṃ mauñjibandhanāt |
tṛtīyaṃ yajñadīkṣāyā iti janmatrayaṃ smṛtam' (manu. 2. 169) iti |
tādṛśa: putra iva raṇvo ramaṇīyo'si ||
kratvā hi droṇe ajayase'gne vājī na kṛtyarya: | parijmeva svadhā gayo'tyo na hvārya: śiśu: || 8 || kratvā | hi | droṇe | ajayase | agne | vājī | na | kṛtyarya: | parijmā'iva | svadhā | gayo: | atya: | na | hvārya: | śiśu: || 8 ||
he ᵥagne ᵥkratvā karmaṇā manthanarūpeṇa ᵥdroṇe drume kāṣṭhe'raṇyāṃ vidyamānastvam ᵥajayase ᵥhi vyajyase2 khalu |
tathā ᵥvājī ᵥna vejanavānaśca iva ᵥkṛtyarya: |
kṛtyiti karmanāma |
havirvahanādīnāṃ karmaṇāṃ kartā |
sa yathā puruṣaṃ vahati tadvaddhavirvahanamagnerathasādṛśyam |
tathā ᵥparijmeva parita: sarvatra gantā vāyuriva dāvānalādirūpeṇa sarvatra gantvā tvaṃ ᵥsvadhā |
annanāmaiṭatat |
annaṃ ᵥgaya: gṛhaṃ ca bhavasi |
ubhayodātetyartha: |
api ca ᵥśiśu: jātamātro bālo'pi tvam ᵥatyo ᵥna satangāmī jātyaśva iva ᵥhvārya: kuṭilamitastato gantāsi |
'hva kauṭilye' ityasyaitadrūpam ||
kārīryāmebharmācchado vaikalpiyakayanuvākyā 'tvaṃ tyā' iti |
sūtritaṃ ca—'tvaṃ tyā cidacyutā dhāmante viśvaṃ bhuvanamadhiśritamiti vā' (ādha. śrau. 2. 13) iti ||
tvaṃ tyā cidacyutāgne paśurna yavase | dhāmā ha yatte ajar vanā vṛśranti śirkasa: || 9 || tvam | tyā | cit | acyutā | agne | paśu: | na | yavase | dhāma | ha | yat | te | ajar | vanā | vṛśranti | śirkasa: || 9 ||
he ᵥagne ᵥtvaṃ ᵥtyā tāni kāṣṭhāni |
cicchabdo'piṣaṭhadsamānārtho bhinnakrama: |
ᵥacyutā acyutāni dyāvayitumaśakyānyapi |
astīti śeṣa: |
tatra dṛṣṭānta: |
ᵥyavase ghāse visṛṣṭa: ᵥpaśurna paśuriva |
sa yathā sarvaṃ bhakṣayati tathā tvaṃ prauḍhakāṣṭhādīni kṣaṇamātreṇa dahasītyartha: |
he ᵥajara jararahitāgne ᵥyat yasya ||
'supāṃ suluk' iti ṣaṣṭhyā luk ||
ᵥśirkasa: dīptasya ᵥte tava ᵥdhāma dhāmāni tejāṃsi ᵥvanā vananīyāni saṃbhajanīyānyaraṇyāni ᵥvṛśranti chindanti bhakṣayanti sa tvamiti pūrvānvaya: |
ᵥha ityetatpādapūraṇam ||
1. kha-na-bha-ra-stotavya: |
2. ta-yajase |
3. na-dhāmā |
veṣi hyadhvarīyatāmagne hotā damai viśām | sampadho viśvate kṛṇu juṣasva havyamaṅgiraḥ || 10 || veṣi | hi | adhvarīyatām | agne | hotā | damai | viśām | sampadhaḥ | viśvate | kṛṇu | juṣasva | havyam | aṅgiraḥ || 10 || he 'gne 'dhvarīyatām adhvaraṃ yajñamārabhan icchatāṃ 'viśāṃ yajamānānāṃ 'dame gṛhe tvaṃ 'hotā devānāmāhvātā san 'veṣi 'hi stutiṃ havīṃṣi vā yataḥ kāmayase ataḥ kāraṇāt 'viśvate viśāṃ pālakāgne 'sampadhaḥ sampradānasmāna yajamānān 'kṛṇu kuru | kṛtvā ca he 'aṅgiraḥ aṅganādiguṇayukta aṅgarūpa vāgne 'havyam asmadīyaṃ haviḥ 'juṣasva sevasva ||
acchā no mitramaho deva devānagne vocaḥ sumatiṃ rodasyoḥ |

11 |
acchā
he ᐞagne ᐞsaḥ yajamānaḥ ᐞkṣayat kṣayatiᐞ nivasati |
cirakālaṃ jīvedityarthaḥ |
kīdṛśo yaja- mānaḥ |
ᐞṛtapāḥ ṛtasya yajñasya pālvakaḥ ᐞṛtejāḥ yajñanimittaṃ jātaḥ |
uṣitvā ca ᐞte tvadīyaṃ ᐞdevayuḥ devānātmana icchan sa yajamānaḥ ᐞuru vistīrṇaṃ ᐞjyotiḥ sūryākhyaṃ ᐞnaśate prāpnoti |
he ᐞdeva dyotamānāgne ᐞyaṃ ᐞmate manuṣyaṃ yajamānaṃ ᐞmitreṇa ᐞvaruṇaḥ |
tṛtīyārthe prathamā |
vahnena ca ᐞsajoṣāḥ saha prīyamāṇaḥ ᐞtvaṃ ᐞyajasmā ᐞyājanasādhanenāyudhena ᐞaṃhaḥ aṃhasaḥ pāpāt ᐞpāssi rakṣasi sa yajamānaḥ iti pūrvatra saṃbandhaḥ ||
īje yajñebhiḥ śaśame śarmībhirṛdhadvārāya agne dadāśa |
evā cana taṃ yaśasāmarjuṣṭirhāho mataṃ naśate na prarhatiḥ ||
2 ||
īje | yajñebhiḥ | śaśame | śarmībhiḥ | ṛdhat'vārāya | agne | dadāśa | eva | cana | tam | yaśasām | arjuṣṭiḥ | na | aṃhaḥ | martyam | naśate | na | pra'rhatiḥ || 2 ||
yaḥ yajamānaḥ ᐞṛdhadvārāya ṛdhatsamṛddhaṃ vāraṃ varaṇīyaṃ dhanaṃ yasya tādṛśāya ᐞagnaye ᐞdadāśa havīṃṣi dadāti sa yajamānaḥ ᐞyajñebhiḥ sarvairyajñaiḥ ᐞīje iṣṭavān bhavati |
tathā ᐞśarmībhiḥ karmabhiḥ kṛcchracāndrāyaṇādibhiḥ ᐞśaśame śāntaśca bhavati |
āgneyena yāgena sarvaṃ phalaṃ labhata ityarthaḥ |
api ca ᐞtaṃ yajamānaṃ ᐞyaśasāṃ yaśasvināṃ putrāṇām ᐞarjuṣṭiḥ aprāptiḥ na ᐞeva ᐞnaśate naiva prāpnoti |
putravān bhavatyevetyarthaḥ |
tathā ᐞmartaṃ manuṣyaṃ tam ᐞaṃhaḥ pāpaṃ ᐞna naśate |
tathā ᐞprarhatiḥ anarthahetuḥ pradarthaśca ᐞtaṃ ᐞna prāpnoti ||
sūro na yasya dṛśatirerapā bhīmā yadeti śucatasta ā dhīḥ |
heṣasvataḥ śurudho nāyamaktoḥ kutrā cidraṇvo vasatirvanejāḥ ||
3 ||
sūraḥ | na | yasya | dṛśatiḥ | arepāḥ | bhīmā | yat | eti | śucataḥ | te | ā | dhīḥ | heṣasvataḥ | śurudhaḥ | na | ayam | aktoḥ | kutra | cit | raṇvaḥ | vasatiḥ | vane'jāḥ || 3 ||
ᐞsūroṃ ᐞna sūryasyeva ᐞyasya agneḥ ᐞdṛśatiḥ darśanam ᐞarepāḥ pāparahitam |
he agne ᐞyat yasya ᐞte tava ᐞśucataḥ jvalataḥ ᐞdhīḥ dhārayitrī |
jvālā ᐞbhīmā bhayaṃkara satī ᐞā samantāt ᐞpūti gacchati |
yaḥ ᐞayam agniḥ ᐞheṣasvataḥ śabdayuktāḥ ᐞśurudho ᐞna śucaḥ śokasya rodhayitrīrgāśca ᐞaktoḥ |
akuriti rātrināma |
tena ca tatra saṃcārī rākṣasādirlakṣyate |
rākṣasādeḥ svabhūtā dadātīti śeṣaḥ |
so'yaṃ ᐞvasatiḥ sarveṣāmāvāsabhūtaḥ ᐞvanejāḥ araṇye jāyamānaḥ ᐞkutrā ᐞcit kacideva sthāne parvatāgrādau ᐞraṇvaḥ ramaṇīyo bhavati ||
tigmaṃ cidema mahi varpau asya bhasadśvo na yamasāna āsā |
vijehamānaḥ parurna jihvāṃ dravirna drāvayati dāru dhakṣat ||
4 ||
tigmam | cit | em | mahi | varpaḥ | asya | bhasat | aśvaḥ | na | yamasāna | āsā | vi'jehamānaḥ | paruḥ | na | jihvām | draviḥ | na | drāvayati | dāru | dhakṣat || 4 ||
1. kha-jha-na-bha-ra-kṣayati | 2. ta-darpakṣa | 3. ya-sūrya iva | 4. kha-gha-bha-na | na cārthe |
^asya^ agre: ^ema gamanasādhanabhūto mārga: ^tigmaṃ tīkṣṇa spṛṣṭumaśakyatvāt |
asya ca ^varpha: rūpaṃ ^mahi mahat prabhūtaṃ ^bhasat dīpyate ||
‘ bhasa bhartsanadīptyo: ’ iti dhātu: ||
kīdṛśo'gni: |
^aśvo ^na aśva iva ^āsā āsyena ^yamamāna: tṛṇādikaṃ niyacchan |
tathā ^paraśurna |
yathā paraśu: svakīyāṃ dhārāṃ kāṣṭhe prakṣipani tathā svakīyāṃ ^jihvāṃ jvālāṃ ^vijehamāna: tarugulmādau kṣipan |
tathā ^dāru kāṣṭhaṃ ^dhakṣat dahan ^dravirna |
dravirdrāvayitā svargakāra: |
sa yathā svarṇādikaṃ drāvayati tathā sarvaṃ vanaṃ ^drāvayati |
mūrtabhūtaṃ sarvaṃ bhasmasātkaronīyartha: ||
sa idastaiv prati dhādasipyañchiśītaṃ tejo'ryaso na dhārāṃ |
citrabhrajatiratiyoṃ akkorbena duṣadvā raghuptmajaṃhā: ||
5 ||
ma: | it | astā'iva | prati | dhāt | amiyyan | śiśīta | teja: | aryasa: | na | dhārāṃ | citra'bhrajati: | arati: | ya: | akko: | ve: | na | duṣadvā | raghuptma'jaṃhā: || 5 ||
^sa ^it sa khalvagni: ^asteva bāṇāde: kṣeteva ^prati ^dhāt svakīyajvālāṃ pratidhatte |
yathā dhanvī lakṣyābhimukhyena bāṇaṃ saṃghatte tathā jvālāṃ saṃghatta ityartha: |
pratidhāya ca ^asiṣyan jvālāṃ prakṣepyan ^teja: svakīyaṃ ^śiśīta niśyati tīkṣṇīkaroti |
tatra dṛṣṭānta: |
^ayaso ^na ^dhārāṃ |
yathā ayomayasya paraśādherdhārāṃ prakṣepakāmastīkṣṇīkaroti tadvat |
tīkṣṇīkṛtya ca ^citrabhrajati: vicitragati: ^akko: rātre: ^arati: abhigamantā ^vene pakṣīva ^duṣadvā vṛkṣepu sīdan ^raghuptmajaṃhā: laghupatanasamartha- pāda: |
evaṃbhūta: san ^ya: agnirvartate sa iti pūrvānvaya: ||
3 ||
sa ī rebho na prati vasta ustrā: śocirṣā rārapīti mitramaṃhā: |
naktaṃ ya īmṛṣo yo divā nūnamartyo aruṣo yo divā nṛn ||
6 ||
sa: | īm | rebha: | na | prati | vaste | ustrā: | śocirṣā | rārapīti | mitra'maṃhā: | naktam | ya: | īm | aruṣa: | ya: | divā | nṛn | amartya: | aruṣa: | ya: | divā | nṛn ||6||
^sa ^īṃ so'yamagni: ^rebho ^na stutya: sūrya iva ^ustrā: dīptā jvālā: ^prati ^vaste ācchādayati |
tathā ^mitramaṃhā: sarvaimānukūlaprakāśa: san ^śociṣā tejasā ^rārapīti bhṛśaṃ śabdaṃ karoti |
^ya: ^īṃ yo'yamagni: ^naktaṃ rātrau ^aruṣa: ārohamāna: san ^divā ahanīva ^nṛn manuṣyān svasvakārye prerayati |
punaryacchabda: pūraka: |
tathā ^amartya: amaraṇadharmā ^aruṣa: ārohamāno roṣarahito vā ^ya: agni: ^divā dyotamānena tejasā ^nṛn netṛnmīyān raśmīn prerayati sa iti pūrvatra saṃbandha: |
yadvā |
naktaṃ rātrau yo'yamabhiruṣa ārohamāno yaśca maraṇarahito'gnirdaivā ahi nṛn netṛn devān havirbhi: saṃyojayati |
ya: divā nṛniti punaruktirādarārthā ||
1. ga-ta-yasya |
2. ta-prakṣipan |
3. kha-jha-ta-na-bha-ra-śa-' jvālā: ' nāsti |
divo na yasya vidhato navīnoddṣā rukṣa oṣadhīṣu nūnot | ghṛṇā na yo dhraṃjasā patmanā yabnā rodasī vasunā daṃ supatnī || 7 || divaḥ | na | yasya | vidhataḥ | navīnot | vṛṣā | rukṣaḥ | oṣadhīṣu | nūnot | ghṛṇā | na | yaḥ | dhraṃjasā | patmanā | yan | ā | rodasī iti | vasunā | dam | supatnī iti su'patnī || 7 ||
vdivo vna dīptasya sūryasyeva vvidhataḥ vidhātu raśmīkurvataḥ |
vyasya agreḥ vnavīnāt bhṛśaṃ śabdo'bhran |
etadeva vyācaṣṭe |
vvṛṣā kāmānāṃ vardhitā vrukṣaḥ dīptaḥ so'gniḥ voṣadhīṣu dahyamānāsu vnūnot bhṛśaṃ śabdamakarot |
vyaḥ ca vghṛṇā vna saṃcalanaśīlena dīpteneva vdhrajasā gamanaśīlena tejasā vpatmanā itastata utpatatā vyan gacchan vartate so'gniḥ vdam asmacchatrun damayan vsupatnī śobhanapatike vrodasī dyāvāpṛthivyau vvasunā dhanena vā pūrayati ||
dhāryobhirvā yo yujyairbhirvairvidyubha divyotsvebhiḥ śuṣmaiḥ | śardho vā yo marutāṃ tatakṣa ṛbhurna tveṣo rabhasāno adyauta || 8 || dhāryaḥ'bhiḥ | vā | yaḥ | yujyaiḥ'bhiḥ | arkaiḥ | | tri'dyuta | na | dvi'dyota | svebhiḥ | śuṣmaiḥ | śardhaḥ | vā | yaḥ | marutām | tatakṣa | ṛbhuḥ | na | tveṣaḥ | rabhasānaḥ | adyauta || 8 ||
vyaḥ agniḥ vdhāryobhirvā dhārakairśvairiva vyujyaibhiḥ yujyaiḥ svayameva yujyamānaiḥ varkaiḥ arcanīyaiḥ dīptibhirgacchati so'gniḥ vvidyunna tridiva vsvebhiḥ svakīyaiḥ vśuṣmaiḥ śoṣakaistejobhiḥ vdvidyota vidyotate |
vyaḥ ca vmarutām etatsaṃjkānāṃ devānāṃ vśardho vvā balamiva vtatakṣa tanūkaroti sarvaṃ tīkṣṇakaroti |
śoṣayatīti yāvat |
so'yam vṛbhurna uru bhāsamānaḥ sūrya iva vtveṣaḥ dīptaḥ vrabhasānaḥ vegaṃ kurvan vadyauta vidyotate prakāśate ||
4 ||
‘ yathā hotaḥ ’ ityṛcaṃ caturthaṃ sūktaṃ bharadvājasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamāgneyam |
‘ yathā hotaḥ ’ ityanukrāntam |
prātaranuvākābhinaśastrayorhyukto viniyogaḥ ||
yathā hotarmanuṣo devatāta yajñebhiḥ sūno sahaso yajāmi | evā nau adya samanā samānānuśabhabhra uśato yakṣi devān || 1 || yathā | hotaḥ | manuṣaḥ | deva'tātā | yajñebhiḥ | sūno | sahasaḥ | iti | maham | | yajāmi | eva | naḥ | adya | samanā | samānān | uśana | agne | uśataḥ | yakṣi | devān || 1 ||
he vhotaḥ devānāmāhvātaḥ vsahasaḥ vsūno balasya putrāye vyathā yena prakāreṇa vmanuṣaḥ manaḥ prajāpateryajamānasya vdevatātā |
yajñanāmaṃtata |
devatātau yajñe vyajñebhiḥ yajamānasādhanairhavirbhiḥ vyajāsi
devān yajasi 'eva' evaṃ' he 'agne 'naḥ 'asmākam 'adya asmin yajñe 'samānān 'svarasadṛśānindrādīn 'uśan kāmayamānasvam 'uśataḥ kāmayamānān yaṣṭavyān 'devān 'samanā kṣipraṃ 'yakṣi yaja |
yahā |
samānaṃ manyante' asmin devāniti samano yajñaḥ |
tasminniti yojyam ||
sa nau vibhāvā cakṣurnirvastaurabhirvandāru vedyathanau dhāt |
viśvāyuryo amṛto martyeṣubhūddutitirjātavedāḥ ||
2 ||
saḥ | naḥ | vibhā'rvā | cakṣuḥniḥ | na | vastauḥ | agniḥ | vandāru | vedhaḥ | canaḥ | dhāt | viśva'āyuḥ | yaḥ | amṛtaḥ | martyeṣu | upaḥ'bhṛt | bhūt | atithiḥ | jāta'vedāḥ || 2 ||
'vastauḥ ahani 'cakṣurniḥ prakāśakaḥ sūrye iva 'vibhāvā viśeṣeṇa dīpyamānaḥ 'vedhaḥ sarvairjñātavyo labhyanīyo vā 'saḥ 'agniḥ 'naḥ asmabhyaṃ 'vandāru vandanīyaṃ stutyaṃ 'canaḥ annaṃ 'dhāt dadhātu prayacchatvityarthaḥ |
'viśvāyuḥ sarvāṅgaḥ sarveṣāṃ jīvanaheturvā 'amṛtaḥ maraṇarahitaḥ 'atithiḥ havirvahanāya satatagāmī 'jātavedāḥ jātānāṃ veditā jātadhano vā evaṃbhūtaḥ 'yaḥ agniḥ 'martyeṣu manuṣyeṣu yajamāneṣu 'uparyudbhūta upaḥkāle'gnihotrādyarthaṃ prabuddho bhavati sa iti pūrvānvayaḥ ||
dyāvo na yasya panayantyabhvaṃ bhāsāṃsi vaste sūryo na śukraḥ |
vi ya inotpajaraḥ pāvakaḥ'śvasya cicchrathatpūrvyāṇi ||
3 ||
dyāvaḥ | na | yasya | punayanti | abhvam | bhāsāṃsi | vaste | sūryaḥ | na | śukraḥ | vi | yaḥ | inoti | ajaraḥ | pāvakaḥ | akṣasya | cit | śikṣathat | pūrvyāṇi || 3 ||
'na iti saṃ|
uktaṃ ca—'astyupamārthasya saṃpratyarthe prayogaḥ' (niru. 7. 31) iti |
'dyāvaḥ dyotāraḥ saṃrā...nī 'yasya agneḥ 'abhvaṃ mahatkarma 'panayanti stuvanti so'gniḥ 'sūryo 'na sūrye iva 'śukraḥ śuklarūpaṃ 'anna 'bhāsāṃsi tejāṃsi 'vaste ācchādayati |

tatra sthita: ^ajarebhi: jarārahitai: ^nānadadbhi: atyarthaṃ śabdaṃ kurvadbhirjarairyukto raśmibhirvā ^yaviṣṭha: yuvatam |
^ya: ^pāvaka: śodhaka: ^agni: ^purutama: atiśayena prabhūta: san ^puruṇi bahūni ^pṛthūni sthūlāni kāṣṭhāni ^bhavan bhakṣayan ^anuyāti anugacchati sa itthamitthaṃ vartate iti pūrvatra saṃbandha: ||
gārhapatyādīnāṃ mitha: saṃsarge'gnaye vivicaya iṣṭi: kartavyā |
tatra ‘vi te’ itye'pānuvākyā’ (āśva. śrau. 3. 13) ||
vi te viśvagvātajūtāso agne bhāmasa: śuce śucayaścaranti |
tuvimrākṣasau divyā navagvā vanā vananti dhṛṣatā rujanta: ||
3 ||
vi | te | viśvak | vātajūtāsa: | agne | bhāmasa: | śuce | śucaya: | caranti | tuvimrākṣasa: | divyā: | navagvā: | vanā | vananti | dhṛṣatā | rujanta: || 3 ||
he ^śuce śuddha dīpta vā ^agne ^te tvadīyā: ^vātajūtāsa: vātena preritā: ^śucaya: nirmalā: ^bhāmasa: dīptaya: ^viśvak sarvata: ^vi ^caranti vividhaṃ gacchanti |
^tuvimrākṣasa: bahūni kāṣṭhāni spṛśantaḥ ^divyā: divi dyotamāne'gnau bhavā: ^navagvā: nūtanagamanāste raśmaya: ^vanā vanāni ^vananti saṃbhajante |
dahantītyartha: |
kiṃ kurvantaḥ |
^dhṛṣatā dharṣakeṇa tejasā ^rujanta: vanāni bhañjayantaḥ ||
ye te śukrāsa: śucaya: śuciṣma: kṣāṃ vapanti virṣitāso aśvā: |
adha bhrasta urviṃyā vi bhāti yātayamāno adhi sānu pṛśnai: ||
4 ||
ye | te | śukrāsa: | śucaya: | śuciṣma: | kṣām | vapanti | virṣitāsa: | aśvā: | adha | bhrasta | urviṃyā | vi | bhāti | yātayamāna: | adhi | sānu | pṛśnai: || 4 ||
he ^śuciṣma: dīptimanne ^te tava ^śukrāsa: śubhā: ^ye ^śucaya: dīptaya: ^kṣāṃ pṛthivīṃ bhūmiṃ ^vapanti muṇḍayanti |
keśasthānīyānoṣadhivanaspatīn dahantītyartha: |
‘agnirdadāti romā pṛthivyā:’
1. tan-bhavan bhajana | 2. na-anuvākyā | sūtritaṃ ca-vi te viśvagvātajūtāso agne tvāmame mānuṣīrīḻate viśa iti | 3. kha-jha-bha-ya-vanā vanāni |
( kra. saṃ. 1. 65. 4 ) iti nigamāntaram |
te raśmayaḥ ᵥvibhitāsaḥ ᵥvimuktāḥ ᵥaśvāḥ iva itastato gacchantīti śeṣaḥ |
ᵥadha asmin kāle ᵥte tvadīyaḥ ᵥbhramaḥ bhramaṇaśīlo jvālāsamūhaḥ ᵥpṛṣṭheḥ nānā- rūpāyā bhūmeḥ ᵥadhi upari ᵥsānu samucchritaṃ deśaṃ parvatāgrādikaṃ prati ᵥyātayamānaḥ svakīyamannaṃ vyāpārayan ᵥurviyā uru bahulaṃ ᵥvi ᵥbhāti viśeṣeṇa prakāśate ||
ardha jihvā pāpatīti pra vṛṣṇo goṣuyudho nāśaniḥ sṛjānā |
śūrasyeva prasiteḥ kṣātirerdurdhurbhīmo dayate vanāni ||
5 ||
ardha | jihvā | pāpatīti | pra | vṛṣṇaḥ | goṣuyudhaḥ | na | aśaniḥ | sṛjānā | śūrasyeva | prasiteḥ | kṣātiḥ | agneḥ | duḥsṛtuḥ | bhīmaḥ | dayate | vanāni || 5 ||
ᵥadha api ca ᵥvṛṣṇaḥ varṣituragneḥ ᵥjihvā jvālā ᵥpra ᵥpāpatīti prakarṣeṇa punaḥpunaḥ patati gacchati |
tatra dṛṣṭāntaḥ |
ᵥgoṣuyudho ᵥnāśaniḥ ᵥsṛjānā |
asurairapahatāsu goṣu yudhyannindro goṣuyut |
tasmāt sṛjyamānāśanivannirdha iva |
tathā ᵥśūrasyeva śauryopetasya narasya ᵥprasiteḥ prabandhanaṃ yathāntyaiḥ duḥsahamaivam ᵥagneḥ ᵥkṣātiḥ jvālā soḍhumaśakyetyarthaḥ |
kṣīyante dahyante'syāmoṣadhivanaspataya iti kṣāyateradhikaraṇe ktin ||
api ca ᵥdurvartuḥ durvvāro'nyairvārayitumaśakyaḥ ᵥbhīmaḥ bhayaṃkaro'gniḥ ᵥvanāni ᵥdayate dahati |
dayatiratra dahati karmā ||
ā bhānunā pārthivāni jyāṃsi mahastodaya dhṛṣatā tatantha |
sa bādhasva bhayā sahobhiḥ spṛdho vanuṣyanvanuṣo ni jūrva ||
6 ||
ā | bhānunā | pārthivāni | jyāṃsi | mahaḥ | todasya | dhṛṣatā | tatantha | maḥ | bādhasva | arpa | bhayā | sahaḥbhiḥ | spṛdhaḥ | vanuṣyan | vanuṣaḥ | ni | jūrva || 6 ||
he agne ᵥbhānunā dīpyā ᵥpārthivāni pṛthivyāṃ bhavāni ᵥjyāṃsi ᵥayatargatikarmāṇi ||
gantavyāni sthānāni ᵥmahaḥ mahataḥ ᵥtodasya prerakasya ᵥdhṛṣatā dharṣakeṇa raśminā saha ᵥā ᵥtatantha āstṛṇāsi ācchādayasi |
ᵥsaḥ tvaṃ ᵥbhayā bhayaṃkarāni ᵥapa ᵥbādhasva |
tathā ᵥsahobhiḥ abhi- bhavanasamarthairbalaistejobhirvā ᵥspṛdhaḥ spardhamānān ᵥvanuṣyan hiṃsan ᵥvanuṣaḥ hiṃsakān |
śatrūniti |
ᵥni ᵥjūrva nijahi |
jūrvatirhantikarmā ||
ukhāsaṃbharaṇīyeṣṭāvagneḥ kṣatravataḥ ‘sa citra’ ityeṣā yājyā |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘sa citra citraṃ citayantasme abhīraśe bṛhataḥ kṣatriyasya’ ( āśva. śrau. 4. 1 ) iti ||
sa citra citraṃ citayantasme citrakṣatra citratamaṃ vayodhām |
candraṃ rayiṃ puruvīraṃ bṛhantaṃ candraṃ candrābhirgṛṇate yuvasva ||
7 ||
saḥ | citram | citram | citayantam | asme iti | citrakṣatra | citratamam | vayaḥdhām | candram | rayim | puruvīram | bṛhantam | candram | candrābhiḥ | gṛṇate | yuvasva || 7 ||
he ᵛcitra cāyanīya he ᵛcitrakṣatra vicitrabala cāyanīyadhana vā he ᵛcandra ālhādakāpe ᵛsaḥ tāraśasvaṃ ᵛcandrābhiḥ ālhādayitrībhiḥ stutibhiḥ ᵛguṇate stuvate |
vacanavyatyayaḥ |
stuvantaḥ ᵛasme asmabhyaṃ dātuṃ ᵛrathi dhanaṃ ᵛyuvasva pṛthak kuru |
kīdṛśaṃ rayim |
ᵛcitraṃ cāyanīyaṃ ᵛcitayantaṃ jñāpayantam |
dhanena hi puruṣaḥ pratyāyate |
ᵛcitratamam atiśayenāśraryabhūtaṃ ᵛvayodhāṃ vayaso'nnasya dhātāraṃ pradātāraṃ ᵛcandram ālhādakaṃ ᵛpuruvīraṃ purubhirbahubhirvīraiḥ putrapautrādibhiryuktaṃ ᵛbṛhantaṃ mahāntam |
īdṛśaṃ dhanamasmabhyaṃ prayacchetyarthaḥ ||
8 ||
‘mūrdhānam’ iti saptacaṃ saptamaṃ sūktaṃ bharadvājasyārṣaṃ vaiśvānarābhidevatākam |
ṣaṣṭhīsaptamyau jagatyau śiṣṭāḥ pañca triṣṭubhaḥ |
tathā cānukrāntaṃ—‘mūrdhānaṃ vaiśvānarīya hi dvijagatyantam’ iti |
gataḥ sūkta- viniyogaḥ |
viṣuvatyābhimāruta ādyastṛco vaikalpikaḥ stotriyaḥ |
tathaiva sūtritaṃ—‘mūrdhānaṃ divo aratiṃ pṛthivyā mūrdhā divo nābhiragniḥ pṛthivyā iti vā’ ( āśva. śrau. 8. 6 ) iti ||
mūrdhānaṃ divo aratiṃ pṛthivyā vaiśvānaramuta ā jātamugnim |
kaviṃ samrājamatithiṃ janānāmāsannā pātraṃ janayanta devāḥ ||
1 ||
mūrdhānam | divaḥ | aratim | pṛthivyāḥ | vaiśvānaram | ṛte | ā | jātam | agnim | kavim | sam'rājam | atithim | janānām | āsan | ā | pātram | janayanta | devāḥ || 1||
ᵛmūrdhānaṃ śirobhūtam | kasya | ᵛdivaḥ dyulokasya ᵛpṛthivyāḥ prathitāyā bhūmeḥ ᵛaratiṃ gantāram | yadvā | gantavyaṃ svāminaṃ ᵛvaiśvānaraṃ viśveṣāṃ narāṇāṃ saṃbandhanam ᵛṛte | ṛtamiti satyasya yajñasya vā nāma | nimittasatyeṣā | ṛtanimittam ᵛā ābhimukhyena ᵛjātaṃ sṛṣṭādāvutpannaṃ ᵛkaviṃ krāntadarśinaṃ ᵛsamrājaṃ samyagrājamānaṃ ᵛjanānāṃ yajamānānām ᵛatithiṃ havirvahanāya satataṃ gantāram | yadvā | atithipūjyam | ᵛāsan āsani āsye | dvitīyārthe saptamī | āsyabhūtam | agnilakṣaṇasyāsyena hi devā havīṃṣi bhuñjate | ᵛpātraṃ pātāraṃ rakṣakam | yadvā | āsyena dhārakam | evaṃguṇaviśiṣṭaṃ vaiśvānarābhi ᵛdevāḥ stotāra ṛtvijo devā eva vā ᵛā janayanta | yajñābhimukhyena .ajījanan | araṇyoḥ sakāśādutpādayan ||
nābhiṃ yajñānāṃ sadanaṃ rayīṇāṃ mahāmāhāvamubhi saṃ nayanta |
vaiśvānaraṃ rathyamadhvarāṇāṃ yajñasya ketuṃ janayanta devāḥ ||
2 ||
nābhim | yajñānām | sadanam | rayīṇām | mahām | ā'hāvam | abhi | sam | nayanta | vaiśvānaram | rathyam | adhvarāṇām | yajñasya | ketum | janayanta | devāḥ || 2 ||
ᵛyajñānāṃ jyotiṣṭomādiyāgānāṃ ᵛnābhiṃ nahanaṃ bandhakaṃ tathā ᵛrayīṇāṃ dhanānāṃ ᵛsadanaṃ sthāna- mekanilayaṃ ᵛmahāṃ mahāntam ᵛāhāvam |
āhūyante'sminnāhutaya ityāhāvaḥ |
tādṛśam |
yadvā |
1. kha-gha-jha-bha-ya-ra-’ yadvā...pūjyaṃ’ nāsti |
2. ya-ajanayan |
sṛṣṭudakadhāraṇāmāhavaṃ nipānasthānīyam |
evaṃbhūtamimam vabhi abhitaḥ vsaṃ vnavanta stotāraḥ samyak stuvanti |
tathā vvaiśvānaraṃ viśveṣāṃ narāṇāṃ saṃbandhanam vadhvarāṇāṃ yajñānāṃ vrathyaṃ rathinam |
yathā rathī svarathaṃ nayati tadvetāraṃ grāhayitāraṃ vgamayitāraṃ vyajñasya vketuṃ prajñāpakam |
evaṃvidhamapi vdevāḥ stotāra ṛtvijo devā eva vā vjanayanta janayanti mathanenotpādayanti ||
tvadvigro jāyate vājyagne tvadvīrāso abhimātiṣāhaḥ |
vaiśvānara tvamasmāsu dhehi vasūni rājantspṛhayāṇyāṇi ||
3 ||
tvat | vipraḥ | jāyate | vājī | agne | tvat | vīrāsaḥ | abhimātiṣāhaḥ | vaiśvānara | tvam | asmāsu | dhehi | vasūni | rājan | spṛhayāṇyāṇi || 3 ||
he vagne vvājī havirlakṣaṇavān puruṣaḥ vtvat tvattaḥ sakāśāt vvipraḥ medhāvī vjāyate |
tubhyaṃ havirdānena medhāvī bhavati |
tathā vvīrāsaḥ vīrāḥ vtvat tvattaḥ vabhimātiṣāhaḥ abhimātīnāṃ śatrūṇāmabhibhavitāro jāyante |
yasmādevaṃ tasmāt he vvaiśvānara vrājan rājamānāgne vtvamasmāsu svasṛkteṣu vspṛhayāṇyāṇi spṛhaṇīyāni vvasūni dhanāni vdhehi nidhehi ||
tvāṃ viśve amṛta jāyamānaṃ śiśuṃ na devā abhi saṃ navante |
tava ṛtubhiramṛtatvamānvaiśvānara yatpitroradīdeḥ ||
4 ||
tvām | viśve | amṛta | jāyamānam | śiśum | na | devāḥ | abhi | sam | navante | tava | ṛtubhiḥ | amṛtatvam | āyan | vaiśvānara | yat | pitroḥ | adīdeḥ || 4 ||
he vamṛta maraṇadharmarahitāgne vviśve sarve vdevāḥ stotāraḥ vjāyamānam araṇyoḥ sakāśāt utpadyamānaṃ vtvāṃ vśiśuṃ vna putramiva vabhi vsaṃ vnavante abhisaṃstuvanti |
yadvā |
dīvyantīti devā raśmayaḥ |
te sarve jāyamānaṃ tvāmabhi saṃ navante abhisaṃgacchante yathā pitaraḥ putramabhigacchanti |
api ca he vvaiśvānara agne vyat yadā vpitroḥ pālayitrorvāvāpṛthivyormadhye vadīdeḥ dīpyase tadānīṃ vtava vsvadīyaiḥ vṛtubhiḥ karmabhiryotiṣṭomādibhirvāgaiḥ vamṛtatvaṃ devatvam vāyanyajamānāḥ prāpnuvanti ||
vaiśvānara tava tāni vratāni mahānyagne nakirā dadharṣa |
yajjāyamānaḥ pitrorupasthe'vindaḥ ketuṃ vayuneṣvahvam ||
5 ||
vaiśvānara | tava | tāni | vratāni | mahāni | agne | nakiḥ | ā | dadharṣa | yat | jāyamānaḥ | pitroḥ | upasthe | avindaḥ | ketum | vayuneṣu | ahvam || 5 ||
1. gha-jña-ta-na-śa-yajñānāṃ saṃbandhanam |
2. jña-ta-prahītāram |
3. ya-maraṇarahitāgne |
4. na- abhisaṃgacchante; śa-abhisaṃgacchante abhisaṃstuvanti |
he vaiśvānara viśveṣāṃ narāṇāṃ saṃbandhin agne tava tvadīyāni tāni prasiddhāni vratāni karmāṇi mahāni mahānti nakirā dadharṣa na kaścanānyo dharṣayati bādhate |
yat yadā tvaṃ pitroḥ dyāvāpṛthivyoḥ upasthe upasthe'ntarikṣe vāyuneṣu gantavyeṣu mārgeṣu jāyamānaḥ san ahnāṃ ketuṃ prajñāpakaṃ sūryaṃ svarbhānunāvṛtam avindaḥ alambhayaḥ |
tamorūpamasuraṃ svatejasā |
nivārayansthāpaya ityarthaḥ |
tadā tāni vratānīti pūrvānvayaḥ |
vaiśvānarasya vimitāna cakṣasā sānūni divo amṛtasya ketunā |
tasyedu viśvā bhuvanādhi mūrdhani vayāiva ruruhuḥ sapta visruhaḥ |

vaiśvānarasya viśva
‘pṛkṣasya’ iti saptarcamaṣṭamaṃ sūktaṃ bharadvājasyārṣaṃ vaiśvānaramidevatākam |
āditaḥ1 ṣaṭ jagatyaḥ saptamī triṣṭup |
tathā cānukrāntaṃ—‘pṛkṣasyāntyā triṣṭup’ iti |
cāturviṃśike'hanyābhimārute idaṃ sūktaṃ vaiśvānaranividdhānam |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘pṛkṣasya vṛṣṇo vṛṣṇe śardhāya’ (āśva. śrau. 7. 4) iti |
ābhiplavike pṛṣṭhyābhiplavapaḍahayoḥ pañcame'hanīdameva sūktaṃ vaiśvānaranividdhānam |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘pṛkṣasya vṛṣṇo vṛṣṇe śardhāya n citsahojā ityābhimārutam’ (āśva. śrau. 7. 7) iti |
viṣuvatyābhimāruta ādyau tṛcau stotriyānurūpau |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘pṛkṣasya vṛṣṇo arupasya nū saha iti stotriyānurūpau’ (āśva. śrau. 8. 6) iti ||
pṛkṣasya vṛṣṇo arupasya nū sahaḥ pra nu vocaṃ vidathā jātavedasaḥ | vaiśvānarāya matirnavyasī śuciḥ someva pavate cārurgraye || 1 || pṛkṣasya | vṛṣṇaḥ | arupasya | nu | sahaḥ | pra | nu | vocam | vidathā | jātavedasaḥ | vaiśvānarāya | matiḥ | navyasī | śuciḥ | somaḥ'iva | pavate | cāruḥ | agraye || 1 ||
vpṛkṣasya saṃvṛktasya vyāptasya |
yadvā |
pukṣaṃ havirlakṣaṇamannam |
taddhataḥ |
vvṛṣṇaḥ kāmānāṃ varṣituḥ varupasya ārohamānasya vjātavedasaḥ jātānāṃ vediturvaiśvānarasyeḥ vsahaḥ balamabhibhavanasamarthaṃ vvidathā vidathe yajñe vnu kṣipraṃ vpra vvocaṃ prabravīmi |
prakarṣeṇa staumītyarthaḥ |
eko nuśabdaḥ pūrakaḥ |
vnavyasī navatarā vśuciḥ nirmalā yadvā |
stotṛṇāṃ śodhayitrī vcāruḥ śobhanā vmatiḥ mananīyā stutiścāsmai vvaiśvānarāya vagraye vpavate |
matprakāśāt sravati |
svayameva nirgacchatītyarthaḥ |
vsomaiva |
yathā somo daśāpavitrāt sravati tadvat ||
sa jāyamānaḥ parame vyomani vratānyagnirbratapā arakṣata | vyantarikṣamamimīta sukraturvaiśvānaro mahimā nākamspṛśat || 2 || saḥ | jāyamānaḥ | parame | vi'aumani | vratāni | agniḥ | vrata'pāḥ | arakṣata | vi | antarikṣam | aminota | su'kratuḥ | vaiśvānaraḥ | mahimā | nākam | aspṛśat || 2 ||
vsaḥ vaiśvānaraḥ vagniḥ vvratapāḥ vratasya pālakaḥ vparame utkṛṣṭe vvyomani vyomandyākāśe vjāya- mānaḥ sūryātmanā prādurbhavan vvratāni karmāṇi laukikāni vaidikāni ca varakṣata rakṣati |
vantarikṣaṃ ca vvi vamimīta |
etadupalakṣitāṃllokānnirmitavān |
tathā vsukratuḥ śobhanakarmā vvaiśvānaraḥ agniḥ vmahinā svamahimnā tejasā vnākaṃ dyulokam vaspṛśat spṛśati sma ||
1. ta-ādyāḥ |
vyastambhādrodasī mitro adbhutò'ntarvāvadakṛṇojjyotiṣā tamaḥ | vi carmaṇīva dhiṣaṇe avartayadvaiśvānaro viśvamadhatta vṛṣṇyam || 3 || vi | astambhāt | rodasī iti | mitraḥ | adbhutaḥ | antaḥ | āvat | akṛṇot | jyotiṣā | tamaḥ | vi | carmaṇī iveti carmaṇī'iva | dhiṣaṇe iti | avartayat | vaiśvānaraḥ | viśvam | adhatta | vṛṣṇyam || 3 ||
^mitraḥ mitrabhūtaḥ sarveṣām ^adbhutaḥ mahān āścaryabhūto vā vaiśvānaro'gniḥ ^rodasī dyāvāpṛthivyau ^vyastambhāt viśeṣeṇa stambhitavān |
yathādyo na patatastathā svakīyadeśe sthāpitavānityarthaḥ |
tathā ^jyotiṣā tejasā ^tamaḥ andhakāraṃ ca ^antarvāvat antarhitaṃ tirohitam ^akṛṇot akarot |
vāva- diti vātergatikarmaṇo yallugantasya rūpam |
api ca ^dhiṣaṇe dhārayitryau dyāvāpṛthivyau ^carmaṇīva yathā paśorviśasitā dve carmaṇī śoṣaṇārtha prasārayati tathā ^vi ^avartayat vivṛte vistṛte akarot |
kiṃ bahunā ^vaiśvānaraḥ ayamagniḥ ^viśvaṃ sarvaṃ ^vṛṣṇyaṃ vīryam ^adhatta dhatte dhārayati ||
apāmupasthe mahiṣā agṛbhṇata viśo rājānamupa tasthurgamiyam | ā dūto agnirmaradvisvarvato vaiśvānaraṃ mātariśvā parāvataḥ || 4 || apām | upa'sthe | mahiṣāḥ | agṛbhṇata | viśaḥ | rājānam | upa | tasthuḥ | ṛmiyam | ā | dūtaḥ | agnim | abharat | trivasvataḥ | vaiśvānaram | mātariśvā | parāvataḥ || 4 ||
āpa ityantarikṣanāma |
^apām antarikṣasya ^upasthe upasthāne madhye ^mahiṣāḥ |
mahantāmaitat |
mahānto marutaḥ ^agṛbhṇata agṛhṇan |
imaṃ vaiśvānaraṃ vaiśvatātmanā vartamānamajānansthityarthaḥ |
gṛhītvā ca ^viśaḥ prajāḥ ^rājānaṃ svāminam ^ṛmiyam arcanīyam ^upa ^tasthuḥ astuvan |
api cemaṃ ^vaiśva- naram ^agniṃ purā sūryasamīpe vidyamānaṃ ^dūtaḥ vegavān devānāṃ dūto vā ^mātariśvā vāyuḥ ^parāvataḥ dūradeśāt ^vivasvataḥ ādityāt ^ā ^abharat imaṃ lokaṃ pratyājahāra ||
yugeyuge vidathyaṃ guṇadbhyo'mai rayiṃ yaśasaṃ dhehi navyasīm | pvyeva rājabhadhaśaṃsamajara nīcā ni vṛśva vuninaṃ na tejasā || 5 || yuge'yuge | vidathyam | guṇat'bhyaḥ | agne | rayim | yaśasam | dhehi | navyasīm | pvyā'iva | gajan | adha'śaṃsam | ajara | nīcā | ni | vṛśva | vuninam | na | tejasā ||5||

vidatho yajñaḥ |
tadahaṃ svāmuddiśya ^navyasīṃ nava- tarāṃ stutiṃ ^guṇadbhyaḥ uccārayitṛbhyo'smabhya ^rayiṃ dhanaṃ ^yaśasaṃ yaśasvinaṃ putraṃ ca ^dhehi vidhehi kuru |
1. kha-jha-ta-na-bha-ra-arcanīyaṃ tam |
kiṃca he rājan rājamāna ajara jararahitāgne patyeva vajreṇevātmīyena tejasā vaninaṃ na vṛkṣamiva aghaśaṃsam aghasthānarthasya śaṃsitāraṃ śatruṃ nīcā nīcīno nyagbhūto yathā bhavati tathā ni vṛkṣa nijahi' ||
asmākamagne maghavatsu dhārayānāmi kṣatramajaraṃ suvīryam |
vayaṃ jayema śatinaṃ sahasriṇaṃ vaiśvānara vājamagne tavotibhiḥ ||
6 ||
asmākam | agne | maghavatsu | dhāraya | anāmi | kṣatram | ajaram | suvīryam | vayam | jayema | śatinam | sahasriṇam | vaiśvānara | vājam | agne | tava | ūtibhiḥ || 6 ||
he agne asmākam |
saptamyarthe ṣaṣṭhī |
asmāsu maghavatsu havirlakṣaṇena dhanena yukteṣu kṣatraṃ dhanaṃ dhāraya sthāpaya |
kīdṛśam |
anāmi anamanīyamanavahāryam ajaraṃ jararahitamanaśvaraṃ suvīryaṃ śobhanavīryopetam |
kiṃca he vaiśvānara agne tavotibhiḥ tvatsaṃbandhibhī rakṣaṇaiḥ vayaṃ śatinaṃ śata-saṃkhyāyuktaṃ śatapuruṣayuktaṃ vā tathā sahasriṇaṃ vājam annaṃ jayema labhemahi ||
ardabdhebhistvaṃ gopābhiriṣṭe 'smākaṃ pāhi tripadhastha sūrīn |
rakṣā ca no duduṣāṃ śardho agne vaiśvānara pra ca tārīḥ stavānaḥ ||
7 ||
ardabdhebhiḥ | tava | gopābhiḥ | iṣṭe | asmākam | pāhi | tri'padhastha | sūrīn | rakṣa | ca | naḥ | duduṣām | śardhaḥ | agne | vaiśvānara | pra | ca | tārīḥ | stavānaḥ || 7 ||
he iṣṭe yajñasya eṣaṇīya vā tripadhastha triṣu lokeṣu sahāvasthāyin yadvā āhavanīyādiṣu triṣvā-yataneṣu yajñairdevaiḥ sahāvatiṣṭhamānāgne adabdhebhiḥ kenāpyahiṃsitaiḥ tava tvadīyaiḥ gopābhiḥ gopakaiḥ rakṣakaistejobhiḥ asmākaṃ sūrīn asmadīyān stotṛn pāhi rakṣa |
he vaiśvānara agne daduṣāṃ havīṣi dattavatāṃ naḥ asmākaṃ śardhaḥ balaṃ rakṣa ca |
stavānaḥ stūyamānastvaṃ pra ca tārīḥ pravardhaya ca ||
10 ||
'ahaśca' iti saptacaṃ navamaṃ sūktaṃ bharadvājasyārṣaṃ vaiśvānarāgnidevatākam |
anukrāntaṃ ca—'ahaśca' iti |
vyūḻahe daśarātre paṣṭhe'hanyāprimāttaśastre vaiśvānaranividdānamidam |
sūyate hi—'ahaśca kṛṣṇaṃ macco vo nāma' (āśva. śrau. 8. 8) iti ||
ahaśca kṛṣṇamahararjunaṃ ca vi vartate rajasī vedyābhiḥ |
vaiśvānaro jāyamāno na rājātirjyotiṣāmāstamāṃsi ||
1 ||
ahaḥ | iti | kṛṣṇam | ahaḥ | arjunam | ca | vi | vartate | rajasī | vedyābhiḥ | vaiśvānaraḥ | jāyamānaḥ | na | rājā | ati | jyotiṣā | agniḥ | tamāṃsi ||1||
1. śa—nitarāṃ chind |
āharati puruṣo'smin karmāṇīti vaahaḥ|
vakṛṣṇaṃ kṛṣṇavarṇam|
etatsāmānādhikaraṇyādahaḥśabdo rātrivacanaḥ|
tamasā kṛṣṇavarṇā rātriḥ vaca vaarjunaṃ vaca saurena tejasā śuklavarṇam vaahaḥ|
divasaśca varajasī svasvabhāsā sarvaṃ jagadrañjayantī vavedyābhiḥ veditavyābhiranukūlatayā jñātavyābhiḥ svapravṛttibhiḥ|
vavi vartate vividhaṃ paryāvartate|
yadvā|
rajaso dyāvāpṛthivyau|
upalakṣaṇametat|
lokatrayaṃ pratyāvartate|
etacca vavaiśvānarāmerājyā1 iti śeṣaḥ|
sa hi devatātvenātra pratipādyaḥ|
sa ca vavaiśvānaraḥ vaagniḥ vajāyamāno vana varājā prādurbhavan pravadhamānaḥ rājeva vajyotiṣā tejasā vatamāṃsi vaavātiran avatirativadhakarmā|
avanirati vināśayati|
nāhaṃ tantuṃ na vi jānāmyotuṃ na yaṃ vayanti samare'rtamānāḥ|
kasya svitputra iha vaktvāni puro vadātyavareṇa pitrā|
2|
na| aham| tantum| na| vi| jānāmi| otum| na| yam| vayanti| sam|are| artamānāḥ| kasya| sthit| putraḥ| iha| vaktvāni| puraḥ| vadāti| avareṇa| pitrā|| 2||
vaiśvānarasya mahasvamālyāsyasṛṣṭistadarthaṃ yajñaṃ vakṣyātmakatayā rūpayan tasya durjñānatvamanayā prati- pādayatīti yajñavādino manyante|
vatantum|
tantavaḥ paṭasya prāgāyatāni sūtrāṇi|
tāni ca yajñātmakasya vakṣasya gāyatrādīni chandāṃsi stutaśastrāṇi ca|
tāni ca ahaṃ vana vavi jānāmi|
tathā vaotum|
otavistaraśrīnāni sūtrāṇi|
tāni cātra vaadhyādyavayāṇi karmāṇi ca|
tānyahaṃ vana vi jānāmi|
api caitadubhayasādhyaṃ taṃ paṭaṃ yajñalakṣaṇaṃ vana vi jānāmi vayaṃ paṭaṃ yajñalakṣaṇaṃ vasamare saṃgamane devayajane vatamānāḥ satataṃ ceṣṭamānā ṛtvijaḥ vavayanti tantūtaṃtvaṃ saṃtanvanti|
vastrarūpeṇa niṣpādayanti ityarthaḥ|
vaiha asmin loke vakasya vasvit|
sviditi vitarke|
kasya khalu vaputraḥ manuṣyaḥ vavaktvāni vaktvāni tānīmāni vaparaḥ parastādamuṣmin loke vartamāno yaḥ sūryastasya vapitrā vaavareṇa avastāt asmin loke vartamānena vaiśvānarābhinnānuśiṣṭaḥ san vavadāti vadet|
na kaścidapi pravadituṃ śaknotītyarthaḥ|
etacca saṃpradāyavidbhiruktaṃ- ‘vaiśvānarasya putro'sau parastādiva yaḥ sthitaḥ|
chandāṃsyadhvaravakṣasya stuta- śastrāṇi tantavaḥ|
yajūṃṣi ceṣṭāśrautaḥ syādvaruṃ vātavyamadhvaraḥ|
paraḥ paraḥ sthitaḥ sūryaḥ pitāgniḥ pārthivo mataḥḥ’ iti|
rūpakatayā jagatsṛṣṭerjñānatvamanayā pratipādayatītyātmavido manyante|
tantuṃ tantūn tantu- nyoturusthānīyānyapi viyadādīni na vijānāmi|
na ca tatkāryaṃ paṭasthānīyaṃ prapañcaṃ vijānāmi yaṃ prapañcaṃ samare tantūnāmotūnāṃ ca saṃgamane'tamānāḥ satataṃ ceṣṭamānāḥ saṃsāriṇo vayanti utpādayanti|
teṣāṃ bhogārthamīśvaraḥ sṛjatīti kartṛtvamupacaryate|
ihāsminviṣaye paraḥ parastādbuddheraviṣaye vartamānāni vaktvāni vaktvānīmānyavareṇārvācīnena sṛṣṭyuttarakālamutpannena pitrā svajanakenānuśiṣṭaḥ san kasya khalu putrāṃ vadāti|
vadet|
svotpatteḥ prācīnaṃ vṛttāntaṃmajānānaḥ kaścidapi na vadedityarthaḥ|
1. kha-jha-ta-na-bha-ra-’ svapravṛttibhiḥ ’ nāsti; śa-svavṛttibhiḥ |
2. gha-etasya |
3. kha-jha-na-bha- vaiśvānarāmerājyā; ra-vaiśvānarājyā; śa-vaiśvānaro rājñaḥ |
sa ittaṃtuṃ sa vi jānātyotuṃ sa vakvānyututhā vadāti |
ya īṃ ciketadamṛtasya gopā avaścaranpuro anyena paśyan ||
3 ||
saḥ | ita | tantum | saḥ | vi | jānāti | otum | saḥ | vakvāni | ṛtu'thā | vadāti | yaḥ | īm | ciketat | amṛtasya | gopāḥ | atra | caran | puraḥ | anyena | paśyan || 3 ||
yadyapi uktena prakāreṇa durjñānāni tathāpyetāni vaiśvānaro'gniḥ jānāti vadati cetanayā pratipādayati |
sa itta sa eva vaiśvānaro'gniḥ tantuṃ tantusthānīyāni gāyatryādīni chandāṃsi stutaśastrāṇi vi jānāti tathā saḥ eva otum otasthānīyāni yajuṣyādhvaryavāṇi ca karmāṇi vi jānāti |
ṛtuthā kāle kāle tattadanuṣṭhānasamaye vakvāni vaktavyāni ca tāni vadāti vadet |
yaḥ ayaṃ vaiśvānaraḥ amṛtasya gopāḥ udakasya gopāyitā rakṣitā avaḥ avastāt bhūloke caran pārthivāmirūpeṇa saṃcaran paraḥ parastāddivi anyena sūryātmanā paśyan sarvaṃ jagat prakāśayan īm imāni paridṛśyamānāni sarvāṇi bhūtāni ciketat jānāti sa eveti pūrvetra sambandhaḥ |
yadvā |
sa ita sa eva tantuṃ tantusthānīyāni sūkṣmabhūtāni vi jānāti nānyaḥ kaścit |
tathautumotasthānīyāni sthūlabhūtāni ca sa eva vi jānāti |
sa eva vakvāni vaktavyānpudeṣyamānīmānyututhā kāle kāle yadā yadā vidyāsaṃpradāyocchedastadā tadā vadāti vadet |
kausau yo vijānīyādadvetyena āha |
yo vaiśvānaro viśvānaramakaḥ paramātmāmṛtasyāmṛtatvasya vimokṣaṇasya gopā rakṣitāvā'vastān saṃsāradaśāyāṃ caran antaḥkaraṇopetaḥ jīvātmabhāvena maṃcaran paraḥ parastādavidyāyā ūrdhvaṃ vartamānenānyenauktavilakṣaṇena nirupādhikena saccidādilakṣaṇena rūpeṇa paśyan sarvaṃ jagat prakāśayan īmimāni ciketat jānāti |
tathā ca paramātmānaṃ prakṛtya śrūyate— ‘tameva bhāntamanubhāti sarvaṃ tasya bhāsā sarvamidaṃ vibhāti’ (śve. u. 6.14) iti ||
ayaṃ hotā prathamaḥ paśyatemamidaṃ jyotiramṛtaṃ martyeṣu |
ayaṃ sa jajñe dhruva ā niṣatto'martyastanvā vardhamānaḥ ||
4 ||
ayam | hotā | prathamaḥ | paśyat | imam | idam | jyotiḥ | amṛtam | martyeṣu | ayam | saḥ | jajñe | dhruvaḥ | ā | ni'sattaḥ | amartyaḥ | tanvā | vardhamānaḥ || 4 ||
ayaṃ vaiśvānaro'gniḥ prathamaḥ ādyaḥ hotā |
mānuṣo hi hotā dvitīyaḥ |
he manuṣyastam imaṃ paśyata bhajatatyarthaḥ |
martyeṣu maraṇasvabhāveṣu śarīriṣu amṛtaṃ maraṇarahitam idaṃ vaiśvānarākhyaṃ jyotiḥ jāṭhararūpeṇa vartata ityarthaḥ |
api ca saḥ ayam agniḥ dhruvaḥ niścalaḥ ā samantāt niṣattaḥ niṣaṇṇaḥ sarvavyāpī ata eva amartyaḥ maraṇarahito'pi tanvā śarīreṇa saṃbandhān jajñe jāyate |
vardhamānaḥ ca bhavatīti upacaryate ||
1. śa-uktaprakāreṇa | 2. śa-antaḥkaraṇopetena | 3 śa-maraṇasvabhāvaśarīreṣu | 4. sva--ucyate |
dhruvaṃ jyotirnihitaṃ dṛśaye kaṃ mano javiṣṭhaṃ patayatsvantaḥ | viśve devāḥ samanasaḥ saketā ekaṃ kratumabhi vi yanti sādhu || 5 || dhruvam | jyotiḥ | ni'hitam | dṛśaye | kam | manaḥ | javiṣṭham | patayat'su | antariti | viśve | devāḥ | ma'samanasaḥ | ma'saketāḥ | ekam | kratum | abhi | vi | yanti | sādhu || 5 ||
vdhruvaṃ niścalaṃ vmanaḥ manasaḥ tasmādapi vjaviṣṭham atiśayena vegavat īdṛśaṃ vaiśvānarasya vjyotiḥ |
vpatayatsu gacchatsu jaṅgameṣu prāṇiṣu vantaḥ madhye vnihitaṃ prajāpatinā sthāpitam |
kimartham |
vdṛśaye vkam |
darśanārtham |
kiṃca vviśve sarve vdevāḥ ca vsamanasaḥ samānamanaskāḥ vsaketāḥ samāna- prajñāśca santaḥ vekaṃ mukhyaṃ gantāraṃ vā vkratuṃ karmaṇāṃ kartāraṃ vsādhu samyak vabhi vvi vyanti ābhi- mukhyena vividhaṃ prāpnuvanti sevanta ityarthaḥ |
yadvā |
patayatsu gacchatsu prāṇiṣvantarmadhye hṛdaye mano javiṣṭhaṃ manaso'pyatiśayena vegayuktaṃ dhruvaṃ niścalaṃ nirvikalpam |
tathā ca vājasaneyakam—‘anejadekaṃ manaso javīyaḥ’ (vā. saṃ. 40, 4) iti |
jyotirbrahma caitanyaṃ nihitam |
na kenacit sthāpitam |
‘yo veda nihitaṃ guhāyāṃ parame vyoman’ (tai. ā. 8. 1) iti hi śrūyate |
kimartham |
dṛśaye darśanārtham |
jñānena hi sarvaṃ jānanti |
api ca |
dīvyantīti devā indriyāṇi |
viśve sarve devāḥ sarvāṇīndriyāṇi cakṣurādyāḥ samanaso manasā saha vartamānāḥ saketāḥ satejaskāḥ santaḥ ekamadvitīyaṃ kratuṃ sṛṣṭyādīnāṃ karmaṇāṃ kartāraṃ viśvanaratmakaṃ paramātmānamabhilakṣya sādhu samyak vi yanti vividhaṃ gacchanti |
devā eva vemamabhi vi yanti |
ābhimukhyena vividhamupayanti |
upāsata ityarthaḥ |
tathā ca śrūyate—‘tadevā jyotiṣāṃ jyotirāyurhopāmate'mṛtam’ (bṛ. u. 4. 4. 16) iti ||
vi me karṇā patayato vi cakṣuridaṃ jyotirhṛdaya āhitaṃ yat | vi me manaścarati dūraādhīḥ kiṃ svidvakṣyāmi kimu nū maniṣye || 6 || vi | me | karṇā | patayataḥ | vi | cakṣuḥ | idam | jyotiḥ | hṛdaye | ā'hitam | yat | vi | me | manaḥ | carati | dūre'ādhīḥ | | kim | svit | vakṣyāmi | kim | u | nū | maniṣye || 6 ||
vaiśvānaraṃ śrotukāmasya vme mama vkarṇā karṇau vvi vpatayataḥ vividhaṃ gacchataḥ |
śrotavyānāṃ tadīya- guṇānāṃ bahutvāt |
tathā vaiśvānaraṃ didṛkṣamāṇasya mama vcakṣuḥ indriyaṃ vvi patayati vividhaṃ gacchati |
draṣṭavyānāṃ tadīyarūpāṇāṃ bahutvāt |
tathā vjyotiḥ prakāśakaṃ vhṛdaye hṛdayapuṇḍarīke vāhitaṃ nihitaṃ vyat buddhyākhyaṃ tattvam vidamapi vvi patayati vividhaṃ gacchati vaiśvānarātmānaṃ jñātum |
api ca vdūra- ādhīḥ |

11 |
‘puro vaḥ ’ iti saptacaṃ daśamaṃ sūktaṃ. bharadvājasyārṣamāgneyam |
saptamī Vdiśikā dvipadā virāṭ śiṣṭāstriṣṭubhaḥ |
tathā cānukrāntaṃ—‘puro vo dvipadāntam ’ iti |
prātaranuvāka āpriye ṛtau traiṣṭubhe chandasīdamādīni catvāri sūktāni |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘puro vo mandramiti catvāri taṃ supratīkamiti ṣaṭ ’ ( āśva. śrau. 4. 13 ) iti |
puro vo mandraṃ divyaṃ suvṛktiṃ prayati yajñe agrimadhvare dadhidhvam |
pura ukthebhiḥ sa hi nau vibhāvā svadhvarā karati jātavedāḥ |
1 |
puraḥ | vaḥ | mandram | divyam | su'vṛktim | pra'yati | yajñe | agrim | adhvare | dadhidhvam | puraḥ | ukthebhiḥ | saḥ | hi | nau | tri'trāṃ | su'adhvaraḥ | karati | jāta'vedāḥ || 1 ||
he ṛtvigyajamānāḥ Vvaḥ yūyaṃ Vmandraṃ modanaṃ stutyaṃ vā Vdivyaṃ divi bhavaṃ Vsuvṛktiṃ suṣṭhu doṣai- rvarjitaṃ sukhenavarjanīyaṃ vā evaṃguṇam Vagniṃ Vprayati pragacchati pravartamāne Vadhvare rakṣaḥprabhṛtibhirahiraye hiṃsāpratyavāyarahite vāsmin Vyajñe Vpuraḥ Vdadhidhvaṃ purastādāhavanīyarūpeṇa dhārayata |
yadvā |
purodhasaṃ kuruta |
Vukthebhiḥ stutaśca Vpuraḥ dadhidhvam |
Vvibhāvā viśeṣeṇa dīpyamānaḥ Vjātavedāḥ jātānāṃ veditā Vsaḥ Vagniḥ Vhi yasmāt Vnaḥ asmān Vsvadhvarā śobhanayāgān Vkarati karoti tasmāt puro dadhidhvamityanvayaḥ |
tapuṃ dyumaḥ purvaṇīka hotarmai agnirmartuṣa idhānaḥ |
stomaṃ yamasmai mamattaiva śuṣaṃ ghṛtaṃ na śurci mataryaḥ pavante |

tam | ū~ iti | dyum | puru'anīka | hotaḥ | agne | agni'bhiḥ | martuṣaḥ | idhānaḥ | stomam | yam | asmai | mamattaḥ'iva | śuṣam | ghṛtam | na | śurci | mataryaḥ | pavante || 2 ||
1. kha-gha-bha-vaiśvānara agne |
2. jñāta-ra- ya: |
he dyuma: dīptiman purvaṇīka bahujvāla hota: devānāmāhvāta: agne abhibhi: tavāvayava- bhūtairanyairagnibhi: sārdham idhāna: samidhyamāna: dīsa: san manuṣa: manuṣyasya stotu: tamu taṃ stomaṃ śṛṇviti śeṣa: |
u iti pādapūraṇam |
yaṃ stomaṃ stotraṃ śūpaṃ sukhakaraṃ ghṛtaṃ na pavitrāmyām utpūtaṃ ghṛtamiva śuci śuddham asmai agnaye mataya: mantāra: stotāra: mamateva |
mamatā nāma brahmavādinī dīrghatamaso mātā |
seva pavante punanti saṃskurvanti doṣavarjitamuccārayantītyartha: |
taṃ stomamityanvaya: ||
pīpāya sa śravasā matyeṃṣu yo agnaye dadāśa vipra ukthai: | citrābhistūtibhiśśiśrcitrajasya mātā gormato dadhāti || 3 || pīpāya | sa: | śravasā | matyeṃṣu | ya: | agnaye | dadāśa | vipra: | ukthai: | citrābhi: | tam | ūti'bhi: | citra'śauci: | vrajasya | mātā | go'rmata: | dadhāti ||3||
sa: 1 yajamāna: matyeṃṣu manuṣyeṣu 2 madhye śravasā annena pīpāya vardhate vipra: medhāvī ya: yajamāna: ukthai: stutibhi: sārdhaṃ havīṃṣi dadāśa prayacchati |
taṃ yajamānaṃ citraśoci: vicitra- dīptiragni: citrābhi: āścaryabhūtābhi: ūtibhi: rakṣābhi: 2 gomata: gobhiryuktasya vrajasya goṣṭhasya sātā sānau saṃbhajane dadhāti dhārayati ||
ā ya: paprau jāyamāna urvī duredṛśā bhāsā kṛṣṇādhvā | ardha bahu cittam ūrmīyāstira: śociṣā dadaśe pāvaka: || 4 || ā | ya: | paprau | jāyamāna: | urvī iti | dure'dṛśā | bhāsā | kṛṣṇa'adhvā | ardha | bahu | cittam | ūrmīyā: | tira: | śociṣā | dadaśe | pāvaka: || 4 ||
kṛṣṇādhvā kṛṣṇavartmā ya: agni: jāyamāna: prādurbhavan urvī vistīrṇaṃ dyāvāpṛthivyau dure- dṛśā dūre dṛśyamānayā bhāsā dīhyā ā paprau āpūryati sa: pāvaka: agni: adha anantaram ūrmīyā: rātrināmaiṭat |

nū naścitraṃ puruvājābhirūtī agne rayiṃ maghavadbhyaśca dhehi | ye rādhasā śravasā cātyanyānsuvīryairmiśrābhi santi janān || 5 || nu | na: | citram | puru'vājābhi: | ūtī | agne | rayim | maghavat'bhya: | ca | dhehi | ye | rādhasā | śravasā | ca | ati | anyāna | su'vīryaibhi: | ca | abhi | santi | janān ||5||
1. śa-so'yam |
2. jha-na-madhye; jha-tara-ra-manuṣyeṣu |
3. kha-jha-ta-na-bha-ya-ra-śa-’ūtibhī rakṣābhi: ’ nāsti |
he ᵛagne ᵛmaghavann‌ya: havirlakṣaṇadhanayuktebhya: ᵛna: asmabhyaṃ ᵛpurujābhi: bahulai: ᵛūtī ūtibhi: rakṣaṇai: saha ᵛcitraṃ cāyanīyaṃ ᵛrayiṃ dhanaṃ ᵛnu kṣipraṃ ᵛdhehi dehi prayaccha |
vaśabdāṃ vakṣyamāṇena samucca- yārtha: |
ᵛye ᵛrādhasā dhanena ᵛśravasā agnena ᵛca ᵛsuvīryaibhi: śobhanairvīryaiśca ᵛanyān ᵛjanān manuṣyān ᵛati atiśayena ᵛabhi ᵛsanti abhivanti tādṛśān putrāṃścāsmabhyaṃ dehītyartha: ||
imaṃ yajñaṃ canau dhā ag uśanyaṃ te āsāno juhute haviṣmān |

imam | yajñam | cana: | dhā: | agne | uśan | yam | te | āsāna: | juhute | haviṣmān | bharadvājeṣu | dadhiṣe | suvṛktim | avī: | vājasya | gadhyasya | sātau || 6 ||
he ᵛagne ᵛuśan kāmayamānastvam ᵛimaṃ purovarti ᵛyajñaṃ yāgasādhanaṃ ᵛcana: havirlakṣaṇadhanaṃ ᵛdhā: dhehi |
svātmani dhāraya |
ᵛyaṃ ca puroḍāśādikam ᵛāsāna: āsīna' upaviśan' ᵛhaviṣmān haviryukto yajamāna: ᵛte tubhyaṃ tvadarthaṃ ᵛjuhute juhoti tamimamityanvaya: |
api ca ᵛbharadvājeṣu ṛṣiṣu ᵛsuvṛktiṃ suṣṭhu dopairvarjitāṃ stutiṃ ᵛdadhiṣe dhāraya |
ᵛgadhyasya |
gadhyatimiśrībhāvakarmā |

vi dveṣoṃsīnuhi vardhayeḻāṃ madeṃ śatahimā: suvīrā: ||

‘yajasva hota:’ iti ṣaṭmekādaśaṃ sūktaṃ bharadvājasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamāgneyam |
anukramyate ca— ‘yajasva ṣaṭ’ iti |
prātaranuvākāśvinaśastrayoryukto viniyoga: ||
yajasva hotariṣito yajīyānagne bādhau marutāṃ na prayukti |
ā nau mitrāvaruṇā nāsatyā dyāvā hotrārya pṛthivī vavṛtvā: ||
1 ||
yajasva | hota: | iṣita: | yajīyān | agne | bādha: | marutām | na | pra'yukti | ā | na: | mitrāvaruṇā | nāsatyā | dyāvā | hotrārya | pṛthivī iti | vavṛtvā: || 1 ||

2. śa-asmānurṣānavātsā: |

api ca ᵥmitrāvaruṇā |
aharabhimānī devo mitro varuṇo rātryabhimānī |
tau ca ᵥnāsatyā satyasya netārau satyasvabhāvāveva vā nāsikāprabhavau vāśvinau ᵥdyāvāpṛthivī divaṃ ca pṛthivīṃ ca etāṃśca devān ᵥhotrāya asmadyajñāya tadartham ᵥā ᵥvavṛttyāḥ āvartya āvahetyarthaḥ ||

‘madhye hotā’ iti ṣaṭkaṃ dvādaśa sūktaṃ bharadvājasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamāgneyam |
‘madhye’ ityanukrāntam |
prātaranuvākābhinaśastrayorukto viniyoga: |
madhye hotā duroṇe barhiṣo rāḻamṛstodasya rodasī yajadhyai |
arya sa sūnu: sahasa ṛtāvā dūrātsūryo na śociṣā tāna |
1 |
madhye | hotā | duroṇe | barhiṣa: | gat | agni: | todasya | rodasī iti | yajadhyai | ayam | sa: | sūnu: | sahasa: | ṛta'vā | dūrāt | sūrya: | na | śociṣā | tāna ||1||
vhomā devānāmāhvātā vbarhiṣa: yajñasya vrāṭ rājā vagni: v
tejiṣṭhā yasya̭ratirvainerāṭ todo a॒dhvana॒ vṛdha॒sāno adyaut | adrogho na dravi̭tā caitati tmanama̭rtyo'vṛtra oṣadhīṣu || 3 || tejiṣṭhā | yasya̭ | aratiḥ | vanḙ'rāṭ | todaḥ | a॒dhvan | na | vṛdha॒sānaḥ | adyaut | adroghaḥ | na | dravi̭tā | cetati̭ | tman | ama̭rtyaḥ | avṛtraḥ | oṣadhīṣu || 3 ||
vyasya agneḥ varatiḥ gantrī jvālā vtejiṣṭhā atiśayena tejasvinī satī vvanerāṭ dāvarūpeṇa araṇye rājamānā vartate vvṛdhasānaḥ vardhamānaḥ so'gniḥ vtodo vadhvanaḥ |
todaḥ sarvasya prerakaḥ sūryaḥ |
sa ivādhvani svamārge'ntarikṣe vadyaut dyotate prakāśate |
api ca vadrogho vna adroghaghnaḥ |
yadvā |
prāṇarūpeṇa sarvasmādadroghnā vāyuriva tādṛśaḥ vamartyaḥ maraṇarahitaḥ so'gniḥ voṣadhīṣu |
oṣaḥ pāka eṣu dhīyata ityoṣadhayo vanāni |
teṣu vdravi tā śīghragāmī san vavṛtraḥ kenāpyavāraṇīyaśca bhavan vtman ātmanā svaprakāśenaiva vcetati cetayati sarvaṃ jagat jñāpayati ||
sāsmākeṃbhiretarī na śubhai̭ragniḥ dhruve dam ā jātavedāḥ | drubho va॒nvana॒ kratvā nāvī̭saḥ piteva jā॒ryāyi ya॒jñaiḥ || 4 || saḥ | a॒smākeṃbhiḥ | e॒tar | na | śubhaiḥ | a॒gniḥ | stavḙ | damḙ | ā | jā॒ta'vedāḥ | du'a̭nnaḥ | va॒nvana॒ | kratvā̭ | na | a॒rvā | u॒sraḥ | pi॒tā'i̭va | jā॒ryāyi | ya॒jñaiḥ || 4 ||
vjātavedāḥ jātadhano jātaprajño vā vsaḥ vagniḥ vasmākeṃbhiḥ asmadīyaiḥ stotrabhiḥ vetarī3 vna |

vusraḥ vpiteva |

14 ||
‘tvadviśvā’ iti ṣaṭkaṃ trayodaśaṃ sūktaṃ bharadvājasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamāgneyam |
‘tvadviśvā’ ityanu- krāntam |

tvadviśvā subhaga saubhagānyagne vi yanti vunino na vayāḥ |

1 ||

'imam ṣu vaḥ' ityekonaviṃśatyṛcaṃ pañcadaśaṃ sūktamāṅgirasasya vītahavyasya bharadvājasya vārṣamāmeyam |
ādau nava jagatyastṛtīyāpaṭhyau tu śakyatiśakyau pañcadaśī śakvarī sasadasyanuṣṭubdhaṣṭādaśī bṛhatī daśamyāthāḥ pañca ṣoḍaśyekonaviṃśī ceti sapta triṣṭubhaḥ |

1. kha-gha-bha - januṣā ita |
^adbhuta mahanme ^saḥ tādṛśaḥ ^tvaṃ ^vītahavya etatsajña ṛṣau ^suprīta: suṣṭhu prīyamāṇo bhava |
yataḥ ^divedive pratidinaṃ ^praśastibhi: prakṛtai: stotrai: ^mahayase pūjyase |
bharadvāja ṛṣiśceta vītahavye dattahaviṣke bharadvāja iti yojanīyam ||
sa tvaṃ dakṣyāvṛko vṛdho bhūryaḥ parasvāntarasya taruṣaḥ |
rāya: sūno sahaso martyeṣvā chardiryaccha vītahavyāya sapratho bharadvājāya saprathaḥ ||
3||
saḥ | tvam | dakṣasya | avṛkaḥ | vṛdhaḥ | bhūḥ | aryaḥ | parasya | antarasya | taruṣaḥ | rāyaḥ | sūno iti | sahasaḥ | martyeṣu | ā | chardiḥ | yaccha | vītahavyāya | saprathaḥ | bharadvājāya | saprathaḥ || 3 ||
he agne ^saḥ tādṛśaḥ ^avṛkaḥ bādhakarahitaḥ ^tvaṃ ^dakṣasya anuṣṭhānasamarthasya ^vṛdhaḥ vardhayitā ^bhūḥ bhavasi |
tathā ^parasya viprakṛṣṭasya ^aryaḥ areḥ śatroḥ ^antarasya antikatarasya saṃnikṛṣṭasya śatroḥ ^taruṣaḥ taritā bhavasi |
ataḥ kāraṇāt he ^sahasaḥ ^sūno ^saprathaḥ sarvataḥ pṛthustvaṃ ^martyeṣu manuṣyeṣu madhye ^bharadvājāya saṃbhṛtahavirlakṣaṇāyāya ^vītahavyāya vīnaṃ gamitaṃ havyaṃ haviyaina tādṛśāya bharadvājā- yeti vā yojyam |
^rāyaḥ ^dhanāni ^chardiḥ gṛhaṃ ^ā ^yaccha prayaccha |

4||

^ghṛtaṃ dīvyamānaṃ ^vaḥ yuṣmākam ^atithim atithi vatpūjyaṃ ^svarvaraṃ svargasya netāraṃ ^manuṣaḥ manaḥ prajāpateryajñe ^hotāraṃ devānāmāhvātāraṃ ^svadhvaraṃ śobhanayajñaṃ ^vipraṃ ^na medhāvinaṃ vipaścitamiva ^dyukṣavarcasam |
dīpternivāsabhūtaṃ dyukṣam |
tādṛśavāggopetam ^aratim arya svāminam ||
1. kha-jha-na-bha-ya-ra-sapratha iti punaruktirādarārthā ( kha-jha-ra-2 'evaṃvidhastvaṃ nāsti ) rāyo dhanāni chardigṛhaṃ cāyaccha prayaccha; ta-sapratha iti punaruktirādarārthā |
pāvakayā yaścitayantyā kṛpā kṣāmnuruca uṣaso na bhānunā |
turvvan yāmubhetaśasya nū raṇa ā yo ghṛṇe na tattuṣāṇo ajareḥ |
5 |
pāvakayā | yaḥ | citayantyā | kṛpā | kṣāman | ruruce | uṣasaḥ | na | bhānunā | turvvan | na | yāman | etaśasya | nu | raṇe | ā | yaḥ | ghṛṇe | na | tattuṣāṇaḥ | ajareḥ || 5 ||
vyaḥ agniḥ vpāvakayā śodhayitryā vcitayantyā cetayantyā prajñāpayantyā vkṛpā dīptyā vkṣāman bhumyāṃ vruruce dīpyate |
vuṣaso vna vbhānunā |
yathoṣasaḥ prakāśena bhāsante tadvat |
api ca vyāman yāmani saṃgrāme vturvvan śatrūn hiṃsan purupa iva vetaśasya etatsaṃjñakasya ṛṣeḥ vraṇe sūryeṇa saha saṃgrāme sahāyārthaṃ vyaḥ agniḥ vnu kṣipram vā vghṛṇe ādīpyate |
'etaśaṃ sūrye paruṣṭānam'
samiddhamagniṃ samidhā girā gṛṇe śuciṃ pāvakaṃ puro adhvare dhruvam | vipraṃ hotāraṃ puruvāramadruhaṃ kaviṃ sumnairīmahe jātavedasam || 7 || sam'iddham | agnim | sam'idhā | girā | gṛṇe | śucim | pāvakam | puraḥ | adhvare | dhruvam | vipram | hotāram | puru'vāram | adruham | kavim | sumnaiḥ | īmahe | jāta'vedasam || 7 ||
samiddhaṃ samyagdīptam agniṃ samidhā samindhanahetubhūtayā girā stutyā gṛṇe staumi |
yadvā |
samidhā samiddhārhabhiḥ samiddhaṃ samyagdīptam |
api ca śuciṃ svayaṃ śuddhaṃ pāvakaṃ sarveṣāṃ śodhakaṃ dhruvaṃ niścalaṃ tamagnim adhvare yajñe puraskaromīti śeṣaḥ |
tathā vipraṃ medhāvinaṃ hotāraṃ devānā- māhvātāraṃ puruvāraṃ bahubhirvarṇīyam adruhaṃ adṛgdhvāraṃ sarvaipāmanukūlaṃ kaviṃ krāntadarśinaṃ jātavedasaṃ jātānāṃ veditāramagniṃ sumnaiḥ sugvakaraiḥ stotraiḥ īmahe saṃbhajāmahe |
yadvā |
dvitīyārthe tṛtīyā |
sumnāni dhanānīmahe yācā mahe ||
tvāṃ dūtaṃmagne amṛtaṃ yugeyuge havyavāhaṃ dadhire pāyumīḍyam | devāsaśca martāsaśca jāgṛviṃ vibhuṃ viśapatiṃ namasā ni ṣedire || 8 || tvām | dūtam | agne | amṛtam | yuge'yuge | havya'vāham | dadhire | pāyum | īḍyam | devāsaḥ | ca | martāsaḥ | ca | jāgṛvim | vibhum | viśapatim | namasā | ni | ṣedire ||8||
he agne tvāṃ devāsaḥ devāḥ ca martāsaḥ manuṣyāḥ ca dūtaṃ dadhire vidadhire kṛtavantaḥ |
kīdṛśaṃ tvām |
amṛtaṃ amaraṇaṃ yugeyuge kāle kāle tattadyāgānuṣṭhānasamaye havyavāhaṃ havyānāṃ haviṣāṃ voḍhāraṃ pāyuṃ pālayitāram īḍyaṃ stutyam |
api ca ta ubhayavidhā jāgṛviṃ jāgaraṇaśīlaṃ vibhuṃ vyāsaṃ viśapatiṃ viśāṃ prajānāṃ pālayitāraṃ tamagniṃ namasā namaskāreṇa havirlakṣaṇānnena vā ni ṣedire upasedire ||
vibhūṣannubhayā~ anu vratā dūto devānāṃ rajasī samīyase | yatai dhītiṃ sumatimāvṛṇīmahe'dha smā nastrivarūthaḥ śivo bhava || 9 || vi'bhūṣan | agne | ubhayān | anu | vratā | dūtaḥ | devānām | rajasī iti | sam | īyase | yata | te | dhītim | su'matim | āvṛṇīmahe | atha | sma | naḥ | tri'varūthaḥ | śivaḥ | bhava ||9||
he agne ubhayān ubhayavidhān devān manuṣyāṃśca vibhūṣan viśeṣeṇa bhūṣayan alaṃkurvaṃstvam anu vratā vratānyanu vrateṣu karmasu yāgeṣu devānāṃ dūtaḥ san rajasī dyāvāpṛthivyau samīyase saṃcarasi |
devānāmetuṃ gulokaṃ gacchasi havīṃṣi ca netumiyaṃ lokam |
kiṃca yat yasmāt te tubhyaṃ tvadartha
vbhītiṃ karma vsumatiṃ śobhanāṃ stutiṃ ca vāvṛṇīmahe vayaṃ saṃbhajāmahe vadha ataḥ kāraṇāt vtrivarūthaḥ trisyānastvaṃ vnaḥ asmākaṃ vśivaḥ sukhakaraḥ vbhava|
vsma ityetat pādapūraṇam ||
prātaranuvākādhinaśasyorāmeye kratau traiṣṭubhe chandasi ‘taṃ supratīkam ’ ityādyāḥ ṣaṭcaḥ |
sutritaṃ ca—‘taṃ supratīkamiti ṣaṭ ’ (āśva. śrau. 4. 13 ) iti ||
taṃ supratīkaṃ sudṛśaṃ svaśvamavidvāṃso viduṣṭaraṃ sapema |
sa yakṣadviśvā vayunāni vidvāntra havyamagiramṛteṣu vocat ||
10 ||
tam | su'pratīkam | su'dṛśam | su'aśvam | avidvāṃsaḥ | viduḥ'taram | sapema | saḥ | yakṣat | viśvā | vayunāni | vidvān | pra | havyam | agniḥ | amṛteṣu | vocat || 10||
vavidvāṃsaḥ vaidupyarahitā alpamatayo vayaṃ vviduṣṭaraṃ vidvattaraṃ sarvajñaṃ vtam agniṃ vsapema paricarema |
kīdṛśam ? vsupratīkaṃ śobhanāṅgaṃ vsudṛśaṃ śobhanadraṣṭāraṃ vsvaśvaṃ suṣṭhvaśvaṃ gacchantam |
kiṃca vsaḥ tādṛśaḥ agniḥ vyakṣat devān yajatu |
vviśvā sarvāṇi vvayunāni |
jñānametata |
iha tu jñātavye vartate |
jñātavyamarthajātāni vvidvān jānan vagniḥ vamṛteṣu maraṇahiteṣu deveṣu vhavyam asmadīyaṃ haviḥ vpra vvocat prabravītu |
yuṣmadarthaṃ haviḥ kalpitaṃ tadarthaṃ yūyamāgacchatetyevaṃ kathayatu ||
18 ||
tarmagre pāsyuta taṃ piparṣi yasta ānaṭkavaye śūra dhītim |
yajñasya vā niśitiṃ vorditiṃ vā tamitpṛṇakṣi śavaso rāyā ||
11 ||
tam | agne | pāsi | uta | tam | piparṣi | yaḥ | te | ānaṭ | kavaye | śūra | dhītim | yajñasya | vā | ni'śitima | vā | uta | ītim | vā | tam | it | pṛṇakṣi | śavasā | uta | rāyā || 11||
he vagne vtaṃ puruṣaṃ vpāsi rakṣasi |
vuta api ca vtaṃ vpiparṣi kāmaiḥ pūrayasi |
vyaḥ puruṣo he vśūra śauryavattvame vkavaye krāntadarśanāya vte tubhyaṃ svadadhaṃ vdhītiṃ karma stutiṃ vā vānaṭ prāpnoti |
yadvā |
‘kriyāgrahaṇaṃ kartavyam ’ iti karmaṇaḥ saṃpradānatvāccaturthī |
kaviṃ tvāṃ dhītimānaṭ prāpayati |
taṃ pāsītyanvayaḥ |
api ca vyajñasya yāgasya vvā tsādhanabhūtasya haviṣo vā vniśitiṃ vvā |
niśitiḥ saṃskāraḥ |
taṃ vā vuditim udgamanaṃ vvā ānaṭ prāpayati vtamin tameva vśavasā balena vpṛṇakṣi pūrayasi |
vuta api ca vrāyā dhanena pūrayasi³ ||
tvamagne vanuṣyato ni pāhi tvaṃ naḥ sahasāvanvaghāt |
saṃ tvā dhvasmanvadbhyaitu pāthaḥ saṃ rayiḥ spṛhayāryaḥ sahasrī ||
12 ||
tvam | agne | vanuṣyataḥ | ni | pāhi | tvam | naḥ | sahasā'van | aghāt | sam | tvā | dhvasman'vat | abhi | etu | pāthaḥ | sam | rayiḥ | spṛhayāryaḥ | sahasrī || 12 ||
1. jña-ta-ya-śobhanaṃ draṣṭāram |
2. gha-śa-utāpi |
3. kha-jha-jha-ta1-na-bha-ya-ra- ca pūrayasi; gha-ta1-āpūrayasi
he 'agne 'tvaṃ 'vanuṣyataḥ hiṃsakācchatroḥ 'ni nitarāmasmāgrakṣa |
he 'sahasāvan balavannagne 'tvasu tvameva 'naḥ asmān 'avadyāt pāpāt nipāhi |
'tvā tvāṃ 'dhvasamanvat dhvaṃsanavat dhvastadoṣaṃ 'pāthaḥ havirlakṣaṇamasmābhirdattamanaṃ 'sam 'abhyetu samyagabhigacchatu |
'spṛhāyāyaḥ spṛhaṇīyaḥ 'sahasrī sahasrasaṃkhyāyuktaḥ 'rayiḥ tvayā dattaṃ dhanaṃ samyagabhigacchatu ||
vyūḻahe daśarātre pañcame'hanyāgnimārute ‘agnihotā’ iti ṛco jātavedasanididvānīyaḥ |
satriṃtaṃ ca—‘agnihotā gṛhapatiḥ sa rājeti tisraḥ’ (āśva. śrau. 8. 8) iti |
patnīsaṃyājeṣu gṛhapaterādhānuvākyā |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘agnihotā gṛhapatiḥ sa rājā havyavāḻbharajaraḥ pitā na iti patnīsaṃyājāḥ’ (āśva śrau. 1. 10) iti |
ādhinaśastrasyaiva pratipat |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘agnihotā gṛhapatiḥ sa rājeti pratipadekapātinī pacchaḥ’ (āśva. śrau. 6. 5) iti ||
agnihotā gṛhapatiḥ sa rājā viśvā veda janimā jātavedāḥ |
devānāmuta yo martyānāṃ yajiṣṭhaḥ sa pra yajatāmṛtāvā ||
13 ||
agniḥ | hotā | gṛha'patiḥ | saḥ | rājā | viśvā | veda | janimā | jāta'vedāḥ | devānām | uta | yaḥ | martyānām | yajiṣṭhaḥ | saḥ | pra | yajatām | ṛta'vā || 13 ||
'hotā devānāmāhvānāṃ 'rājā rājamānaḥ 'saḥ 'agniḥ 'gṛhapatiḥ gṛhāṇāṃ patiradhipatirbhavati |
tathā 'viśvā viśvāni sarvāṇi 'janima janmāni janmavanti bhūtajātāni 'jātavedāḥ jātaprajñaḥ san 'veda vetti jānāti |
'yaḥ agniḥ 'devānām indrādīnām 'uta api ca 'martyānāṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ ca madhye 'yajiṣṭhaḥ atiśayena yaṣṭā bhavati 'ṛtāvā |
ṛtaṃ satyaṃ yajño vā |
tadvān 'saḥ agniḥ 'pra 'yajatāṃ prakarṣeṇa devān yajatu ||
‘agne yadadya’ iti darśapūrṇamāmayoḥ sviṣṭakṛto yājyā |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘agne yadadya viśo adhvarasya hota rityanavānaṃ yajati’ (āśva. śrau. 1. 6) iti ||
agne yadadya viśo adhvarasya hotaḥ pāvakaśoce veṣṭu hi yajvā |
ṛtā yajāsi mahinā vi yaddhūvyā vaha yaviṣṭha yā tai adya ||
14 ||
agne | yat | adya | viśaḥ | adhvarasya | hotariti | pāvaka'śoce | veḥ | tvam | hi | yajvā | ṛtā | yajāsi | mahinā | vi | yat | bhūḥ | havyā | vaha | yaviṣṭha | yā | te | adya || 14 ||
he 'adhvarasya 'hotaḥ yajñasya niṣpādaka 'pāvakaśoce śodhakadīpte evaṃbhūta he 'agne 'atha asmin kāle 'viśaḥ manuṣyasya yajamānasya 'yat kartavyaṃ tat 'veḥ kāmayasva |
'hi yasmāt 'tvaṃ 'yajvā devānāṃ yaṣṭā bhavasi tasmāt 'ṛtā ṛte yajñe 'yajāsi devān yaja |
api ca 'mahinā mahimnā svamāhātmyena 'yat yasmāt 'vi 'bhūḥ vibhavasi vyāpto bhavasi ataḥ kāraṇāt he 'yaviṣṭha yuvatamāgne 'te tubhyaṃ svadartham 'adya idānīṃ 'yā yāni havyāni juhumastāni 'havyā havyāni havīṃṣi 'vaha |
svakīyāṃ sviṣṭakṛdākhyāṃ tanuṃ prāpaya ||
abhi prayāṃsi sudhitāni hi ravyo ni tvā dadhīta rodasī yajadhyai | arvā no maghavanvājasātāvame viśvāni duritā tarema tā tarema tavāvasā tarema ||15|| abhi | prayāṃsi | sudhitāni | hi | ravyaḥ | ni | tvā | dadhīta | rodasī iti | yajadhyai | arva | naḥ | maghavan | vājasātau | agne | viśvāni | duḥitā | tarema | tā | tarema | tava | avasā | tarema || 15 ||
he agne sudhitāni sunihitāni vedhāmāsāditāni prayāṃsi annāni havirlakṣaṇāni abhi ravyaḥ abhipaśyasi |
hi pūrakaḥ |
tādṛśaṃ tvāṃ rodasī dyāvāpṛthivyāvetadupalakṣitān sarvān devān yajadhyai yaṣṭuṃ ni dadhīta |
ayaṃ yajamāno nihitavān |
he maghavan agne naḥ asmān vājasātau saṃgrāme annasya saṃbhajane vā nimittabhūte ava rakṣa |
vayaṃ ca viśvāni sarvāṇi duritā duritāni duḥkhāni tarema atikrāmema |
anyadratam |
15 |
agnipraṇayane 'agne viśvebhiḥ' ityeṣānuvaktavyā |
sūtritaṃ ca—'agne viśvebhiḥ svanīka devairitya- dharca āramet' (āśva. au. 2. 17) iti |
agne viśvebhiḥ svanīka devairūrṇāvantaṃ prathamaḥ sīda yonim | kulāyinaṃ ghṛtavantaṃ savitre yajñaṃ naya yajamānāya sādhu || 16 || agne | viśvebhiḥ | su'anīka | devaiḥ | ūrṇā'vantam | prathamaḥ | sīda | yonim | kulāyinam | ghṛta'vantam | savitre | yajñam | naya | yajamānāya | sādhu || 16 ||
he svanīka sujavāla agne viśvebhiḥ viśvaiḥ sarvaiḥ devaiḥ saha ūrṇāvantam ūrṇāstukāvantaṃ yonim uttaravedilakṣaṇaṃ sthānaṃ prathamaḥ sarveṣu deveṣu mukhyastvaṃ sīda upaviśa |
kīdṛśaṃ yonim |
kulāyinam |
kulāyo nīḍam |
tattadahaṃ guggulvādiṃsabhāreṇopetam |
tathā ca śrūyate—'kulāyamiba hyetadyajñe kriyate yattaitadudāravāḥ paridhayo guggulvāstukāḥ sugandhitojanāni' (ai. brā. 1. 24) iti |
ghṛtavantaṃ vyāghāraṇādyayuktam |
evamuttaravedyāṃ niṣpannastvaṃ savitre haviṣāṃ prerayitre yaja- mānāya |
paṣṭhyarthe caturthyapā |
īdṛśasya yajamānasya yajñaṃ sādhu ārjavena naya devān prāpaya |
imamu tyamatharvavadagniṃ manthanti vedhasaḥ | yamaṅkayantamānayanmūraṃ śyāvyābhyaḥ || 17 || imam | ūṃ iti | tyam | atharva'vat | agnim | manthanti | vedhasaḥ | yam | aṅku'yantam | ā | anayan | amūram | śyāvyābhyaḥ || 17 ||
1. kha-jha-na-bha-ya-śa-sudhitāni |
2. ta-ya-havirlakṣaṇānyannāni |
3. kha-bha-tvā tvāṃ tādṛśam; jha-tvā tādṛśam; na-ra-tvā taḥdṛśaṃ tvām; śa-tvādeśaṃ tvām |
4. kha-jha-na-bha-śa-ityeṣānuvākyā |
ṛ. 3-6
vedhasa: karmaṇāṃ vidhātāra ṛtvija: |
imaṃ dṛśyamānaṃ tyaṃ taṃ pūrvoktaguṇam |
abhimam atharvavat yathātharvasya ṛṣi: purāmbhāt tathā manthanti maśnanti |
aravyo: sakāśājjanayanti |
akuyantam |
kutsitamajñānaṃ gamanam aṅku |
tadātmana icchantaṃ devebhyo nirgatyetastata: palāyamānam amūram amūḍhaṃ yam agniṃ śyāvyābhya: |
śyāvīti rātrinām |
tatra bhavāstamasa: saṃhataya: śyāvyā: |
tābhya: sakāśāt ānayan āgamanayan taṃ manthantīyanvaya: ||
janiṣvā devavītaye sarvatātā svastaye |
ā devānvakṣyamṛtā~ ṛtāvṛdho yajñaṃ deveṣu pispṛśa: ||
18 ||
janiṣvā | deva'vītaye | sarva'tātā | svastaye | ā | devān | vakṣa | amṛtān | ṛta'vṛdha: | yajñam | deveṣu | pispṛśa: || 18 ||
he agne sarvatātā sarvastāyamāne yajñe madhyamānastvaṃ janiṣva prādurbhava |
kimartham |
devavītaye devakāmāya yajamānāya |
paṣṭhyarthe caturthyapā |
īdṛśasya yajamānasya svastaye avināśārtham |
janitvā ca amṛtān maraṇarahitān ṛtāvṛdha: yajñasya vardhayitṛn devān ā vakṣa āvaha |
tadanantaraṃ teṣu deveṣu asmadīyaṃ yajñe pispṛśa: sparśaya |
prāpayetyartha: ||
vayamu tvā gṛhapate janānāmagne arkama samidhā bṛhantam |
asthūri no gārhapatyāni santu tigmena nastejasā saṃ śiśādhi ||
19 ||
vayam | ū~ iti | tvā | gṛha'pate | janānām | agne | arkam | sam'idhā | bṛhantam | asthūri | na: | gārhapatyāni | santu | tigmena | na: | tejasā | sam | śiśādhi || 19 ||
he gṛhapate yajñānāṃ pālaka agne janānāṃ prāṇināṃ madhye vayamu vayameva tvā tvāṃ samidhā samindhanena bṛhantaṃ mahāntam arkama kṛtavantaḥ sma: |
ata: kāraṇāt na: asmākaṃ gārhapatyāni gṛhapatitvāni asthūri asthūrīṇi |
ekāśrayukka: śakaṭa: sthūrirityucyate tadviparītau bahubhirathairupetaḥ śakaṭo'sthūri: |
tena ca saṃpūrṇatā lakṣyate |
asthūrīṇi putrapaśudhanādibhi: saṃpūrṇāni bhavantu |
api ca na: asmān tigmena tīkṣṇena tejasā saṃ śiśādhi samyaṅniśaya samyak tīkṣṇīkuru |
saṃyojayetyartha: ||
20 ||
1 ||
dvitīye'nuvāke'ṣṭa sūktāni |
tatra 'svamagne yajñānām' ityaṣṭācatvāriṃśadṛcaṃ prathamaṃ sūktaṃ bharadvāja- syārṣamāgneyam |
ādyā ṣaṣṭhī ca vardhamānā gāyatrī 'ṣaṭkasaptakā: sā vardhamānā' ityuktalakṣaṇope- tattvāt |
saptaviṃśayanuṣṭup |
'ā te agne, agnidevā: ' ityete ṛcāvanuṣṭubhau |
tayo: pūrvā 'vītī yo devam' iti triṣṭup |
śiṣṭā dvācatvāriṃśadṛco gāyatrya: |
tathā cānukrampate 'tvamagne'ṣṭācatvāriṃśadyaṃ vardha-
1. kha-jña-jha-ta-na-bha-ra-aṅku: |
2. manthantītyanvaya: |
3. kha-na-bha-tathānukampate; śa-tathā cānukāntam |
mānādhā paṣṭhī ca sasarvisyanuṣṭup triṣṭuppūrve cānye' iti |
prātaranuvākādinā śasyo'greṇe kṛtī gāyatre chandasīvaṃ sūktamucyate |
tatrānyāstisra ṛca uddhṛtavyāḥ |
sūyate hi—'tvamagne yajñānāmiti tisra uttamāṃ uddharet' (āśva. śrau. 4. 13) iti |
viśvajityābhimārutaṃ bṛhatsāma yadyabhiṣṭhomasāma syātta- dānīmādhāḥ pañcaḥ stotriyānurūpāthāḥ |
sūtritaṃ hi—'tvamagne yajñānāmiti stotriyānurūpā' (āśva. śrau. 8. 7) iti ||
tvamagne yajñānāṃ hotā viśveṣāṃ hitaḥ | devebhirmānuṣe janai || 1 || tvam | agne | yajñānām | hotā | viśveṣām | hitaḥ | devebhiḥ | mānuṣe | janai || 1 ||
he agne tvaṃ viśveṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ sasaṃsthārūpeṇa bhikṣāṇāṃ yajñānāṃ hotā homaniṣpādako'si |
yadvā |
yajñānāṃ sambandhī devānāmāhvātā bhavasi |
kuta ityata āha |

he ᵥghṛṣṇo śatrūṇāṃ gharṣaka ᵥvajrahasta vajrapāṇe he indra ᵥyaḥ tvaṃ ᵥśavobhiḥ balaiḥ sahitaḥ san ᵥviśvā viśvaṃ ᵥvṛtram āvarakam ᵥamitriyā amitraṃ śatrum ||
amitraśabdāt dvitīyaikavacanasyeyādeśaḥ ||
ᵥvi ᵥvadhiṣaḥ vyavadhīḥ sa tvaṃ somaṃ pibeti saṃbandhaḥ ||
sa īṃ pāhi ya ṛjīṣī tarutro yaḥ śipravānvṛṣabho yo matīnām |
yo gotrabhidvajrabhṛdyo hariṣṭhāḥ sa indra citrā~ abhi tṛndhi vājān ||
2 ||
saḥ | īm | pāhi | yaḥ | ṛjīṣī | tarutraḥ | yaḥ | śipra'vān | vṛṣabhaḥ | yaḥ | matīnām | yaḥ | gotra'bhit | vajra'bhṛt | yaḥ | hari'sthāḥ | saḥ | indra | citrān | abhi | tṛndhi | vājān || 2 ||
he indra ᵥṛjīṣī |
ṛjīpaśabdena gatarasaḥ somo'bhidhīyate |
tadvān² ᵥyaḥ tvaṃ ᵥtarutraḥ śatrūṇāṃ tārako'si ᵥyaḥ tvaṃ ᵥśipravān śobhanahanurasi ᵥyaḥ tvaṃ ᵥmatīnāṃ stotrāṇām ||
manyateḥ stutikarmaṇaḥ kartari ktic ||
ᵥvṛṣabhaḥ kāmānāṃ varṣitāsi ᵥsaḥ tvam ᵥīm enaṃ somaṃ ᵥpāhi piba |
he indra yastvaṃ³ ᵥvajrabhṛt vajradharaḥ ᵥyaḥ tvaṃ ᵥgotrabhit gotrāṇāṃ parvatānāṃ meghānāṃ vā bhettāsi ᵥyaḥ tvaṃ ᵥhariṣṭhāḥ haryoḥ sthātāsi he ᵥindra ᵥsaḥ tvaṃ ᵥcitrān vicitrān ᵥvājān ajñāni ᵥabhi ᵥtṛndhi asmabhyaṃ prakāśaya ||
evā pāhi pratnathā mandatu tvā śrudhi brahma vāvṛdhasvota gīrbhiḥ |
āviḥ sūryaṃ kṛṇuhi pīpihīṣo jahi śatrū~rabhi gā indra tṛndhi ||
3 ||
eva | pāhi | pratna'thā | mandatu | tvā | śrudhi | brahma | vāvṛdhasva | uta | gīḥ'bhiḥ | āviḥ | sūryam | kṛṇuhi | pīpihi'īṣo | jahi | śatrūn | abhi | gāḥ | indra | tṛndhi || 3 ||
he indra tvaṃ ᵥpratnathā pratnān purāṇān ᵥsomān yathāpibaḥ tathā ᵥeva evam imamasmadīyaṃ somaṃ ᵥpāhi piba |
sa ca somastvayā pītaḥ san ᵥtvā tvāṃ ᵥmandatu madayatu⁴ |
ᵥbrahma asmābhiḥ kṛtaṃ stotraṃ ᵥśrudhi śṛṇu |
ᵥuta api ca ᵥgobhiḥ stutirūpābhirgīrbhiḥ ᵥvavṛdhasva tvaṃ vardhasva |
api ca ᵥsūrya sarvasya prerakaṃ devam ᵥāviḥ ᵥkṛṇuhi āviṣkuru |
vayaṃ sūrye yathā paśyema tathā kurvityarthaḥ |
ᵥiṣaḥ ajñāni ca ᵥpīpihi asmabhyaṃ pyāyaya⁵ |
ᵥśatrūn asmatpamānān ᵥjahi nāśāya |
he ᵥindra tvaṃ paṇibhirapahṛtāḥ ᵥgāḥ ᵥabhi ᵥtṛndhi prakāśaya ||
1. jña-prākāśayaḥ prakāśitavānasi; ta-bha-ya-prakāśaya |
2. gha-śa-tadvān gatasāramapi somama- parityajan |
3. kha-jha-ta-na-bha-ra-' yastvaṃ ' nāsti |
4. jña-mādayatu kiṃca; gha-śa-madayayuktaṃ karotu |
5. kha-bha-pāyaya; śa-āpyāyaya |
te tvā madā bṛhadindra svadhāva ime pītā ukṣayanta ghumantam | mahāmanūnaṃ tavasaṃ virbhūti matsarāso jahṛṣanta prasāham || 4 || te | tvā | madāḥ | bṛhat | indra | svadhā'vaḥ | ime | pītāḥ | ukṣayanta | ghumantam | mahām | anūnam | tavasam | vi'bhūtim | matsarāsaḥ | jahṛṣanta | pra'saham || 4 || he svadhāvaḥ annavan indra madāḥ madakarāḥ te ime pītāḥ somāḥ ghumantaṃ dīptimantaṃ tvā tvāṃ bṛhat atyantam ukṣayanta secayantu | 1 api ca he indra tvāṃ matsarāsaḥ madakarāḥ somāḥ jahṛṣanta bhṛśaṃ harṣayantu | kīdṛśam | mahāṃ mahāntaṃ prabhūtam anūnaṃ sarvaguṇairnyūnatārahitam | saṃpūrṇaguṇa- mityarthaḥ | 2 tavasaṃ pravṛddhaṃ virbhūtiṃ vibhavantaṃ pramāhaṃ śatrūṇāmabhibhavitāram ||
yebhiḥ sūryamuṣasaṃ mandasāno'vāsayo'pa dulahāni dardrata | mahāmadriṃ pari gā indra santaṃ nutthā acyutaṃ sadasaḥ pari khāt || 5 || yebhiḥ | sūryam | upasam | mandamānaḥ | avāmayaḥ | apa | dulahāni | dardrata | mahām | adrim | pari | gāḥ | indra | santam | nutthāḥ | acyutam | sadasaḥ | pari | khāt ||5|| he indra yebhiḥ yaiḥ somaḥ mandasānaḥ modamānastvaṃ sūrya sarvasya prerakaṃ devam upasam uṣo- devatāṃ ca avāmayaḥ svasthāne nyavāsayaḥ | athavā sūryapaso yathā tamāṃsi vivāsayatastathākārṣīḥ | kiṃ kurvan | dulahāni dṛḍhāni tamāṃsi apa dardrata apadārayan | kiṃca he indra tvaṃ paṇibhiḥ apahatāḥ gāḥ pari mantaṃ parito vidyamānaṃ mahā mahāntam adriṃ parvataṃ nutthāḥ anudaḥ | kīdṛśam | khāt svakīyāt sadasaḥ sthānāt pari pagnaḥ acyutaṃ avicalitaṃ vināśarahitam | sthira- mityarthaḥ | 3
tava kratvā tava taṃhasanābhirāmāsu pakaṃ śacyā ni dīghaḥ | aurṇordura usriyābhyo vi dulahodurvādvā asrajo aṅgirasvān || 6 || tava | kratvā | tava | taṃhasanābhiḥ | āmāsu | pakam | śacyā | ni | dīghariti dīdhaḥ | aurṇoḥ | duraḥ | usriyābhyaḥ | vi | dulahaḥ | uta | ūrvāta | gāḥ | amṛjaḥ | aṅgirasvān || 6 || he indra tava kratvā tvadītayā prajñayā tava taṃhasanābhiḥ tvadīyeḥ karmabhiḥ śacyā sāmarthyena āmāsu apakkāsu goṣu pakaṃ pariṇataṃ tat prasiddhaṃ payaḥ ni dīghaḥ nitarāṃ dhārayasi | api ca he indra tvam usriyābhyaḥ gobhyaḥ | 4 gā nirgamayitumiyarthaḥ | 5 dulaha dṛḍhāni | 6 valena pāṣāṇādibhirbhedī- 7
1. kha-jña-ta-na-bha-ya-ra-siṣyantu ; jha-siṣyantu ; śa-rocayanta | 2. kha-jña-ta-na-bha-ya-ra-' saṃpūrṇa- mityarthaḥ | 3. kha-jña-ta-na-bha-ya-ra-' ā~cālita ' nāsti | 4. jña-ta-sthitamityarthaḥ | 5. gha-śa- prakṛtyā | 6. śa-gobhyaḥ | tādarthye caturthī | aṅgirasām | 7. śa-niyamitāmityarthaḥ | kra. 3-5
kṛtānītyarthaḥ | 'duraḥ dvārāṇi 'vi 'bhaunauḥ udghāṭitavānasi | 'aṅgirasvān aṅgirobhiryutasvam 'urvāt samūhāt | goṣṭhādityarthaḥ | 'gāḥ 'ut 'asṛjaḥ udgamayaḥ ||
praprātha kṣāṃ mahi daṃso nyu1vīmupa dyāmpṛṣo bṛhadindra stabhāyaḥ |
adhārayo rodasī devaputre pratne mātarau yahī ṛtasya |
7 |
praprātha | kṣām | mahi | daṃsaḥ | | vi | urvīm | upa | dyām | ṛṣaḥ | | bṛhat | indra | stabhāyaḥ | | adhārayaḥ | rodasī iti | devaputre iti deva'putre | pratne iti | mātarau | yahī iti | ṛtasya || 7 ||
he indra tvaṃ 'mahi mahatā 'daṃsaḥ daṃsamā karmaṇā 'urvīṃ vistīrṇāṃ 'kṣāṃ bhūmiṃ 'vi 'praprātha viśeṣeṇa pūritavānasi |
'prā pūraṇe ' iti dhātuḥ |
he 'indra 'ṛṣvaḥ mahāstvaṃ 'bṛhata bṛhatīṃ 'yāṃ divam 'upa 'stabhāyaḥ upastambhāsi |
nirālambaṃ vitiṣṭhantīṃ dyauryathā na patatīndrastathā karotītyarthaḥ |
api ca tvaṃ 'rodasī dyāvāpṛthivyau 'adhārayaḥ poṣarṇairdhārayasi |
kīdṛśyau |
'devaputre |
devāḥ putrā yayoste |
'pratne purāṇe 'mātarā mātarau nirmātryau 'ṛtasya udakasya yajñasya vā 'yahī yajñau mahyau |
yadvā |
viśvasya mātarau ṛtasya brahmaṇo yahī puṇyo |
yaduritipatyanāmaitat |
ardha tvā viśve pura indra devā ekaṃ tavasaṃ dadhire bharāya |
adaivo yadabhyauhiṣṭa devānsvarṣātā vṛṇata indramatra |
8 |
ardha | tvā | viśve | puraḥ | indra | devāḥ | ekam | tavasam | dadhire | bharāya | adaivaḥ | yat | abhi | auhiṣṭa | devān | svaḥ'sātā | vṛṇate | indram | atra || 8 ||
'adha adhunā asmin kāle 'viśve devāḥ1 sarve vahvādayo he 'indra 'ekaṃ mukhyaṃ 'tavasaṃ pravṛddhaṃ balavantaṃ 'tvā tvāṃ 'bharāya saṃgrāmāya 'puraḥ 'dadhire purastāt cakrire |
'yat yadā 'adaivaḥ vṛtrā'suraḥ 'devān 'abhyauhiṣṭa saṃgrāmārthamabhigatavān |
āṃhatirgatikarmā |
kiṃcāsmin kāle 'svarṣātā svarṣātau saṃgrāme 'indraṃ 'vṛṇate saṃbhajante sma |
maruta iti śeṣaḥ2 |
maruto hīndraṃ deveṣu palāyiteṣu samabhaja- nnityarthaḥ |
adhunā puraṃ |
dadhire iti sambandhaḥ |

9 |

2. kha-jha-jha-ta-na-bha-ya-ra-devā maruta iti śeṣaḥ |
indraṃ gṛte saṃbhajante sma |
^sā prasiddhā devānāmāśrayatvena ^dyauścit dyuloko'pi he indra ^adha adhunāsmin kāle ^dvitā^ dvidhā dvipakārān ^te tvadīyāt ^vajrāt ^svasya ātmīyān ^manyo: krodhācca ^bhiyasā bhayena^ ^apa ^amanat apananāma |
^nu ^iti^ pūraṇa: |
^viśvayu: bahujña: ^indra: ^yat yadā ^abhyosānam abhigacchantam ^ahiṃ vṛtraṃ ^śayathe śayananimitte |
maraṇāyelyartha: |
^ni ^jaghāna nyavadhīt |
^cit iti pūraṇa: |
yadā vṛtraṃ jaghānāsmin^ kāle dyaurnāmeti saṃbandha: |
adha tvaṣṭā te maha ugraṃ vajraṃ sahasrabhṛṣṭiṃ vavṛtacchatāśrim | nikomamaramaṇasaṃ yena narvantamihaṃ saṃ piṇagṛjīṣin || 10 || adha | tvaṣṭā | te | maha: | ugra | vajram | sahasrabhṛṣṭim | vavṛtat | śata'aśrim | ni'komam | ara'menasam | yena | narvantam | ahiṃm | mam | piṇak | ṛjīṣin || 10 ||
^adha adhunā he ^ugra udghaṇabalendra ^maha: mahata: ^te tava ^tvaṣṭā devaśilpī ^vajraṃ ^vavṛtat nyavartayat niṣpannaṃ kṛtavān |
kīdṛśam |
^sahasrabhṛṣṭiṃ sahasradhāraṃ ^śatāśri śataparvāṇam |
he ^ṛjīṣin gatarasasomavannandra ^yena vajreṇa ^nikāmam |
niyataḥ kāmo yasya tam |
^aramaṇasam |
śatrūṇā- maramabhigantu mano yasya tam |
^narvantaṃ śabdāyamānam ^ahiṃ vṛtramasuraṃ ^saṃ ^piṇak saṃpiṣṭavānasi taṃ vajraṃ kṛtavānityartha: |
2 |
vardhanyaṃ viśve marutaḥ majoṣā: parcacchataṃ mahipāṃ indra tubhyam | pūṣā viṣṇustrīṇi sarauṃsi dhāvanvatrahaṇaṃ madirmaśurmasmai || 11 || vardhān | yam | viśve | marutaḥ | ma'joṣā: | parcata | śatam | mahimān | indra | tubhyam | pūṣā | viṣṇu: | trīṇi | sarāṃsi | dhāvan | vṛtrahanam | madim | aṃśam | asmai || 11 ||
^sajoṣā: saha prīyamāṇā: ^viśve ^marutaḥ sarve devā he ^indra ^yaṃ tvāṃ ^vardhān stotrairvardhayanti he indra tasmai ^tubhyaṃ ^pūṣā enannāmako deva: ^viṣṇu: etannāmakaśca ^śataṃ śatasaṃkhyākān ^mahimān guṃpaṣūn ^pacata pacet |
api ca ^asmai tubhyaṃ ^sarāṃsi droṇakalaśapatabhradādyavanīyālyāni ^trīṇi pātrāṇi ^aṃśu somaṃ ^dhāvan agacchan |

kīdṛśaṃ somam |
