ā kṣodo mahi vṛtaṃ nadīnāṃ pariṣṭhitamasaja ūrmimapām | tāsāmanu pravarta indra panthāṃ prārdayo nīcīrapasa: samudram || 12 || ā | kṣode: | mahi | vṛtam | nadīnām | pari'sthitam | amṛja: | ūrmim | apām | tāsām | anu | pra'varta: | indra | panthām | pra | ārdya: | nīcī: | apama: | samudram || 12 ||
1. kha-gha-ma-śa-‘dvitā...dvipakārān’ nāsti | 2. kha-gha-ma-śa-bhayena dvitā dvipakāreṇa kāṣṭhena (bha-kālena ) manasā ca | 3. nū ciditi | 4. śa-jaghāna tasmin | 5. jha-ta-na-bha-aramanasam | 6. kha-ma-āpīte |
he indra tvaṃ mahi mahat vṛtaṃ vṛtreṇa parivṛtamācchāditaṃ pariṣṭhitaṃ paritaḥ sthitaṃ nadīnāṃ sindhūnāṃ saṃbandhi kṣodaḥ udakam ā asṛjaḥ ā samantāt sṛṣṭavānasi|

punarvacanamādarārtham|

13 ||
eva| tā| viśvā| cakruvāṃsam| indram| mahām| ugram| ajūryam| sahaḥ'dām| suvīram| tvā| su'āyudham| su'vajram| ā| brahma| navyam| avase| vavṛtyāt ||13||
he indra tvā tvāṃ navyaṃ nūtanamabhyairakṛtapadaṃ brahma asmābhiḥ kṛtaṃ stotram avase asmākaṃ rakṣaṇāya ā vavṛtyāt āvartayatu|
kīdṛśaṃ tvām|
eva evamuktaprakāreṇa tā tāni prasiddhāni viśvā viśvāni karmāṇi cakruvāṃsaṃ kṛtavantam indram īśvaraṃ mahām mahāntam ugram ojasvinm ajūryam ajarāṃ sahodāṃ sahaso balasya dānāraṃ suvīraṃ śobhanā vīrā mastāṃ yasya taṃ svāyudhaṃ śobhanā-yudhaṃ suvajraṃ śobhanavajraṃ.etam ||
sa no vājāya śravasa iṣe ca rāye dhehi ghumataṃ indra viprān|
bharadvāje nṛta indra sūrīndvi ca smaidhi pāryaṃ na indra ||
14 ||
saḥ| naḥ| vājāya| śravase| iṣe| ca| rāye| dhehi| ghu'mataḥ| indra| viprān| bharata'vāje| nṛtaḥ| indra| sūrīn| dvi| ca| sma| edhi| pāryam| naḥ| indra || 14 ||
he indra saḥ tvaṃ ghumataḥ dīptimataḥ viprān medhāvinaḥ naḥ asmān vājāya balāya śravase yaśase iṣe ca annāya ca rāye dhanāya dhehi dhāraya|
api ca he indra bharadvāje mayi nṛtaḥ manuṣyavataḥ|
paricārakayuktāvityarthaḥ|

asmākaṃ rakṣitā khalu bhavetyarthaḥ ||
paṣṭhe'hani tṛtīyasavane brāhmaṇācchaṃsikhaṇḍe'nurūpatasya 'ayā vājam' iti tṛtīyā|

2. jña-ta-dhātujatvāt |

kīdṛśam |
ᵥbṛhantaṃ mahāntam ᵥṛṣvaṃ gantāram ᵥajaraṃ jarārahitaṃ ᵥyuvānaṃ nityataruṇam ᵥarvāḻahena śatrubhiranabhibhūtena ᵥśavasā balena ᵥśṛṇuvāṃsaṃ pravṛddham |
śrayateridaṃ rūpam |
ᵥyaḥ indraḥ ᵥsadyaścit sadya eva jānamātraḥ san ᵥarvāmi alpamadhikaṃ yathā bhavati tathā ᵥvavṛdhe vardhate ||
pṛ॒thuṃ ka॒rtvā ba॒hulā̭ ga॒bhastī॒ asma̭drya॒ksaṃ mi̭mīhi śra॒vāṃsi̭ |
yū॒theva̭ pa॒śvaḥ pa̭śu॒pā da̭ma॒nā a॒smā~ i̭ndrā॒bhyā va̭vṛ॒tsvājaṷ ||
3 ||
pṛ॒thum | ka॒rtvā | ba॒hulā̭ | ga॒bhastī̭ | a॒sma̭drya॒k | sam | mi̭mīhi | śra॒vāṃsi̭ | yathā̭'iva | pa॒śvaḥ | pa॒śu॒pāḥ | da॒ma॒nāḥ | a॒smān | i॒ndra | a॒bhyā | va॒vṛ॒tsva | ā॒jaṷ || 3 ||
he indra tvaṃ ᵥpṛthū vistīrṇau ᵥkartvā karmaṇāṃ kartarī ᵥbahulā bahupradau ᵥgabhastī tvadīyo bāhū ᵥśravāṃsi annāni |
dātumiti śeṣaḥ |
ᵥasmadryak asmadabhimukhaṃ ᵥsaṃ ᵥmimīhi kuru |
kiṃca he ᵥindra ᵥdamūnāḥ dāntamanāstvam ᵥājau saṃgrāme ᵥasmān ᵥabhi ᵥā ᵥvavṛtsva abhyāvartasva |
tatra dṛṣṭāntaḥ |
ᵥpaśupāḥ paśūnāṃ pālakaḥ ᵥpaśvaḥ paśūnāṃ ᵥyūtheva yūthāni yathā āvartayati ᵥtadvat ||
taṃ va̭ indra॒ṃ cati̭namasya śā॒kairi॒ha nūna̭ṃ vā॒jaya̭nto huvema |
yathā̭ ci॒tpūrvḙ ja॒ritāra॒ āsṷrnai॒dhā a̭na॒vadyā̭ a॒riṣṭā̭ḥ ||
4 ||
tam | vaḥ | i॒ndram | ca॒ti॒nam | a॒sya | śā॒kaiḥ | i॒ha | nū॒nam | vā॒ja'ya̭ntaḥ | hu॒vema̭ | yathā̭ | ci॒t | pūrvḙ | ja॒ritāra̭ḥ | ā॒suḥ | a॒ne॒dhāḥ | a॒na॒vadyā̭ḥ | a॒riṣṭā̭ḥ || 4 ||
1. gha-śa-aṃhisitaḥ yaḥ |
2. kha-bha-avadhayat; ja-avardhata; a-ta-na-ya-ra-avadhayata; śa- vardhate |
3. kha-gha-bha-karatvā kartvā; śa-karatvā |
4. jha-ta-ya-ra-āvakatsva; na-āvṛtasvati |
vnūnam adya viha asmin yajñe vvājayantaḥ amicchanto vayaṃ stotāraḥ vśākaiḥ śaktaiḥ samarthaiḥ vasya tava sahāyairmarudbhiḥ sahitaṃ vcatinaṃ śatrūṇāṃ cātakam |
nāśakamityarthaḥ |
vindraṃ parameśvaraṃ vtaṃ vvaḥ prasiddhaṃ tvāṃ vhuvema stumaḥ |
vyathā vcitta yathaiva vpūrve purātanāḥ vjaritāraḥ stotāraḥ vanedhāḥ aninyāḥ vanavadyāḥ pāparahitāḥ variṇāḥ ahiṃsitāśca vāsuḥ babhūvuḥ he indra tvatprasādāt vayamapi tathaiva bhavemetyarthaḥ ||
dhṛtavrato dhanudāḥ somavṛddhaḥ sa hi vāmasya vasuṃnaḥ purukṣuḥ |
saṃ jagmire pṛthyā3 rāyau asminsamudre na sindhavo yādamānāḥ ||
5 ||
dhṛta'vrataḥ | dhanu'dāḥ | soma'vṛddhaḥ | maḥ | hi | vāmasya | vasuṃnaḥ | purukṣuḥ | sam | jagmire | pṛthyāḥ | rāyaḥ | asmin | samudre | na | sindhavaḥ | yādamānāḥ || 5
^pṛtanāpāṭ śatrusenānāmabhibhavitā ^amṛdhaḥ ahiṃsitaḥ ^yaḥ ^madaḥ harṣaḥ he ^indra ^te tvayā deyaḥ ^śṛṇuvāṃsaṃ praṣṭuṃ ^taṃ madaṃ ^naḥ asmabhyam ^ā ^bhara āhara |
^tvotāḥ tvayā rakṣitāḥ ^jigīvāṃsaḥ jitavanto vayaṃ ^torakasya putrasya ^tanayasya tatputrasya ca ^sātāṃ lābhe nimittabhūte sati ^yena harṣeṇa ^maṃsīmahi tvāṃ stuvīmahi tamā bharati pūrveṇānvayaḥ ||
ā nau bhara vṛṣaṇaṃ śuṣmamindra dhanuspṛtaṃ śṛṇuvāṃsaṃ mudakṣam |
yen vaṃsām pṛtanāsu śatranvotibhirūta jāmīrajāmīn ||
8 ||
ā | naḥ | bhara | vṛṣaṇam | śuṣmam | indra | dhana'spṛtam | śṛṇuvāṃsam | sudakṣam | yena | vaṃsām | pṛtanāsu | śatrūn | tave | ūti'bhiḥ | uta | jāmīna | ajāmīna || 8 ||
he ^indra tvaṃ ^vṛṣaṇaṃ varṣitāraṃ ^śuṣmaṃ senālakṣaṇaṃ balaṃ ^naḥ asmabhyam ^ā ^bhara āhara |
kīdṛśam ? ^dhanaspṛtaṃ dhanasya pālakaṃ ^śṛṇuvāṃsaṃ pravṛddhaṃ ^sudakṣaṃ śobhanabalam |
he indra ^tavotibhiḥ svadīyābhī rakṣābhiḥ ^pṛtanāsu saṃgrāmeṣu |
pṛtanā iti saṃgrāmanāma |
^yena balena ^śatrūn ^vaṃsām hanāma' |
utatyatra bhinnakramaḥ |
^jāmīna bandhūn ^uta api ca ^ajāmīna śatrūṃśca yena śubhena vaṃsāma taṃ śuṣmamā bharati pūrveṇa saṃbandhaḥ ||
ā te śuṣmo vṛṣabha etu paścādottarādadharādā purastāt |
ā viśvato abhi sametvarvāḍindra dyumnaṃ sarvadevyasme ||
9 ||
ā | te | śuṣmaḥ | vṛṣabhaḥ | etu | paścāt | ā | uttarāt | adharāt | ā | purastāt | ā | viśvataḥ | abhi | mam | etu | arvāṅ | indra | dyumnam | sarva'trata | dhehi | asme iti ||9||
he indra ^vṛṣabhaḥ varṣitā ^te tvadīyaṃ ^śuṣmaḥ balam ^arvāṅ asmadabhimukhaḥ san ^paścāt paścimabhāgān ^ā ^etu āgacchatu |
^uttarāt uttarabhāgāt ^ā gacchatu |
^adharāt dakṣiṇabhāgā- ccaitu |
^purastāt pūrvabhāgācca ^ā gacchatu |
^viśvataḥ sarvasmādbhāgāt ^abhi asmānabhilakṣya ^ā ^sametu samyagāgacchatu |
he ^indra tvaṃ ^sarvatra suyuktaṃ ^dyumnaṃ dhanam ^asme asmabhyaṃ ^dhehi prayaccha ||
nuvat indra nṛtamābhirūtī vaṃsīmahi vāṃ śromatebhiḥ |
īkṣe hi vasva ubhayasya rājandhā rtanaṃ mahi sthuraṃ bṛhantam ||
10 ||
nu'vat | te | indra | nṛtama'bhiḥ | ūtī | vaṃsīmahi | dhāmam | śromatebhiḥ | īkṣe | hi | vasvaḥ | ubhayasya | rājan | dhāḥ | ratnam | mahi | sthuram | bṛhantam || 10 ||
^nuvat manuṣyaiḥ paricārakajanairyuktaṃ ^śromatebhiḥ śrotavyairyaśobhiḥ sahitaṃ ^vāmaṃ vananīyaṃ dhanaṃ he ^indra ^te tvattaḥ ^nṛtamābhiḥ netṛtamābhiḥ ^ūtī ūtībhiḥ rakṣābhiḥ ^vaṃsīmahi vayaṃ saṃbhajemahi |
^hi yasmāt kāraṇāt he ^rājan rājamānendra tvam ^ubhayasya pārthivasya divyasya ca ^vasvaḥ dhanasya
1. kha-a-ta-na-bha-ra-' hanāma ' nāsti |
^īkṣe īśiṣe tasmāt kāraṇāt ^mahi mahāntaṃ ^sthūraṃ vipulaṃ ^bṛhantaṃ guṇaiḥ pariṣṭubaṃ ^ratnaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ dhanam |
atra ratnaśabdaḥ pulliṃgaḥ |
^dhāḥ dhehi |
marutvantaṃ vṛṣabhaṃ vāvṛdhānamarvāri divyaṃ śāsimendram |
viśvāsāhamavase nūtanāyograṃ sahodāmiha taṃ huvema ||
11 ||
marutvantam | vṛṣabham | vavṛdhānam | arkavat'arim | divyam | śāsam | indram | viśva'saham | avase | nūtanāya | ugram | mahaḥ'dām | iha | tam | huvema || 11 ||

āhvayāmo vā |
kīdṛśam |
^mahattvantaṃ marudbhiyuktaṃ ^vṛṣabhaṃ kāmānāṃ varṣitāraṃ ^vavṛdhānaṃ pravṛddham ^akavārim akutsitaśatruṃ ^divyaṃ dyotamānaṃ ^śāsaṃ śāsitāraṃ ^viśvasahaṃ viśvasya lokasya abhabhavi- tāram ^ugram ojasvinaṃ ^sahodāṃ valapradam |
janai vajrinmahi cinmanyamānebhyo nṛbhyo randhayā yeṣvasmi |
adhā hi tvā pṛthivyāṃ śūrasātau havāmahe tanaye goṣvapsu ||
12 ||
janam | vajrin | mahi | cit | manyamānam | ebhyaḥ | nṛ'bhyaḥ | randhaya | yeṣu | asmi | adha | hi | tvā | pṛthivyām | śūra'sātau | havāmahe | tanaye | goṣu | apsu || 12 ||
^yeṣu yeṣāṃ nṛṇāṃ madhye'ham ^asmi ekāṃ bhavāmi he ^vajrin vajravan indra tvam ^ebhyo ^nṛbhyaḥ manuṣyebhyaḥ ^mahi mahadatyantamānaṃ ^manyamānaṃ ^janam |
bahumanyamānamityarthaḥ |
^randhaya vaśīkuru |
^cita iti pādapūraṇaḥ |
^adha adhunā ^pṛthivyāṃ bhūmyāṃ ^śūrasātau yuddhe pravṛtte sati ^tanaye putre nimitte ca ^goṣu paśuṣu ^apsu udakeṣu ca nimitteṣu he indra ^tvā dhuloke sthitaṃ tvāṃ ^havāmahaṃ vayamāhvayāmaḥ |
vayaṃ te ebhiḥ puruhūta sakhyaiḥ śatroḥśatroruttara itsyāma |
ghnanto vṛtrāṇyubhayāni śūra rāyā madena bṛhatā tvotāḥ ||
13 ||
vayam | te | ebhiḥ | puru'huta | sakhyaiḥ | śatroḥ'śatroḥ | uttaraḥ | it | syāma | ghnantaḥ | vṛtrāṇi | ubhayāni | śūra | rāyā | madena | bṛhatā | tvā'ūtāḥ || 13 ||
he ^puruhūta bahubhirāhatendra ^ebhiḥ prasiddhaiḥ ^sakhyaiḥ stotralakṣaṇaiḥ sakhyakarmabhiḥ ^te tvayā saha ^vayam ^ubhayāni jāmi rūpaṇyajāmirūpāṇi ^vṛtrāṇi amitrāṇi ^ghnantaḥ hiṃsantaḥ ^śatroḥśatroḥ sarvasmācchatroḥ ^uttare ^it syāma adhikā eva bhavema |
he ^śūra vikrāntendra ^tvotāḥ vayaṃ tvayā rakṣitāḥ santaḥ ^bṛhatā mahatā ^rāyā dhanena ^madena hṛṣyāmaḥ |
8 |
‘dyaune ya indra’ iti trayodaśarcaṃ pañcamaṃ sūktaṃ bharadvājasyāpamaindraṃ ṛjubham |
‘vi piproḥ’ ityeṣā saptamī daśākṣaracatuṣṭayayuktā virāḍ |
anukramyate ca—‘dyaune vi piprorvirāṭ’ iti |
pṛṣṭhaya-
1. śa-viśvāsāham |
2. jha-ra-hṛṣyāsma |
3. kha-bha-daśakacatuṣṭayaḥ; jha-a-ta-na-ra- daśacatuṣṭayaḥ |
ṣaḍahasya ṣaṣṭhe'hani tṛtīyasavane ukthyastotrāṇi yadi dvipadāsu stuvīran yadi vābhiṣṭomasaṃsthaḥ syāt tadānīṃ mādhyaṃdine savanecchāvākaḥ svaśastra ārambhaṇīyābhya ūrdhvametasūktaṃ śaṃset |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘dyaurna ya indratyacchāvākaḥ’ (āśva. śrau. 8. 4) iti |
idameva sūktam indrāviṣṇvorakrāntināmyekāhe nikṣevalyanividvānam |
‘dyaurna ya indrati madhyaṃdinaḥ’ (āśva. śrau. 9. 7) iti ||
dyaurna ya indrābhi bhūmaryastasthau rayiḥ śarvasā pṛtsu janān | taṃ naḥ sahasrabharamurvarāsāṃ daddhi sūno sahaso vṛtraturam || 1 || dyauḥ | na | yaḥ | indra | abhi | bhūma | aryaḥ | tasthau | rayiḥ | śarvasā | pṛtsu | janān | tam | naḥ | sahasra'bharam | urvarā'sām | daddhi | sūno | sahasaḥ | vṛtra'turam || 1 ||
pyaḥ rayiḥ putrarūpaṃ dhanaṃ śarvasā balena pṛtsu saṃgrāmeṣu aryaḥ arīn śatrūn janān abhi tasthau ākrāman |
tatra dṛṣṭāntaḥ |
dyaurna |
dyonamānaḥ sūryo yathā bhūma bhūtāni ākrāmati tadvat |
he sahasaḥ sūno balasya putra indra tvaṃ taṃ putraṃ naḥ asmabhyaṃ daddhi dehi |
kīdṛśam |
sahasrabharaṃ sahasrasya dhanasya bhartāram urvarāsām urvarāṇāṃ sasyādyānāṃ bharmāṇaṃ sanitāraṃ sambhaktāraṃ vṛtraturaṃ vṛtrāṇāṃ śatrūṇāṃ taritāraṃ turvitāraṃ vā ||
divo na tubhyamanvinra satrāsuryaṃ devebhirdhāyi viśvam | ahiṃ yadvṛtramapo vavrivāṃsaṃ hanṛjīṣinviṣṇunā sacānaḥ || 2 || divaḥ | na | tubhyam | arnu | indra | satrā | asuryam | devebhiḥ | dhāyi | viśvam | ahim | yat | vṛtram | apaḥ | vavrivāṃsam | han | ṛjīṣin | viṣṇunā | sacānaḥ ||2||
divo na dive sūryāyeva he indra tubhyaṃ satrā satyameva viśvaṃ vyāptam asuryam asuratvaṃ balaṃ devebhiḥ devaiḥ stotṛbhiḥ anu dhāyi vyadhāyi |
akāri |
stotraiḥ stūyamānā devatā balavatī bhavatītyarthaḥ |
he ṛjīṣin vigatarasamomendra viṣṇunā sacānaḥ saṃgacchamānastvam |
‘paca mama- vāye’ iti dhāno rūpam |
yat yenāsuryeṇa apaḥ udakāni vavrivāṃsaṃ paritṛṇvantam ahim āgatya hantāraṃ vṛtram asuraṃ han ahan avadhīḥ tadvalaṃ vyadhāyi pūrveṇa saṃbandhaḥ ||
turvojīyāntvasastavīyānkṛtabrahmendro vṛddhamahāḥ | rājābhavanmadhunaḥ somyasya viśvāmāṃ yatpurāṃ durnumāvarta || 3 || turvan | ojīyān | tvamaḥ | tarvīyān | kṛta'brahmā | indraḥ | vṛddha'mahāḥ | rājā | abhavat | madhunaḥ | somyasya | viśvā'mām | yat | purām | durnum | āvarta || 3 ||
yat yadā indraḥ viśvāsāṃ sarvāsāṃ purāṃ purāṇāṃ danuṃ dārakaṃ vajram āvata prāpat tadā somyasya somamayasya madhunaḥ madhurasasya rājā abhavat svāmī āsīt |

turvan
1. kha-na-bha-ākrameta; jña-ākramyaṃta |

ᵥvṛddhamahāḥ vṛddhatejāḥ |
evaṃbhūta indro rājābhavaditi ||
śatairapadranpuṇarya indrāt daśoṇaye kavaye'rkasātau | vṛdhaiḥ śuṣṇasyāśurṣasya māyāḥ pitvo nārirecītik cana pra || 4 || śateḥ | apadran | puṇaryaḥ | indra | atra | daśa'oṇaye | kavaye | arka'sātau | vṛdhaiḥ | śuṣṇasya | aśupasya | māyāḥ | pitvaḥ | na | arirecīta | kim | cana | pra || 4 ||
ᵥatra asmin ᵥarkasātau |
arka'saṃ prāpyate'sminjhityarkasātiryuddham |

pañcamyarthe caturthī |
he ᵥindra tvatsahāyayāta kutsāta bibhyataḥ ᵥvaṇayaḥ etannāmakāsurāḥ ᵥśataiḥ śatasaṃkhyākaiḥ balaiḥ sārdham ᵥapadran apādravan |
apalāyanta |
api cendraḥ ᵥaśuṣasya aśuṣkasya |
saṃpūrṇabalasyetyarthaḥ |

piturityakṣanāmaitat |

maho duho arpa viśvāyu dhāyi vajrasya yatapartane pādi śuṣṇaḥ | uru ṣa sarathaṃ sārathaye karindraḥ kutsāya sūryasya sātau || 5 || mahaḥ | duhaḥ | arpa | viśva'āyu ᵥdhāyi | vajrasya | yata | patane | pādi | śuṣṇaḥ | uru | maḥ | mu'ratham | mārathaye | kṛḥ | indraḥ | kutsāya | sūryasya | sātau || 5 ||
ᵥyat yadā saḥ ᵥśuṣṇāḥ asuraḥ ᵥvajrasya ᵥpatna pāte ᵥpādi agacchat |
agniyatatyarthaḥ |

ᵥapa ᵥdhāyi apahitamakāri |
tiraskṛtamityarthaḥ |
ᵥmaḥ ᵥindraḥ ᵥmārathaye sārathibhūtāya ᵥkutsāya ᵥsarathaṃ samānarathaṃ ᵥsūryasya ᵥsātau bhajane nimittabhūte ᵥuru vistīrṇaṃ yathā bhavati tathā ᵥkaḥ akarot |

pra śyeno na madirmaṃśurasmai śiro dāsasya namucermathāyan | prāvanmī sādhyaṃ sasantaṃ pṛṇagrāyā samiṣā saṃ svasti || 6 || pra | śyenaḥ | na | madirm | aṃśum | asmai | śiraḥ | dāsasya | namuceḥ | mathāyan | pra | āvat | namīm | sādhyam | sumantam | pṛṇak | gayā | sam | iṣā | mam | svasti || 6 ||

ayā | ha | tyam | māyayā | vavṛdhānam | manaḥ'juvā | sva'tavaḥ | parvatena | acyutā | cit | vīḻitā | su'ojaḥ | rujaḥ | vi | duḻhā | dhṛṣatā | vi'rapśin || 6 ||
he ˅svatavaḥ svabhūtabalendra tvaṃ ˅manojuvā manovadgacchatā anena ˅parvatena bahuparvaṇā svakīyenāyudhena vajreṇa |
˅vajreṇa śataparvaṇā ' (ṛ. saṃ. 1. 80. 6) iti darśanāt |
˅ayā a nayā ˅māyayā ˅vavṛdhānaṃ vardhamānaṃ ˅tyaṃ taṃ prasiddhaṃ vṛtraṃ ˅vi ˅rujaḥ viśeṣaṇābhaṅkṣīḥ |
tathā he ˅svojaḥ śobhanatejaḥ he ˅virapśin he mahaṣmindra tvam ˅acyutā acyutāni ˅cit vināśarahitāmyapi ˅vīḻitā vīḻitāni aśithilakṛtāni ˅duḻhā dṛḍhāni purāṇi ˅dhṛṣatā dharṣakeṇa vajreṇa vyarujaḥ bhañjanānasi |
1. kha-gha-bha - puruhūta bahubhirāhūta |
2. gha - puruvaso bahudhana |

6. jha-jha-ta-na-ya-ra - 'ayā anayā' nāsti |
7. kha-bha - ciddha |
taṃ vo̭ dhi॒yā na॒vyā॒sā śavi̭ṣṭha॒ pratna̭ṃ pra॒tna॒vatpari̭ta॒saya̭dhyai | sa naṷ va॒kṣa॒nni॒māna̭ḥ su॒va॒hendro॒ viśvā̭ya॒ti du॒rhaṇā̭ni || 7 || tam | vaḥ | dhi॒yā | na॒vyā॒sā | śavi̭ṣṭha | pra॒tnam | pra॒tna॒'vat | pari̭'ta॒saya̭dhyai | saḥ | naḥ | va॒kṣat | a॒ni॒'māna̭ḥ | su॒'va॒haḥ | indra̭ḥ | viśvā̭ni | a॒ti | du॒ḥ'haṇā̭ni ||7||
^navyasā navatarayā ^dhiyā stutyā ^śaviṣṭha balavattamaṃ ^pratnaṃ purāṇaṃ he indra ^taṃ ^vaḥ tvāṃ ^pratnavat ciraṃtanā ṛṣayaḥ iva ^paritasayadhyai parito vistārayitum |
ahaṃ pravṛtto'smīti śeṣaḥ |
^animānaḥ aparimāṇaḥ ^suvahaḥ śobhanavahan ^saḥ ^indraḥ ^viśvāni samastāni ^durhāṇi durgāṇi ^naḥ asmabhyam ^ati ^vakṣat ativahatu ||
ā ja॒nāya̭ du॒haṇḙ pā॒rthi॒vāni̭ di॒vyāni̭ dī॒payo̭'nta॒rikṣā̭ | ta॒pā vṛ॒panvi॒śvata̭ḥ śo॒ci॒ṣā tā॒nbrahma̭dvi॒ṣe śo̭caya॒ kṣāma̭pa॒śca || 8 || ā | ja॒nāya̭ | du॒haṇḙ | pā॒rthi॒vāni̭ | di॒vyāni̭ | dī॒paya̭ | a॒nta॒rikṣā̭ | ta॒pāḥ | vṛ॒pan | vi॒śvata̭ḥ | śo॒ci॒ṣā | tān | brahma̭'dvi॒ṣe | śo̭caya | kṣām | a॒paḥ | ca || 8 ||
he indra tvaṃ ^duhaṇe sādhujanānāṃ drogdhuḥ ^janāya janasya rākṣasādeḥ |
pañcamyarthe caturthī ||
^pārthivāni pṛthivyāṃ bhavāni ^divyāni divi bhavāni ^antarikṣā antarikṣe bhavāni ca sthānāni ^ā ^dīpaya : ā samantāt tāpaya |
he ^vṛpan kāmānāṃ varṣitarindra tvaṃ ^viśvataḥ sarvato vidyamānān ^tān rākṣasādīn ^śociṣā tvadīyayā dīdhyā ^tapa daha |
kiṃca ^brahmadviṣe brāhmaṇadveṣṭre rākṣasādye |
brahmadviṣaṃ dagdhumiśyarthaḥ |
^kṣāṃ pṛthivīm ^apaśca antarikṣaṃ ca ^śocaya dīpaya |
āpa ityantarikṣanāmaitat ||
bhuvo̭ ja॒nasya̭ di॒vyasya॒ rājā̭ pā॒rthi॒vasya॒ jaga̭ta॒stveṣa̭saṃdṛk | dhi॒ṣva va॒jraṃ da̭kṣi॒ṇe indra॒ haste॒ viśvā̭ a॒jūrya॒ daya̭se॒ vi mā॒yāḥ || 9 || bhuvaḥ | ja॒nasya̭ | di॒vyasya̭ | rājā̭ | pā॒rthi॒vasya̭ | jaga̭taḥ | tveṣa̭'saṃdṛk | dhi॒ṣva | va॒jram | da̭kṣi॒ṇe | indra̭ | hastḙ | viśvā̭ | a॒jūrya | daya̭se | vi | mā॒yāḥ || 9 ||
he ^tveṣasaṃdṛk dīptadarśanendra ^divyasya divi bhavasya ^janasya ^rājā īśvaraḥ ^bhuvaḥ bhavasi |
^jagataḥ jaṅgamasya ^pārthivasya ca rājā bhavasi |
^dakṣiṇe ^haste ^vajraṃ ^dhiṣva nidhehi |

‘daya dānahiṃsāgati-rakṣaṇeṣu’ iti dhātuḥ |
he ^indra ^ajūrya jarayitumaśakyendra tvamiti ||
1. kha-gha-jha-bha-tāpayaḥ; śa-dīpaya | 2. kha-gha-bha-drogdhumiḥ; śa-drāgdhumiḥ | 3. gha-dhiṣva dhāraya | 4. kha-bha-‘tena ca’ nāsti | 5. śa-purīḥ | 6. kha-bha-‘daya...dhātuḥ’ nāsti | 7. śa-tvamiti śeṣaḥ |
ā saṃyatamindra ṇaḥ svasti śatrutūryāya bṛhatīmaṣṭrām | yathā dāsānyāryāṇi vṛtrā karo vajrinsutukā nahupāṇi || 10 || ā | sam | yatam | indra | naḥ | svastim | śatru | tūryāya | bṛhatīm | aṣṭrām | yathā | dāsāni | āryāṇi | vṛtrā | karaḥ | vajrin | su | tukā | nahupāṇi || 10 || he indra śatrutūryāya śatrūṇāṃ tāragāya bṛhatīṃ mahatīm amṛṣṭām ahiṃsitāṃ saṃyataṃ saṃyatīṃ saṃgacchamānāṃ svasti kṣemalakṣaṇāṃ saṃpadaṃ he indra naḥ asmabhyam ā bhara | vajrin vajravabhindra yathā svastyā dāsāni karmahīnāni manuṣyajātāni āryāṇi karmayuktāni karaḥ akaroḥ | nahupāṇi manuṣyasambandhīni | nahuṣā iti manuṣyanāmainat | vṛtrā vṛtrāṇi śatrūn sutukā sutukāni śobhanahiṃso- petānyakaroḥ ||
sa nau niyudbhiḥ puruhūta vedho viśvavārābhirā gahi prayajyo | na yā adevo varate na deva ārbhiyāhi tūyamā madyadrik || 11 || saḥ | naḥ | niyut | bhiḥ | puru | hūta | vedhaḥ | viśva | vārābhiḥ | ā | gahi | prayajyo iti pra'yajyo | na | yāḥ | adevaḥ | varate | na | devaḥ | ā | ābhiḥ | yāhi | tūyam | ā | madya'drik || 11 || he puruhūta he vedhaḥ vidhātarhe prayajyo prakṛṣṭena yajanīyendra saḥ tvaṃ viśvavārābhiḥ viśvavara- ṇīyābhiḥ saṃbhajanīyābhiḥ niyudbhiḥ aśvaiḥ naḥ asmān ā gahi āgaccha | adevaḥ asuraḥ yāḥ niyuto yānaśvān na varate na vārayati devaḥ ca na varate ābhiḥ niyudbhiḥ tūyam ā kṣiprameva madyadrik madabhimukhaḥ san ā yāhi āgaccha | adyāgamasya dvivacanaṃ chāndasam || 14 ||
‘suta ita’ iti daśarcamaṣṭamaṃ sūkaṃ bharadvājasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamaindram |

mahāvrate'pi nikevalye idamādīni trīṇi sūktāni |

suta itvaṃ nimiśla indra some stome brahmaṇiśasyamāna ukthe | yadvā yuktābhyāṃ maghavanharībhyāṃ bibhradvajraṃ bāhorindra yāsi || 1 || sute | ita | tvam | ni'miślaḥ | indra | some | stome | brahmaṇa | śasyamāne | ukthe | yat | vā | yuktābhyām | maghavan | harībhyām | bibhrat | vajram | bāhroḥ | indra | yāsi || 1 || some sute ita abhiputa eva sati brahmaṇa bṛhati mahati stome stotre uccāryamāṇe sati ukthe śasyamāne mati he indra tvaṃ nimiślaḥ nimiśraḥ san harī saṃyojayan he maghavan

dhanavan ᐡindra ᐡtvaṃ ᐡbāhvoḥ hastayoḥ ᐡvajraṃ svakīyamāyudhaṃ ᐡbibhrat dhārayan ᐡyuktābhyāṃ rathe niyuktābhyāṃ ᐡharibhyām aśvābhyāṃ ᐡyāsi ᐡyadvā āgacchasīti yacca tatsome'bhiśute iti saṃbandhaḥ ||
yadvā divi pārye suṣmimindra vṛtrahatye'vasi śūrasātau |
yadvā dakṣasya bibhyuṣo abibhyadarandhayaḥ śardhata indra dasyūna ||
2 ||
yat | vā | divi | pārye | suṣmim | indra | vṛtra'hatye | avasi | śūra'sātau | yat | vā | dakṣasya | bibhyuṣaḥ | abibhyat | arandhayaḥ | śardhataḥ | indra | dasyūna || 2 ||
he ᐡindra tvaṃ ᐡdivi dyuloke ᐡśūrasātau śūraiḥ bhaṭaiḥ saṃbhajanīye ᐡvṛtrahatye yuddhe ca ᐡpārye prāptavye sati ᐡsuṣmim abhiṣotāraṃ yajamānam ᐡavasi ᐡyadvā rakṣasīti yacca |
veti samuccaye |
ᐡdakṣasya yajñādiṣu samarthasya ᐡbibhyuṣaḥ śatrubhyo bibhyato janasya ᐡśardhataḥ saṃgrāme utsahamānān ᐡdasyūna upakṣapayitṝn śatrūn he ᐡindra tvam ᐡabibhyat bhītirahitaḥ san ᐡarandhayaḥ ᐡyadvā vaśīkaroṣīti yacca tatsarvaṃ some'bhiśute iti pūrveṇa saṃbandhaḥ ||
tathā coktaṃ—‘yadvādyordhārayanvajraṃ haribhyāṃ yāsi vṛtrahan |
yacca rakṣasi saṃgrāme nṝn somamabhiśupataḥ ||
yacca karmasu dakṣasya yajamānasya ye'rayaḥ |
vaśī- karoṣi tāṃstasmai bibhyate bhītivarjitaḥ ||
tassome'bhiśute stotraśastrayośca pravṛttayoḥ |
iti dūco'yame- kārthaḥ suta itvamiti dvayam’ iti ||
pātāṃ sutamindrau astu somaṃ praṇenīrugro jaritāramūtī |
kartā vīrāya survaya u lokaṃ dātā vasu stuvate kīraye cit ||
3 ||
pātā | sutam | indraḥ | astu | somam | pra'nenīḥ | ugraḥ | jaritāram | ūtī | kartā | vīrāya | su'rvaye | ūṃ iti | lokam | dātā | vasu | stuvate | kīraye | cit || 3 ||

nunantvāt ‘na lokā- vyayaniṣṭhā’ iti ṣaṣṭhīpratiṣedhaḥ ||
kīdṛśaḥ |
ᐡūtī ūrjayā mārgeṇa ᐡjaritāraṃ stotāraṃ ᐡpraṇenīḥ prakarṣeṇa netā ᐡugraḥ udūrṇaḥ ᐡvīrāya yajñādikarmasu dakṣāya ᐡsurvaye somābhiṣavaṃ kurvate yajamānāya ᐡlokaṃ sthānaṃ ᐡkartā |

4 ||
gantā | iyanti | sarvanā | hari'bhyām | bṛbhiḥ | vajram | pupiḥ | somam | didiḥ | gāḥ | kartā | vīram | naryam | sarva'vīram | śrotā | havam | gṛṇataḥ | stoma'vāhāḥ || 4 ||
indraḥ ᐡharibhyāṃ svakīyābhyām ᐡiyanti hṛdayasthāni trīṇi ᐡsavanā savanāni ᐡgantā gamanaśīlo bhavatu |

ᐡvajraṃ svakīyamāyudhaṃ ᐡbabhriḥ bhartā dhārakaḥ ᐡsomam abhiṣutaṃ ᐡpapiḥ pātā ᐡgāḥ |
1. kha-jha-ta-na-bha-ra-arandhaya iti |
^dadiḥ dātā ^naryaṃ manuṣyahitaṃ ^sarvavīraṃ bahuputropetaṃ ^vīraṃ putraṃ ^kartā yajamānāya dātā ^gṛṇataḥ stuvataḥ stotuḥ saṃbandhi ^havaṃ stotraṃ ^śrotā śrāvakaḥ ^stomavāhāḥ stomaiḥ stotrairvahanīyaḥ ||
asmai vayaṃ yadvāvān tadviviṣm indrāya yo naḥ pradivo apuskaḥ |

5 ||
asmai | vayam | yat | vatrān | tat | viviṣmaḥ | indrāya | yaḥ | naḥ | pra'divaḥ | apaḥ | kariti kaḥ | sute | some | stumasi | śaṃsat | ukthā | indrāya | brahma | vardhanam | yathā | asat || 5 ||
^pradivaḥ purātanaḥ ^yaḥ indraḥ ^naḥ asmadartham ^apaḥ popaṇādikaṃ karma ^kaḥ karoti ayamindraḥ ^yat stotrādikaṃ ^vavāna kāmayate ^asmai ^indrāya ^tadviviṣmaḥ vyāpnumaḥ |
kurma ityarthaḥ |
^some ^sute abhiṣute sati ^stumasi stumaḥ |
indrāya stotrāṇi kurmaḥ |
^ukthā ukthāni śastrāṇi ^śaṃsat śaṃsantaḥ |
prathamābahuvacanasya luk |

15 ||
brahmāṇi hi cakruṣe vardhanāni tāvatta indra matibhirviiviṣmaḥ |

6 ||
brahmāṇi | hi | cakruṣe | vardhanāni | tāvat | te | indra | matibhiḥ | viviṣmaḥ | sute | some | suta'pāḥ | śam'tamāni | rāndrathā | kriyāsma | vakṣaṇāṇi | yajñaiḥ || 6 ||
ha ^indra tvaṃ ^hi yasmāt kāraṇāt ^brahmāṇi stotrāṇi ^vardhanāni svayameva vṛddhikarāṇi ^cakruṣe kṛtavānasi tasmāt kāraṇāt ^tāvat nāvanta tādṛśāni stotrāṇi ^te tubhyaṃ ^matibhiḥ buddhibhirvayaṃ ^viviṣmaḥ vyāpnumaḥ |
vardhanāni stotrāṇi mama yathā bhaveyustvaṃ tādṛśāni kalpitavānasītyarthaḥ |
^api ca he ^sutapāḥ abhiṣutasya somasya pātastvāmuddiśya ^sute ^māṃme abhiṣute sati ^śaṃtamāni sukhakṛttamāni ^rāndrathā rāndrayāṇi ramaṇīyāni ^yajñaiḥ havirbhiryuktāni ^vakṣaṇāṇi vāhakāni stotrāṇi ^kriyāsma karavāma ||
sa nau bodhi puroḻāśaṃ rarāṇaḥ pibā tu somaṃ gotrajīkamindra |
edaṃ barhiryajamānasya sīdoruṃ kṛdhi tvāyata u lokam ||
7 ||
saḥ | naḥ | bodhi | puroḻāśam | rarāṇaḥ | piba | tu | somam | gotra'jīkam | indra | ā | idam | barhiḥ | yajamānasya | sīda | urum | kṛdhi | tvā'yata | ū iti | lokam ||7||
he ^indra ^rarāṇaḥ ramamāṇaḥ ^saḥ ^tvaṃ ^naḥ asmadīyaṃ ^puroḻāśam etallakṣaṇaṃ haviḥ ^bodhi budhyasva |
kiṃca ^gotrajīkaṃ govikāradadhyādibhiḥ ^saṃskṛtamabhiṣutaṃ ^somaṃ ^tu kṣipraṃ ^piba pibeḥ |
tadarthaṃ ca ^yajamānasya saṃbandhi ^idaṃ ^barhiḥ ^ā ^māṃtra abhiṣvā |
tadanantaraṃ ^svāyataḥ svāmicchato yajamānasya ^lokaṃ sthānam ^uruṃ vistīrṇaṃ ^kṛdhi kuru |
^u iti pūraṇaḥ ||
1. gha - ca sute some |
2. gha - govikāraṃdadhyādibhiḥ; śa - govikāraṃ dadhyādibhiḥ |
3. kha-jha-jha-ta-na-bha-ra - 'uṃ vistīrṇaṃ ' nāsti |
sa mandasvā hyanu joṣamugra pra tvā yajñārta ime aśuvantu | preme harvāsa: puruhutasme ā tveyaṃ dhīrarvasa indra yamyā: || 8 || sa: | mandasva | hi | anu | joṣam | ugra | pra | tvā | yajñārta: | ime | aśuvantu | pra | ime | harvāsa: | puru'hutam | asme iti | ā | tvā | iyam | dhī: | avase | indra | yamyā: ||8||
he 'ḍama uṭūrṇebalendra 'sa: tvam 'anu 'joṣaṃ kāmānuguṇaṃ yathā bhavati tathā 'mandasva modasva ||
hiśabda: pādapūraṇa: |
hiyoge mandasvetyākhyātasya vyatyayāt sarvānudāttatvam ||
'ime 'yajñāsa: yajñā: somā: |
'tvā tvāṃ 'pra 'abhuvantu prāpnuvantu |
he 'puruhuta bahubhirāhūta 'indra 'tvām 'asme asmadīyāni 'ime 'havāsa: havā: stotrāṇi prāpnuvantu ' |
'iyaṃ 'dhī: stuti: 'tvā tvām 'avase asmākaṃ rakṣaṇāya 'ā 'yamyā: āyacchantu 'niyacchantu ' ||
taṃ va: sakhāya: saṃ yathā suteṣu somaṃbhirīṃ pṛṇatā bhojamindram | kuvittasmā asati no bharāya na suṣmimindro'vase mṛdhāti || 9 || tam | va: | sakhāya: | sam | yathā | suteṣu | somaṃbhi: | īm | pṛṇata | bhojam | indram | kuvit | tasmai | asati | na: | bharāya | na | suṣmim | indra: | avase | mṛdhāti || 9 ||
he 'sakhāya: stotāra: 'va: yūyaṃ someṣu 'suteṣu abhiṣuteṣu satsu 'bhojaṃ dātāraṃ 'tam 'īm etam 'indraṃ 'somebhi: somairyathākāmaṃ 'saṃ 'pṛṇata saṃpūrayata |
'tasmai indrāya 'kuvit bahukaraṇam |
kuviditi bahunāmetat |
'asati astu |
kimartham |
'na: asmākaṃ 'bharāya bharaṇāya poṣaṇāya |
'indra: 'suṣmim abhibhavaṇaśīlaṃ yajamānam 'avase tarpaṇāya 'na 'mṛdhāti na bādhate ||
evedindra: sute astāvi some bharadvājeṣu kṣayadinmaghona: | asadyathā jaritra uta sūrindro rāyo viśvavārasya dātā || 10 || eva | it | indra: | sute | astāvi | some | bharadvājeṣu | kṣayat | it | maghona: | asat | yathā | jaritre | uta | sūri: | indra: | gaya: | 'viśva'vārasya | dātā || 10 ||
'maghona: dhanavato haviṣmato yajamānasya 'kṣayat īśvara: 'indra: 'some 'sute abhiṣute sati 'bharadvājeṣu bharadvāje mayi 'eva evam 'astāvi stuto'bhūta |

1 ||

yadvā |
sarvajanāhlādakasya varṣaṇasya kartā bhavati |
he ᵥśūra vikrāntendra ᵥbṛhan mahān ᵥte tvadīyaḥ ᵥmahā mahimā ᵥrodasyoḥ dyāvāpṛthivyoḥ ᵥpra ᵥśirice atiricyate |

he ᵥpuruhūta bahubhirāhūta ᵥindra tvadīyāḥ ᵥputrīḥ bahvyaḥ ᵥutayaḥ rakṣāḥ ᵥvi ᵥhahuḥ viśeṣeṇa rohanti |

vatsānāṃ na tantaryasta indra dāmanvanto adāmānaḥ sudāman ||
4 ||
śacī'vataḥ | te | puru'śāka | śākāḥ | gavām'iva | sutaryaḥ | su'caraṇīḥ | vatsānām | na | tantaryaḥ | te | indra | dāman'vantaḥ | adāmānaḥ | su'dāman || 4 ||
he ᵥpuruśāka bahukarmendra ᵥśacīvataḥ prajñāvataḥ ᵥte tvadīyāḥ ᵥśākāḥ śaktayaḥ karmāṇi vā sarvataḥ saṃcarantīti śeṣaḥ |
tatra dṛṣṭāntaḥ |
ᵥgavāmiva dhenūnāṃ ᵥsutayaḥ mārgā yathā ᵥsaṃcaraṇīḥ sarvatra saṃcāriṇo bhavanti tadvat |
api ca ᵥvatsānāṃ ᵥna ᵥtantayaḥ |
tantirnāma dīrghaprasāritā rajjuḥ yatra niyatairviśākhadāmabhiḥ bahavo vatsā badhyante |
yathā tantayo bahūnāṃ vatsānāṃ bandhakā he ᵥsudāman śobhanadāna ᵥindra tathā ᵥte tvadīyāḥ śākāḥ ᵥdāmanvantaḥ bandhanavantaḥ bahūnāṃ śatrūṇāṃ bandhakāḥ ᵥadāmānaḥ svayamamyairabaddhāḥ ||
anyadya karvaramanyadya śo'sacca sanmuhurācakirindraḥ |
mitro no atra varuṇaścapūṣāryo vaśasya paryaitāsti ||

anyat | adya | karvaram | anyat | ū~ iti | śvaḥ | amat | ca | sat | muhuḥ | ā'cakriḥ | indraḥ | mitraḥ | naḥ | atra | varuṇaḥ | ca | pūṣā | aryaḥ | vaśasya | pari'etā | asti || 5 ||

yadvā |
triśiraso viśvarūpasya vadhādikam asatkarma lokopadravakāriṇo vṛtrāderasurasya vadhādirūpaṃ satkarma ca ᵥmuhuḥ punaḥpunarasau ᵥindraḥ ᵥācakriḥ kartā bhavati |
evaṃ parasparavilakṣaṇaṃ karma svamahasā punaḥpunaravartayan pratidivasamanyadeva pareṣāmāsādhāraṇaṃ karma karotītyarthaḥ |
evaṃvidha indraḥ ᵥatra asmin yajñe ᵥnaḥ asmākaṃ ᵥvaśasya kāmayitavyasya phalasya svargādeḥ ᵥparyaitāsti parigamayitā bhavatu |
astailaṃṭi rūpam |

he indra tvat tvatskāśāt ukthebhiḥ ukthaiḥ śastraiḥ yajñaiḥ havirbhiḥ stotāraḥ kāmanā- tmanaḥ vi anayanta vividhaṃ prāpayanti |

vājamannamicchanto bharadvājāḥ stotāraḥ ābhiḥ pūrvoktābhiḥ sustutibhiḥ śobhanābhiḥ stutibhiḥ jagmuḥ prāpuḥ |
kṣipragamane dṛṣṭāntaḥ |
aśvāḥ vāhāḥ ājiṃ na saṃgrāmaṃ yathā śīghraṃ prāpustadvat ||

7 ||
na | yam | jaranti | śaradaḥ | na | māsāḥ | na | dyāvaḥ | indram | avakarṣayanti | vṛddhasya | cit | vardhatām | asya | tanūḥ | stomaiḥ | ukthaiḥ | ca | śasyamānā || 7 ||
śaradaḥ saṃvatsarāḥ yam indraṃ na jaranti na jarayanti nāpakṣīṇayanti |
tathā māsāḥ ca nāpakṣīṇayanti |
tathā dyāvaḥ divasaśca yam indraṃ na avakarṣayanti nālpībhāvayanti vṛddhasya cit pravṛddhasyāpi asya indrasya tanūḥ śarīraṃ stomaiḥ asmadīyaiḥ stotraiḥ ukthaiśca śastraiśca śasyamānā stūyamānā satī vardhatāṃ pravṛddhā bhavatu ||
na vīḻave narmate na sthirāya na śardhate dasyujūtāya stvān |

8 ||
na | vīḻave | narmate | na | sthirāya | na | śardhate | dasyujūtāya | stvān | ajñāḥ | indrasya | giryaḥ | cit | ṛṣvāḥ | gambhīre | cit | bhavati | gādham | asmai ||8||

he ᵥindra ᵥābhiḥ asmadīyābhiḥ stutibhiḥ ᵥmithatīḥ śatrusainyāni hiṃsatīḥ ᵥspṛdhaḥ asmadīyāḥ senāḥ ᵥariṣaṇyan ahiṃsan |
pālayantyarthaḥ |

3 ||
indra | jāmayaḥ | uta | ye | ajāmayaḥ | arvācīnāsaḥ | vṛnapaḥ | yuyuje | tvam | eṣām | vithurā | śavāṃsi | jahi | vṛṣṇyāni | kṛṇuhi | parācaḥ || 3 ||
he ᵥindra ᵥjāmayaḥ jñātirūpāḥ saṃnikṛṣṭā ye śatravaḥ |
ᵥuta api ca ᵥajāmayaḥ dūradeśe sthitā ᵥye śatravaḥ ᵥarvācīnāsaḥ asmadabhimukhāḥ ᵥvanupaḥ hiṃsantaḥ ᵥyuyuje udyuktā bhavanti ᵥeṣām ubhayavidhānāṃ śatrūṇāṃ saṃbandhāni ᵥśavāṃsi balāni ᵥvithurā' vithurāṇi' hīnāni ᵥtvaṃ kuruvita śeṣaḥ |
tathā ᵥvṛṣṇyāni eṣāṃ vīryāṇi ᵥjahi nāśaya |

he ᵥindra ᵥśūraḥ ᵥtvadanugṛhīto vīraḥ ᵥśarīraiḥ aṅgaiḥ ᵥśūraṃ ᵥvā vīramapi ᵥvanate hanti |

śūrau |
kadā banata ityetadāha |
vtanvāhā śarīreṇa śobhamānau parasparavirodhinau vtasya yudve vyat yadā vkṛṇvate saṃgrāmaṃ kurvāte |
vyat yadā ca vtoke vvā putranimitte vā vgoṣu nimittabhūtāsu vā vtanaye pautre nimittabhūte vā vapsu udakeṣu nimitteṣu vā vurvarāsu sarvasyābvāsu bhūmiṣu nimittāsu vkrandasī krandamānāvākrośantau vvi vbravate vivadate |
he indra putrādilābhajayaḥ 1 svadanagṛhītasya bhavatītyarthaḥ ||

sākamedhepu māhenyāmiṣṭau indrasya vṛtraghno2 yājyā |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘anu te dāyi maha indriyāya viśvakarmanhaviṣā vāvṛdhānaḥ’ (āśva. śrau. 2. 18) iti |

anu te dāyi maha indriyārya satrā te viśvamanu vṛtrahatyai | anu kṣatramanu sahau yajñendraṃ devebhiranu te nṛpahai || 8 || anu | te | dāyi | mahe | indriyārya | satrā | te | viśvam | anu | vṛtra'hatyai | anu | kṣatram | anu | sahaḥ | yajñatra | indra | devebhiḥ | anu | te | nṛ'sahau || 8 || he indra ▼mahe mahate ▼te tumyam ▼indriyāya aiśvaryārtham ▼anu ▼dāyi anvadāyi | anudīyate sma | ▼vṛtrahatye vṛtravadhe nimitte ▼te tumyaṃ ▼viśvaṃ samastaṃ ▼satrā satyam ▼anu dāyi | kaiḥ | kimanu dāyīti tadubhayamāha | yena viśvaṃ bibharti tata ▼kṣatraṃ balam ▼anu dāyi | yena śatrūnabhibhavati tadguṇaviśiṣṭaṃ2 ▼sahaḥ balam ▼anu dāyi | he ▼yajatra yajanīya ▼indra ▼te tumyaṃ ▼nṛpahai yude ▼devebhiḥ sarvairdevairetasarvam ▼anu dāyi ||

1. kha-jha-ta-na-bha-ra-śa- ‘te’ nāsti | 2. kha-jha-jha-bha-vṛtraghnaḥ; ta-ra-vṛtrahaṇe; śa-vṛtrahaṇeti | 3. kha-jha-ta-na-bha-ra-°viśiṣṭakam | 4. jha-ta-nṛsahye | 5. kha-bha-śa-dandra tvam | 6. jha-spṛdha- mānāḥ senāḥ |

‘śrudhī na indra’ ityṛcaṃ tṛtīyaṃ sūktaṃ bharadvājasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamaindram |
tathā cānukramyate—‘śrudhī no'ṣṭau’ iti |
viniyogo laiṅgikaḥ ||
śrudhī na indra hayāmasi tvā maho vājasya sātau vāvṛṣaṇāḥ | saṃ yadi'śo'yanta śūrasātā ugraṃ no'vaḥ pārye ahandāḥ || 1 || śrudhi | naḥ | indra | hayāmasi | tvā | mahaḥ | vājasya | sātau | vāvṛṣaṇāḥ | sam | yat | viśaḥ | aryanta | śūra'sātau | ugram | naḥ | avaḥ | pārye | ahan | dāḥ ||1||
he ^indra ^vavṛṣaṇāḥ somasavāṃ siñcanto vayaṃ stotāraḥ ^mahaḥ mahataḥ ^vājasya annasya ^sātau lābhārthaṃ ^tvā tvāṃ ^hayāmasi āhvayāmaḥ |
he indra tvaṃ ^naḥ asmākaṃ tadāhvānaṃ ^śrudhi śṛṇu |
^yat yadā ^viśaḥ janāḥ ^śūrasātau yuddhe ^sam ayanta saṃgacchante tadā ^pārye antime ^ahan ahani divase ^naḥ asmabhyam ^ugram uttarṇam ^avaḥ rakṣaṇaṃ ^dāḥ adāḥ prayaccha ||
tvāṃ vājī havate vājineyo maho vājasya gadhyasya sātau | tvāṃ vṛtreṣvindra satpatiṃ tarutraṃ tvāṃ caṣṭe muṣṭihā goṣu yudhyan || 2 || tvām | vājī | havate | vājineyaḥ | mahaḥ | vājasya | gadhyasya | sātau | tvām | vṛtreṣu | indra | sat'patim | tarutram | tvām | caṣṭe | muṣṭi'hā | goṣu | yudhyan ||2||
^vājī havirlakṣaṇavān ^vājineyaḥ vājinyāḥ putro bharadvājo he indra ^tvāṃ ^gadhyasya sarvaiḥ prāpyasya ^mahaḥ mahataḥ ^vājasya annasya ^sātau lābhe nimitte ^havate stauti |
api ca he ^indra ^satpatiṃ sañjanānāṃ pālakaṃ ^tarutraṃ durjanānāṃ tārakaṃ ^tvāṃ ^vṛtreṣu upadraveṣu nimitteṣu bharadvājo havate |
^muṣṭihā muṣṭibalena śatrūṇāṃ hantā ^goṣu nimittabhūtāsu ^yudhyan śatrubhiḥ saha yuddhaṃ kurvan bharadvājaḥ ^tvāṃ ^caṣṭe paśyati |
pratipālayate ||
tvaṃ ka॒viṃ cauda॒yo'rka̭sā॒tau tvaṃ ku॒tsāya̭ śu॒ṣṇaṃ dā॒śuṣḙ va॒rka | tvaṃ śi॒rau a॒marma̭ṇaḥ parā॒han a॒tithi̭gvāya॒ śaṃsya̭ṃ ka॒riṣyaṋ || 3 || tvam | ka॒vim | cauda॒yaḥ | arka̭'sā॒tau | tvam | ku॒tsāya̭ | śu॒ṣṇam | dā॒śuṣḙ | va॒rka | tvam | śi॒raḥ | a॒marma̭ṇaḥ | parā̭ | a॒han | a॒tithi̭gvāya | śaṃsya̭m | ka॒riṣyaṋ || 3 ||

varka iti
1. kha-jha-jha-ta-na-ma-ya-ra-te tvattaḥ |
2. kha-jha-jha-ta-na-bha-ra-śūrasātā śūrasātau |
vṛṇakbhecchedanārthasya' luṭhi rūpam ||
tathā tvam atithivvāya atithīnāmabhigantre divodāsāya śaṃsyaṃ stutyaṃ sukhaṃ kariṣyan amarmaṇaḥ marmahīnamātmānaṃ manyamānasya śambarasya śiraḥ śīrṣa parāhana avadhīḥ ||
tvaṃ rathaṃ pra bharau yodhamṛṣamāvo yudhyantaṃ vṛṣabhaṃ darśadhum |
tvaṃ tugraṃ vetasave sacāṃhanvaṃ turjiṃ guṇantamindra tūtoḥ ||
4 ||
tvam | ratham | pra | bharaḥ | yodham | ṛṣvam | āvaḥ | yudhyantam | vṛṣabham | darśadhum | tvam | tugram | vetasave | sacā | ahan | tvam | turjim | guṇantam | indra | tutoriti tūtoḥ ||4||
he indra tvaṃ vṛṣabhasaṃjñakāya rājñe yodhaṃ yuddhasādhanam ṛṣvaṃ mahāntaṃ rathaṃ pra bharaḥ prāpayaḥ |
api ca yudhyantaṃ śatrubhiḥ saha yuddhaṃ kurvantaṃ daśadhuṃ daśa divasā yudhyato yasya gatāḥ taṃ vṛṣabham etadākhyaṃ rājānam āvaḥ yuddhādapīpalaḥ |
kiṃca tvaṃ vetasave etannāmne rājñe sacā sahāyabhūtaḥ san tugram asuram ahan hatavānasi |
vetasurnāma kaścidasuraḥ |
atra tasmādanyo'sāvucyate |
yadvā |
vetasava iti tṛtīyārthe caturthī |
vetasunāsureṇa sahitaṃ tugraṃ hatavānasi |
tathā ca manvāntare śrūyate— ‘ahaṃ piteva vetasū~rabhiṣṭaye tugraṃ kutsāya smadibhaṃ ca randhayam’ (ṛ. saṃ. 10. 49. 4) iti |
he indra tvaṃ guṇantaṃ tvāṃ stuvantaṃ turjim etadākhyaṃ rājānaṃ tūtoḥ avadhayaḥ ||

ava giredāsaṃ śambaraṃ hantrāvo divodāsaṃ citrābhirūtī ||
5 ||
tvam | tat | uktham | indra | bṛhaṇā | kariti kaḥ | pra | yat | śatā | sahasrā | śūra | darṣi | ava | gireḥ | dāsam | śambaram | han | pra | āvaḥ | diveḥ'dāsam | citrābhiḥ | ūtī ||5||
he indra barhaṇā barhaṇaḥ śatrūṇāṃ hiṃsakaḥ tvam ukthaṃ praśasyaṃ tat karma kaḥ akaroḥ |
kiṃ tatkarmetyucyate |

tathā ca nigamāntare—‘adhvaryavo yaḥ śataṃ śambarasya’ (ṛ. saṃ. 2. 14. 6) iti |
tathā tvaṃ dāsaṃ yajñādikarmaṇāmupakṣapayitāraṃ gireḥ parvatāntargataṃ śambaram asuram ava han avāvadhīḥ |
tathāpi śrūyate—‘yaḥ śambaraṃ parvateṣu kṣiyantam’ (ṛ. saṃ. 2. 12. 11) iti |
kiṃca citrābhiḥ vicitrābhiḥ ūtī ūtibhiḥ rakṣābhiḥ divodāsaṃ rājānaṃ prāvaḥ prakarṣeṇa pālayasi sma ||
1||

1. kha-jña-ta-na-ma-ra-chedanārthasya | 2. kha-jña-na-ma-ra-śa-yudhyanto; ta-yuddhanto | 3. kha-na-bha- tathā ca | 4. gha-śa-vicitrābhir indra tvamābhiḥ |

avadhīrityartha: |
kiṃca he indra tvaṃ piṭhīnase etannāmakāya rajim etadākhyāṃ kanyāṃ vā rājyaṃ vā daśasyan prayacchan śraddhayā prajñayā vaṣṭi ṣaṣṭisaṃkhyākāni sahasrā bhaṭānāṃ sahasrāṇi sacā saha yugapadeva ahan avadhī: ||

' kimasya made ' ityaṣṭarcaṃ caturthaṃ sūktaṃ bharadvājasyārṣam |

ᵥasya somasya ᵥmade sati ᵥindra: ᵥkiṃ ᵥcakāra kṛtavān |
ᵥkimu kiṃca ᵥasya somasya ᵥpītau pāne sati kiṃ cakāra |

pānāt pūrvam indra: saṃmena saha vasatītyartha: |

ᵥnūtanāsa: nūtanā idānīṃtanā: stotāraśca ᵥkimu lebhire ||

evamākṣisa indrastasmā ṛpaya īpsitaṃ dhanaṃ pradadau |

‘abhūrekaḥ’ iti pañcarcamekaṃ sūktaṃ bharadvājasya suhotrasyārṣam |

indro devatā |

tathā pañcamāraṇyakam—‘abhūreko rathipate rathīṇā- mityaṣṭau sūktāni’ (ai. ā. 5. 2. 2) iti |

vi toke apsu tanaye ca sūre'vaucanta carṣaṇīyo vivācaḥ ||
1 ||
abhūḥ | ekaḥ | rayipate | rathīṇām | ā | hastayoḥ | adhithāḥ | indra | kṛṣṭīḥ | vi | toke | apsu | tanaye | ca | sūre | avaucanta | carṣaṇīyoḥ | vivācaḥ || 1 ||

apūrvyā purutamānyasmai muhe vīrāya tavase turāya | virāśvine vajriṇe śaṃtamāni vacā~syāsā sthavirāya takṣam || 1 || apūrvyā | puru'tamāni | asmai | muhe | vīrāya | tavase | turāya | vi'raśmine | vajriṇe | śam'tamāni | vacā~si | āsā | sthavirāya | takṣam || 1 ||

sa vahnirṛkbhirgoṣu śardhanmṛñjibhiḥ puruhatvā jigāya | puraḥ purohā sakhibhiḥ sakhīndvalhā ruroja kavibhiḥ kaviḥ san || 3 || saḥ | vahni'bhiḥ | ṛk'bhiḥ | goṣu | śardhat | mita'jñu'bhiḥ | puru'kṛtvā | jigāya | puraḥ | puraḥ'hā | sakhi'bhiḥ | sakhi''yan | dṛlhā | ruroja | kavi'bhiḥ | kaviḥ | san ||3||
puruhatvā bahukarmakṛt saḥ indraḥ vahnibhiḥ haviṣāṃ voḍhṛbhiḥ ṛkbhiḥ stotṛbhiḥ śaśvat sarvadā mitajñubhiḥ saṃkucitajānubhirjharobhiḥ saha goṣu nimittabhūteṣu jigāya asurān jitavān |

turāṣāṭ turāṇāṃ hiṃsakānāṃ soḍhābhivitā saḥ indraḥ sargeṇa sarvadodyuktena śavasā balena atyaiḥ aśvaiḥ satatagāmibhistejobhirvā taktaḥ saṃgataḥ san dakṣiṇataḥ dakṣiṇāyane apaḥ udakāni visṛjatīti śeṣaḥ |
itthā itthamena prakāreṇa sṛjānāḥ sṛjyamānāstā āpaḥ arthaṃ gantavyam apramṛpyam anyairakṣobhyaṃ samudraṃ divedive pratidinam anapāvṛt apāvartanaṃ punarāgamanaṃ yathā na bhavati tathā vivipuḥ vyāpnuvanti ||

‘ya ojiṣṭhaḥ’ iti pañcamaṃ daśamaṃ sūktaṃ śunahotrasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamaindram |

1 |
yaḥ | ojiṣṭhaḥ | indra | tam | su | naḥ | dāḥ | madaḥ | vṛṣan | su'abhiṣṭiḥ | dāsvān | saurvaśyam | yaḥ | vanavat | su'aśvaḥ | vṛtrā | samatsu | sasahata | amitrān || 1 ||

puruhūta yoḥ purugūrta ṛmvā ekaḥ purupraśasto asti yajñaiḥ | ratho na mahe śavase yujāno'smābhirindrau anumādyau bhūta || 2 || puru'hūtaḥ | yaḥ | puru'gūrtaḥ | ṛmvā | ekaḥ | puru'praśastaḥ | asti | yajñaiḥ | rathaḥ | na | mahe | śavase | yujānaḥ | asmābhiḥ | indraḥ | anu'mādyaḥ | bhūta || 2 ||
puruhūtaḥ bahubhiruhūtaḥ purugūrtaḥ bahubhirudyamitaḥ ṛmvā mahān ekaḥ pradhānabhūta evaṃguṇaviśiṣṭaḥ yaḥ indraḥ yajñaiḥ yaṣṭṛbhiḥ purupaiḥ yajanasādhanaiḥ stotrairvā purupraśastaḥ asti bahudhā praśastaḥ stuto bhavati saḥ indraḥ ratho na ratha iva mahe mahate śavase balāya tadartha yujānaḥ asmatstutibhiryujya- mānaḥ san asmābhiḥ stotrabhiḥ anumādyaḥ bhūta sarvadā stutyo bhavatu ||
na yaṃ hiṃsanti dhītayo na vāṇīrindraṃ nakṣantīdabhi vardhayantīḥ | yadi stotāraḥ śataṃ yatsasahasraṃ gṛṇanti girvaṇasaṃ śaṃ tadasmai || 3 || na | yam | hiṃsanti | dhītayaḥ | na | vāṇīḥ | indram | nakṣanti | it | abhi | vardhayantīḥ | yadi | stotāraḥ | śatam | yat | sahasram | gṛṇanti | girvaṇasam | śam | tat | asmai || 3 ||
yam indraṃ dhītayaḥ karmāṇi paricaraṇāni na hiṃsanti na bādhante |
vāṇīḥ vācaḥ stutayaśca yaṃ na bādhante |
dātumasamarthaḥ puruṣo'nyakṛtaiḥ paricaraṇaiḥ stutibhiśca bhṛśaṃ khidyate'sya cendrasya bahudhanatvādvātvācca tasmin prayujyamānāni paricaraṇāni stotrāṇi ca bādhahetavo nābhūvannityarthaḥ |
kiṃ tarhītyata āha |

yat yadi ca sahasraṃ sahasrasaṃkhyākāḥ stotāraḥ stuvanti tat sarvaṃ stotram asmai indrāya śaṃ sukhakaraṃ bhavati ||

⁷etat etasmin ⁷divi divase saumye'hani ⁷arcaiva arcanādhanena stotreṇaiva ⁷māsā mānena ‘indrāya tvā vṛtraghne’ ityādimantrasādhyena yuktaḥ ⁷mimiksaḥ vasatīvaryakadhanāsyābhirindrāsiktaḥ ⁷somaḥ ⁷asmai |
vibhaktivyatyayaḥ |
asmin ⁷indre ⁷nyayāmi miyato'bhūt |
yadvā |
asmai indrāyaitastotraṃ bravāṇīti śeṣaḥ |
divi dyuloke arcaiva arkaḥ sūrya iva māsā |
luptopametat |
māti paricchinattīti māsaścandramāḥ |
sa iva mimikso yuṣṭyudakānāṃ sektā ya indro vartate'sminnindre'bhidhutaḥ somo niyayate sma |
kiṃca tamindra ⁷yajñaiḥ yajanasādhanaiḥ puroḍāśādibhirhavirbhiḥ sārdhaṃ ⁷havanāni stotrāṇi ⁷satrā saha ⁷vāvṛdhuḥ avardhayan |
tatra dṛṣṭāntaḥ |
⁷dhanvan dhanvani maruddeśe ⁷abhi abhimukhaṃ ⁷saṃ ⁷yat samyagyatyo⁹ gacchantyaḥ ⁷āpaḥ ⁷janaṃ prāṇinamiva |
yathā maruddeśa upalabhyamānā āpaḥ prāṇinaṃ vardha- yanti tadvat ||
asmā etanmahaukṣpamasmā indrāya stotraṃ matibhiravāci |
asadyathā mahati vṛtratūrya indro viśvāyuravitā vṛdhaśca ||
5 ||
asmai | etat | mahai | āṅgupam | asmai m̐indrāya | stotram | mati'bhiḥ | avāci | asat | yathā | mahati | vṛtra'tūrye | indraḥ | viśva'āyuḥ | avitā | vṛdhaḥ | ca || 5 ||
⁷asmai indrāya ⁷mahi mahat ⁷etat ⁷āṅgupaṃ stotraṃ ⁷matibhiḥ stotṛbhiḥ ⁷avāci uktamabhūta |
⁷asmā ⁷indrāya ⁷stotram iti punaruktirādarārthā |
⁷viśvāyuḥ sarvatra gantā saḥ ⁷indraḥ ⁷mahati prabhṛte ⁷vṛtratūrye saṃgrāme ⁷yathā yena prakāreṇāsmākam ⁷avitā rakṣakaḥ ⁷vṛdhaḥ vardhayitā ⁷ca ⁷asat bhavet tathāvācītyanvayaḥ ||
6 ||
‘kadā bhuvana’ iti pañcarcaṃ dvādaśaṃ sūktaṃ narasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamaindram |
tathā cānukrāntaṃ—‘kadā narastu’ iti |
tṛtīye chandome mahāvrate ca viniyoga ukto viniyogaḥ ||
kadā bhuvanarthakṣayāṇi brahma kadā stotre sahasrapoṣyaṃ dāḥ |
kadā stomaṃ vāsayo'sya rāyā kadā dhiyaḥ karasi vājiratnāḥ ||
1 ||
kadā | bhuvana | ratha'kṣayāṇi | brahma | kadā | stotre | sahasra'poṣyam | dāḥ | kadā | stomam | vāsayaḥ | asya | rāyā | kadā | dhiyaḥ | karasi | vāja'ratnāḥ || 1 ||
1. kha-jha-bha- taṃ samyagyantyo; jña-ya-samyagyantyo |
he indra ^brahma brahmāṇi stotrāṇyasmadīyāni ^rathakṣyāṇi rathanivāsāni ^kadā kasminkāle ^bhuvan bhaveyuḥ |
rathe'vasthitaṃ tvāṃ kadā prāpnuvantītyarthaḥ |
^kadā kasmin kāle ^stotre stuvate mahyaṃ ^sahasrapoṣayaṃ sahasrasaṃkhyāpuruṣapoṣakaṃ gosamūhaṃ putraṃ vā ^dāḥ dadyāḥ |
^kadā ca ^asya mama stotuḥ ^stomaṃ stotraṃ ^rāyā dhanena ^vāsayaḥ vyāpayeḥ |
^dhiyaḥ karmāṇi cāmihotrādīni ^vājaratnāḥ vājairatnaiḥ ramaṇīyāḥ ^kadā kasmin kāle ^karasi kuryāḥ |
^evaṃ stotā phalasya vilambamasahamānaḥ ^ indramanayā pṛṣṭavān ||
kahiṃ svitdindra yanṛbhirnṛvīrairnīḻayāse jayājīn | tridhātu gā adhi jayāsi goṣbindra dyumnaṃ sarvvadeṣmasme || 2 || kahiṃ | svit | tat | indra | yat | nṛ'bhiḥ | nṛn | vīraiḥ | vīrān | nīḻayāse | jaya | ājīn | tri'dhātu | gāḥ | adhi | jayāsi | goṣu | indra | dyumnam | svaḥ'vata | dhehi | asme iti || 2 ||
phalasya vilambamasahamāna ṛṣiranayāpīndraṃ pṛcchati |
he ^indra ^kahiṃ ^svita kadā khalu ^tat bhavet |
^nṛbhiḥ asmadīyaiḥ puruṣaiḥ ^nṛn śatrupuruṣān tathā ^vīraiḥ asmadīyaiḥ putraiḥ ^vīrān śatruputrāṃśca ^yat ^nīḻayāse tvaṃ saṃśleṣayeḥ^2 ||
nīḻayatiḥ saṃśleṣaṇakarmā^3 ||
īdṛśaṃ yuddhe yatsaśleṣaṇaṃ tatkadā bhavediti vitarkaḥ |
api cāsmadarthaṃ tvam ^ājīn saṃgrāmān kadā ^jaya jayeḥ |
kadā ca ^goṣu abhigantṛṣu śatruṣu vidyamānāḥ ^tridhātu tridhātuḥ kṣīradadhighṛtānāṃ trayāṇāṃ dhārayitrīḥ ^gāḥ ^adhi ^jayāsi adhikaṃ jayeḥ |
he ^indra tvaṃ ^svarvat vyāptimat ^dyumnaṃ dhanam ^asme asmāsu kadā ^dhehi dhārayeḥ ||
kahiṃ svitdindra yarjaritre viśvapsu brahma kṛṇvaḥ | śaviṣṭha | kadā dhiyo na niyutau yuvāse kadā gomaghā harvanāni gacchāḥ || 3 || kahiṃ | svit | tat | indra | yat | jaritre | viśva'pnu | brahma | kṛṇvaḥ | śaviṣṭha | kadā | dhiyaḥ | na | ni'yutaḥ | yuvāse | kadā | go'maghā | harvanāni | gacchāḥ || 3 ||
anayāpi katicit praśnāḥ kriyante |
he ^indra ^kahiṃ ^svita kadā khalu ^tat bhavet |
he ^śaviṣṭha balavattamendra tvaṃ ^jaritre stotre ^viśvapsu bahuvidharūpaṃ ^brahma annaṃ ^kṛṇavaḥ karopīti ^yat tatkadā bhavediti vitarkaḥ |
^kadā ca ^dhiyaḥ karmāṇi |
^na iti samuccaye |
^niyutaḥ stutīśca ^yuvāse ātmani ^yojayeḥ^4 |
^kadā ca tvaṃ ^gomaghā gomaghāni gavāṃ dātṝṇi ^maṃhaterdānārthasya rūpamuttarapadam ^harvanāni stotrāṇi ^gacchāḥ abhigaccheḥ^5 ||
sa gomaghā jaritre aśvaścandrā vājaśravaso adhi dhehi pṛkṣaḥ | pīpīhīṣaḥ sudughāmindra dhenuṃ bharadvājeṣu surucau rurucyāḥ || 4 || saḥ | go'maghāḥ | jaritre | aśva'candrāḥ | vāja'śravasaḥ | adhi | dhehi | pṛkṣaḥ | pīpihi | iṣaḥ | su'dughām | indra | dhenum | bharat'vājeṣu | su'rucaḥ | rurucyāḥ || 4 ||
1. gha-^masahamānaḥ saḥ; śa-^masahamānaḥ san | 2. gha-saṃhareḥ | 3. kha-na-ma--saṃśleṣakarmā | 4. jha-ta-ya-ātmanā niyojayeḥ | 5. kha-1-ja-jha-na-bha-āgaccheḥ |
he indra vsaḥ tvaṃ vjaritre stotre vgomaghā: gavāṃ dātrī: vaśvaśrandhā: aśvairāhlādayantī: vvāja- śravasa: vājairbalai: prasiddhā evaṃbhūtā: vpṛkṣa: annāni vbharadvājeṣu bharadvājaputreṣvasmāsu vadhi vdhehi dhāraya |
tathā viṣa: tānyannāni vsudughāṃ śobhanadoghnīṃ vdhenuṃ gāṃ ca he vindra vpīpihi pyāyaya |
api ca tā iṣo gauśca vsuruca: śobhanadīptayo yathā bhavanti tathā tvaṃ vruhathyā: dīpaye: |
śobhana- dīsiyuktāśca tā: santvityartha: |
tamā nūnaṃ vṛjanaṃmanyathā ciccharo yacchakra vi duro gṛṇīṣe |
mā niraraṃ śukradudhasya dhenorāṅgirasānbrahmaṇā vipra jinva ||
5 ||
tam | ā | nūnam | vṛjanam | anyathā | cit | śūra: | yat | śakra | vi | dura: | gṛṇīṣe | mā | niḥ | aram | śukra'dudhasya | dheno: | āṅgirasān | brahmaṇā | vipra | jinva || 5 ||
he indra tvaṃ vnūnam adyatanaṃ vvṛjanaṃ bādhakamasmadīyaṃ śatrum vanyathā vcit anyenaiva prakāreṇa yojaya |
yena prakāreṇa sarve jīvanti tadvilakṣaṇaṃ prakāraṃ maraṇākhyaṃ prāpayetyartha: |
ākāra: pūraka: |
he vśakra sarvakāryeṣu śaktendra vśūra: śauryopeta: vvi vdura: viśeṣeṇa dārayitā śatrūṇām |
yadvā |
duro dvārāṇi śatrusambandhīni vighaṭayastvaṃ vyat yasmāt vgṛṇīṣe asmābhi: stūyase tasmāt kāraṇāt vśukra- dudhasya śukrāṇāṃ nirmalānāṃ dogdhrindrasya vdheno: vāca: stotrāt vmā vniraraṃ mā nirgaccheyam |
yadvā |
śukrasya payaso dogdhyā dhenostvayā dattāyā go: sakāśāt mā nirgaccheyam ||
aramityākhyātāt ‘ṛ gatau’ ityasya rūpam ||
sā sarvadāsmāsu nivasadityartha: |
he vvipra prājñendra vāṅgirasān aṅgirogotrajānasmān vbrahmaṇā annena vjinva prīṇaya ||
7 ||
‘satrā madasa:’ iti pañcadaśa trayodaśa sūktaṃ narasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamaindram |
‘satrā’ ityanukrāntam |
pṛṣṭhasya pañcame'hani niṣkevalya etatsūktaṃ śasyam |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘predaṃ brahondro madāya satrā madasa iti niṣkevalyam’ (āśva. śrau. 7. 12) iti |
viśvajiti mādhyandinasavane praśasta: śastra idaṃ sāmasūktamya śaṃsanīyam |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘satrā madaso yo jāta evābhūraika iti sāmasūktāni’ (āśva. śrau. 8. 7) iti |
mahāvrate'pi niṣkevalya ukto viniyoga: ||
satrā madasastvaṃ viśvajanyā: satrā rāyo'dha ye pārthivāsa: |
satrā vājānāmabhava vibhaktā yadeveṣu dhārayathā asuryam ||
1 ||
satrā | madasa: | tava | viśva'janyā: | satrā | rāya: | adha | ye | pārthivāsa: | satrā | vājānām | abhava: | vibhaktā | yat | deveṣu | dhārayathā: | asuryam || 1 ||
he indra vtava svadīyā: vmadāsa: somapānajanitā madā: | vsatrā | satyanāmaiṭatat | satyaṃ vviśvajanyā: sarvajanahitā bhavanti | yadvā | satreti mahannāma | mahāntastva madā iti yojyam | vadha api ca vpārthivāsa: pṛthivyupalakṣite lokatraye bhavāstvadīyā: vye vrāya: dhanasamūhā: santi te'pi
1. kha-jha-tara-na-bha-ra-anūnam |
2. kha-gha-jha-ta1-na-bha-ra-śa- ‘śasyaṃ’ nāsti; jha-śaṃstyam |
3. ta- mahāvrate |
ᐯsatrā satyaṃ viśvajanahitā bhavantītyanvayaḥ |
kiṃca tvaṃ ᐯvājānām annānāṃ ᐯsatrā satyaṃ ᐯvibhaktā dātā ᐯabhavaḥ bhavasi |
yadvā |
satrā mahatāṃ vājānāmiti yojyam |
ᐯyat yasmāttvaṃ ᐯdeveṣu madhye ᐯasuryaṃ balaṃ ᐯdhārayathāḥ ataḥ kāraṇatvameva karoṣītyarthaḥ |
anu pra yeṃje jan ojau asya satrā dadhire anu vīryam | syumagṛbhe dudhaye'rvate ca kratuṃ vṛñjantyapi vṛtrahatyai || 2 || anu | pra | yeṃje | jane | ojaḥ | asya | satrā | dadhire | anu | vīryam | syumagṛbhe | dudhaye | arvate | ca | kratum | vṛñjanti | api | vṛtrahatyai || 2 ||
ᐯasya indrasya ᐯojaḥ balaṃ ᐯjanaḥ yajamānaḥ ᐯanu ᐯpra yeṃje saṃtataṃ prakarṣeṇa yajate pūjayati |
api ca ta
indrasya bahunāmāhādakasya ᵥvasvaḥ nivāsakasya |
api ca ᵥasamaḥ anupamaḥ ᵥsarvokṛṣṭatvaṃ ᵥjanānāṃ sarveṣāṃ prāṇināṃ ᵥpatiḥ adhipataḥ ᵥbabhūthaᵥ |
tathā ᵥviśvasya sarvasya ᵥbhuvanasya bhūtajātasya ᵥekaḥ asādhāraṇo ᵥrājā ᵥīśvaraśca babhūvithaᵥ ||
sa tu śṛdhi śrutyā yo duvaudyaurna bhūmābhi rāyo aryaḥ |
amo yathā naḥ śavasā cakāno yugeyuge vayamā cekitānaḥ ||
5 ||
maḥ | tu | śṛdhi | śrutyā | yaḥ | duvaḥ'yuḥ | dhyauḥ | na | bhūma | abhi | rāyaḥ | aryaḥ | armaḥ | yathā | naḥ | śavasā | cakānaḥ | yuge'yuge | vayasā | cekitānaḥ || 5 ||
he indra ᵥśrutyā śrutyāni śrotavyāni stotrāṇi ᵥtu kṣipraṃ ᵥśṛdhi śṛṇu |
ᵥyaḥ tvaṃ ᵥduvauyuḥ aham- dīyaṃ paricaraṇamātmana icchan ᵥaryaḥ areḥ saṃbandhini ᵥrāyaḥ dhanāni ᵥbhūma bahutarāṇi ᵥdyaurna sūrya iva ᵥabhi bhavasi ᵥsaḥ tvaṃ śṛdhītyanvayaḥ |
api ca ᵥśavasā ātmīyena balena stotavyena yuktaḥ ᵥcakānaḥ stutyamānaḥ ᵥyugeyuge kāle kāle ᵥvayasā havirlakṣaṇenānnena ᵥcekitānaḥ prajñāyamānastvaṃ ᵥyathā yena prakāreṇa ᵥnaḥ asmākamasādhāraṇo'si tathā ᵥasaḥ syāḥ ||
8 ||
‘ arvāgratham ’ iti pañcarcaṃ caturthaṃ sūktaṃ bharadvājasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamaindram |
‘ arvāgratham ’ ityanuktvā- ntam |
mahāvrate nikevalye ukto viniyogaḥ ||
arvāgrathaṃ viśvavāraṃ ta ugrendra yuktāso harayo vahantu |
kīriśriddhi tvā havate svarvānṛdhīmahi sadhamādeste adya ||
1 ||
arvāk | ratham | viśva'vāram | te | ugra | indra | yuktāsaḥ | harayaḥ | vahantu | kīriḥ | cit | hi | tvā | havate | svaḥ'vān | ṛdhīmahi | sadha'mādaiḥ | te | adya || 1 ||
he ᵥugra udbhaṇabala ᵥindra ᵥyuktāmaḥ rathena yuktāḥ ᵥharayaḥ aśvāḥ ᵥte tvadīyaṃ ᵥviśvavāraṃ viśva- caraṇīyaṃ ᵥratham ᵥarvāk asmadabhimukhaṃ ᵥvahantu ānayantu |
ᵥhi yasmāt ᵥsvarvān ᵥsvaḥ suṣṭhu araṇīyaṃ guṇajātam |
tadvān ᵥkīriśrit stotā bharadvāja ṛṣiḥ ᵥtvā tvāṃ ᵥhavate āhvayati tasmāt kāraṇāt tvadīyaṃ rathamimukhaṃ vahantvityarthaḥ |
vayaṃ ca ᵥadya idānīṃ ᵥte tvayā ᵥsadhamādaḥ saha mādyantaḥ ᵥṛdhī- mahi samṛddhaḥ bhavema ||
tṛtīye paryāye'cchāvākasya ‘ pro droṇe ’ ityeṣā śastrājyā |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘ pro droṇe harayaḥ karmāmpanityā jyā ’ ( āśva. śrau. 6. 4 ) iti ||
pro droṇe harayaḥ karmāmpanunām ṛjyanto abhūvan |
indro no asya pūrvyaḥ papīyāddukṣo madasya somyasya rājā ||
2 ||
pro | droṇe | harayaḥ | karman | agman | punānāsaḥ | ṛjyantaḥ | abhūvan | indraḥ | naḥ | asya | pūrvyaḥ | papīyāt | dukṣaḥ | madasya | somyasya | rājā || 2 ||
1. jha-babhūtha babhūvitha |
2. jha-babhūtha |
^haraya: haritavarṇā: somā: ^na: ^karma asmadīyaṃ yajñaṃ ^pro ^agman prakarṣeṇa gacchanti prāpnuvanti |
prāpya ca ^punānāma: pūyamānā: ^droṇe droṇakalaśe ^ṛjyantaḥ ṛju gacchantaḥ ^abhūvan bhavanti |
^pūrtha: purātana: ^dhukṣa: dīptinivāsabhūto dhusthāno vā ^madasya madakarasya ^somasya somasya ^rājā īśvara evaṃ- bhūta: ^indra: ca ^asya imaṃ somaṃ ^papīyāt pibatu ||
āsannāsa: śabasānamacchendraṃ sucakre rathyāso aśvā: |
abhi śrava ṛjyanto vaheyurnu citru vāyoramṛtaṃ vi dasyeta ||
3 ||
ā'sannāsa: | śabasānam | accha | indram | su'cakre | rathyāṃsa: | aśvā: | abhi | śrava: | ṛjyantaḥ | vaheyu: | nu | citru | nu | vāyo: | amṛtam | vi | dasyeta || 3 ||
^āsannāsa: parito gacchantaḥ ^rathyāsa: rathe yuktā: ^aśvā: ^ṛjyantaḥ ṛjugamanā: mantaḥ ^sucakre śobhanacakre rathe'vasthitaṃ ^śabasānaṃ balamivācarantam ^indra ^śrava: śravaṇīyamasmadīyaṃ havi: ^accha ābhimukhyena ^abhi ^vaheyu: āvaheyu: |
nṛ cin iti nipāto niṣedhe'tra vartate |
^amṛtam amaraṇahetubhūtaṃ somalakṣaṇaṃ havi: ^vāyo: heto: ^nu idānīṃ ^nu ^cit naiva ^vi ^dasyena kṣayaṃ na prāpnu- yāt |
pātre gṛhītaḥ somo vāyunā duṣyati tataḥ puraiva tahānārthamaśvā indramabhiheyurityartha: ||
bariṣṭho asya dakṣiṇāmiyarttīndro maghonāṃ tuvikūrmitama: |
yayā vanniva: pariyāsyahau maghā ca dhṛṣṇo dayase vi sūrīn ||
4 ||
bariṣṭha: | asya | dakṣiṇām | iyarti | indra: | maghonām | tuvikūrmitama: | yayā | vanniva: | pari'yāsi | aha: | maghā | ca | dhṛṣṇo | dayase | vi | sūrīn || 4 ||
^variṣṭha: urutama: ^tuvikūrmitama: atiśayena bahuvidhakarmā ^indraṃ ^maghonāṃ maghavatāṃ havirlakṣaṇadhanāpetānāṃ madhye ^asya yajamānasya ^dakṣiṇāṃ yajñe dātavyām ^iyarti prerayati |
yadvā |
maghonāmiti asyatyanena samānādhikaraṇam ||
vacanavyatyaya: ||
maghonāṃ haviṣmatā'sya yajamānasyetyartha: |
śeṣa: pratyakṣakṛta: |
he ^vanniva: vajravacchindra ^yayā dakṣiṇayā datayā ^aha: pāpaṃ yajñamabandha ^pari- yāsi vināśayasi he ^dhṛṣṇo dharṣaka ^maghā maghāni dhanāni ^sūrīn stotṛn putrāṃśca yayā ca dakṣiṇayā ^vi ^dayase prayacchasi tādṛśīṃ dakṣiṇāṃ prerayasītyartha: ||
indro vājasya sthavirasya dātendro gīrbhirvardhatāṃ vṛddhamahā: |
indro vṛtraṃ haniṣṭho astu satvā tā sūri: pṛṇati tūtajāna: ||
5 ||
indra: | vājasya | sthavirasya | dātā | indra: | gī:'bhi: | vardhatām | vṛddhamahā: | indra: | vṛtram | haniṣṭha: | astu | matvā | ā | tā | sūri: | pṛṇati | tūtajāna: || 5 ||
ayam ^indra: ^sthavirasya ^vājasya annasya balasya vā ^dātā bhavatu |
sa ca ^indra: ^vṛddha- mahā: pravṛddhatejaska: san ^gīrbhi: asmadīyābhi: stutibhi: ^vardhatām |
^matvā śatrūṇāṃ śātayitā 1. jña-sthavirasya śreṣṭhasya |
sa: vindra: vvṛtram āvarakaṃ śatruṃ vhaniṣṭa: hantutama: vastu bhavatu |
api ca vsūri: preraka: sa indra: vtutujāna: tvaramāṇa: vtā tāni dhanāni vā 'pṛṇati asmabhyaṃ prayacchatu ||
9 ||
'apādita: ' iti pañcarcaṃ pañcadaśaṃ sūktaṃ bharadvājasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamaindram |
'apāt ' ityanukrāntam |
mahāyate niṣkevalya ukto viniyoga: ||
apādita udu naścitratamaṃ mahīṃ bharṣadyumatīmindrahūtim | paryanyasīṃ dhītiṃ daivyasya yāmyarjanasya rātiṃ vanate sudānu: || 1 || apāt | ita: | ut | ūṃ iti | na: | citra'tama: | mahīm | bharṣat | ghu'matīm | indra'hūtim | paryanyasīm | dhītim | daivyasya | yāman | janasya | rātim | vanate | su'dānu: || 1 ||
vcitratama: cāyanīyatama āścaryatamo vā sa indra: vna: asmadīyāt vitaḥ asmāt grahacama- sāde: vapāt somaṃ pibatu |
yadvā |
ito'smāt śatruno'smān apāt pātu rakṣatu |
vudu ityetau pūrṇau |
tathā vmahīṃ mahatīṃ vdyumatīṃ dīptimatīm vindrahūtim indrasya stutiṃ vbharṣat sa indro bibhartu dhārayatu |
vdaivyasya devasambandhino vjanasya vyāman yāmani yajñe kriyamāṇāṃ vparyanyasīṃ stutyatamāṃ vdhītiṃ pari- caraṇarūpāṃ kriyāṃ vrātiṃ dātavyaṃ haviśva vsudānu: śobhanadāna indra: vvanate saṃbhajatu ||
dūrāccidā vaṃsato asya karṇā ghoṣādindrasya tanyati bruvāṇa: | eyamenaṃ devahūtirvṛtyānumnr gindraṃmiyamucyamānā || 2 || durāt | cit | ā | vamata: | asya | karṇī | ghoṣāt | indrasya | tanyati | bruvāṇa: | ā | iyam | enam | deva'hūti: | vṛtyāt | madyak | indram | iyam | ṛcyamānā || 2 ||
vasya indrasya vkarṇā karṇau vdūrāccit dūradeśādapi vā vvasata: stotraśravaṇārthamāgacchataḥ |
vindrasya vghoṣāt ghoṣaṇīyāt stotrāddheto: vbhuvāṇa: stuvan stotā vtanyati śabdaṃ karoti |
yadvā |
sarvatra vistārayati |
vdevahūti: devasyendrasyāhvānarūpā viyaṃ stutiśca vṛcyamānā svayaṃ preryamāṇā satī venam vindram vmadyak madabhimukham vā vvavṛtyāt āvartayatu |
puna: viyam iti pūrṇa: ||
taṃ vo dhiyā paramayā purājāmajaramindraṃmabhyanūṣakai: | brahmā ca giroṃ dadhire samasminmahāṃśa stomo adhi vardhadindre || 3 || tam | va: | dhiyā | paramayā | purā'jām | ajam | indram | abhi | anuṣi | akeṃ: | brahma | ca | gira: | dadhire | sam | asmin | mahān | ca | stoma: | adhi | vardhat | indrai || 3 ||
he indra vtaṃ tādṛśam vindraṃ vva: tvām ||
vyatyayena bahuvacanam ||
vparamayā utkṛṣṭatamayā vdhiyā stutyā varkai: arcanasādhanairvibhi: sārdham vabhyanūṣi abhiṭaumi |
kīdṛśam |
vpurājāṃ pūrvasmin kāle jātaṃ ciraṃtanam vajaraṃ jarārahitaṃ nityam |
ataḥ kāraṇāt vasmin vindre vbrahma 1. kha-bha-nityamindramaiśvaryavantam |
mahābhi havirlakṣaṇanyāti gira: stutayaśca saṃ dadhire saṃdhīyante saṃśliṣyante |
mahān pravṛddhaḥ stoma: ca adhi vardhat adhikaṃ vardhate ||
vardhāghaṃ yajña uta soma indraṃ vardhādbrahma giraṃ ukthā ca manmaṃ | vardhāhainamupaso yāmanktorvardhanmāsāṃ: śarado dyāva indram || 4 || vardhāt | yam | yajñaḥ | uta | somaḥ | indram | vardhāt | brahma | giraḥ | ukthā | ca | manmaṃ | vardhai | aha | enam | uṣasaḥ | yāman | akteḥ | vardhan | māsāḥ | śaradaḥ | dyāvaḥ | indram ||4||
yam indraṃ yajña: yāgaḥ vardhāt vardhayati |
uta api ca tatsādhanabhūtaḥ soma: ca yamindraṃ vardhayati |
tathā brahma havirlakṣaṇamannaṃ ca puroḍāśādikaṃ yamindraṃ vardhāt vardhayati |
gira: stotrāṇi manma mananīyāni ukthā ukthāni śastrāṇi ca yaṃ vardhayanti enam indram uṣasa: ca akto: rātreḥ yāman yāmani gamane sati vardha' vardhayanti |
māsā: śarada: saṃvatsarāśca dyāva: divasāścainam indraṃ vardhān vardhayanti ||
evā jajñānaṃ sahase asāmi vāvṛdhānaṃ rādhase ca śrutāyaṃ | mahāmugramavase vipra nūnamā vivāsema vṛtratūryeṣu || 5 || eva | jajñānam | sahase | asāmi | vavṛdhānam | rādhase | ca | śrutāyaṃ | mahām | ugram | avase | vipra | nūnam | ā | vivāsema | vṛtra'tūryeṣu || 5 ||
he vipra medhāvinnindra eva evaṃ jajñānaṃ prādurbhavantaṃ sahase śatrūṇāmabhibhavārtham asāmi bahulaṃ vāvṛdhānaṃ vardhamānaṃ mahāma mahāntam ugram udgūrṇabalaṃ tvāṃ nūnam atha vṛtratūryeṣu śrutāya viśrutāya rādhase dhanāya ca avase rakṣaṇāya ca ā vivāsema paricarema ||
10||
‘ mandrasya kave: ’ iti pañcacaṃ ṣoḍaśaṃ sūktaṃ bharadvājasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamaindram |
‘ mandrasya ’ ityanukrāntam |
gato viniyoga: ||
mandrasya kaverdivyasya vahnervipramanmano vacanasya madhvaḥ | apā ntastasya sacanasya deveṣo yuvasva gṛṇate goaṃgrā: || 1 || mandrasya | kaveḥ | divyasya | vahneḥ | vipre'manmanaḥ | vacanasya | madhvaḥ | apāḥ | na | tasya | sacanasya | deva | īṣaḥ | yuvasva | gṛṇate | go'aṃgrāḥ || 1 ||
mandrasya modanasya madakarasya vā kave: vikrāntasya divyasya divi bhavasya vahne: voḍhuḥ vipra manmana: |
viprā medhāvino manmānaḥ stotāro yasya sa tathoktaḥ |
tasya vacanasya vacanīyasya stutyasya
1. kha1-ma-vardhaya āvardhayanti; jha-jha-na-vardhā vardhayanti; tara-vardhā vardhayaṃsi |
2. kha1-gha-ja-ṇa- bha-vardhanti aha (kha1-bha-ahaḥ ) pūraṇaḥ |
3. kha-gha-bha-asāmi saṃpūrṇam |
4. gha-vavṛdhānam |
5. gha-vṛtratūryeṣu raṇepu; jha-śa-vṛtratūryeṣu saṃgrāmeṣu |
6. kha-jha-ta-na-bha-ra-‘ śrutāya ’ nāsti |

^tasya sarvatra prasiddhasya ^sacanasya sevyasya^ evaṃbhūtasya ^madhvaḥ madhunaḥ somasya |

apa oṣadhīrviṣā vanāni gā arveto nṛncasai ririhe ||

nu | gṛṇānaḥ | gṛṇate | pratna | rājan | iṣaḥ | pinva | vasudeyāya | pūrvīḥ | apaḥ | oṣadhīḥ | avipā | vanāni | gāḥ | arvataḥ | nṛn | ṛcase | ririhe || 5 ||

‘indra piba’ iti pañcadaṃ saptadaśaṃ sūktaṃ bharadvājasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamaindram |
‘indra piba’ itya- nukrāntam |
gataḥ sūktaviniyogaḥ |
prathame rātriparyāye'cchāvākasyādyā^ śastrāyā^ |

indra piba tubhyaṃ suto madāyārba sya harī vi mucā sakhāya |
uta pra gāya guṇa ā niṣadyāthā yajñārya gṛṇate vayo dhāḥ ||
1 ||
indra | piba | tubhyam | sutaḥ | madāya | arve | sya | harī | vi | muñca | sakhāya | uta | pra | gāya | guṇe | ā | ni'sadha | artha | yajñārya | gṛṇate | vayaḥ | dhāḥ || 1 ||

tadanantaraṃ ^vi ^muca rathādvisraja |

asya piba yasya jajñāna indra madāya ṛtve arpibo virapśin | tamu te gāvo nara āpo adririnduṃ samahanpītaye samasmai || 2 || asya | piba | yasya | jajñānaḥ | indra | madāya | ṛtve | arpibaḥ | virapśin | tam | ū~ iti | te | gāvaḥ | naraḥ | āpaḥ | adriḥ | indum | sam | ahan | pītaye | sam | asmai ||2||

kimartham |

'hi gatau' ityasyaitadrūpam |

samiddhe agnau suta indra soma ā tvā vahantu harayo vahiṣṭāḥ | tvāyatā manasā johavīmīndrā yāhi suvitārya mahe naḥ || 3 || sam'iddhe | agnau | sute | indra | somam | ā | tvā | vahantu | harayaḥ | vahiṣṭāḥ | tvā'yatā | manasā | johavīmi | indra | ā | yāhi | suvitārya | mahe | naḥ || 3 ||

imaṃ yajñaṃ prīyantyantu |

he indra 'vṛṣṇe yūne nityataruṇāya 'vṛṣabhāya kāmānāṃ varṣayitre 'tubhyaṃ 'vṛṣabhiḥ kāmābhivarṣakairgāvābhiḥ 3 'sutānām abhiṣutānāṃ somānāṃ madhye tvadāgarūpaṃ 'somaṃ yasmādadhvaryavaḥ 'pra 'bharanti praharanti homārthaṃ praṇayanti tasmādāgacchetyarthaḥ ||
19 ||
vṛṣāsi divo vṛṣabhaḥ pṛthivyā vṛṣā sindhūnāṃ vṛṣabhaḥ stiyānām | vṛṣṇe ta indurvibhaya pīpāya svādū raso madhupeyo varāya || 21 || vṛṣā | asi | divaḥ | vṛṣabhaḥ | pṛthivyāḥ | vṛṣā | sindhūnām | vṛṣabhaḥ | stiyānām | vṛṣṇe | te | induḥ | vṛṣabha | pīpāya | svāduḥ | rasaḥ | madhu'peyaḥ | varāya || 21 ||
he indra tvaṃ 'divaḥ dyulokasya 'vṛṣāsi |
havirbhiḥ sektā bhavasi |
'pṛthivyāḥ bhūmeśca 'vṛṣabhaḥ kāmānāṃ varṣitā |
'sindhūnāṃ syandanaśīlānāṃ ca nadīnāṃ 'vṛṣā varṣaṇa pūrayitāsi |
tathā 'stiyānāṃ saṃghībhūtānāṃ sthāvarajaṅgamātmanāṃ prāṇināṃ 'vṛṣabhaḥ kāmānāṃ varṣitā bhavasi |
yadvā |

tāsāṃ varṣitāsi |

ayaṃ devaḥ sahasā jāyamāna indreṇa yujā puṇarmastabhāyat |
ayaṃ svasya pitarāyudhāninduramuṣṇātaśivasya māyāḥ ||
22 ||
ayam | devaḥ | sahasā | jāyamānaḥ | indreṇa | yujā | puṇam | astabhāyat | ayam | svasya | pituḥ | āyudhāni | induḥ | amuṣṇāta | aśivasya | māyāḥ || 22 ||
devaḥ dyotamānaḥ yaayama induḥ somaḥ indreṇa yujā sakhyā saha jāyamānaḥ prādurbhavan paṇaṃ vaṇijaṃ gavāmādātāraṃ valākhyamasuram |

api ca svasya dhanasya gorūpasya pituḥ pālayituḥ aśivasya asukhakaramya valasya āyudhāni vajrādīni amuṣṇāta apāharat |
tadīyāḥ māyāḥ ca amuṣṇāta |
he indra tvarasyabalādetasarvaṃ somaḥ kṛtavān atastatra mahimā kena varṇayituṃ śakyata itīndrastutiḥ ||

ayaṃ tridhātuṃ divi rocaneṣu triteṣu vindadamṛtaṃ nigūḻaham ||
23 ||
ayam | akṛṇot | upasaḥ | su'patnīḥ | ayam | sūrye | adhāt | jyotiḥ | antaḥ | ayam | tri'dhātum | divi | rocaneṣu | triteṣu | vindat | amṛtam | ni'gūḻaham || 23 ||
ayaṃ somaḥ upasaḥ upaḥkālān supatnīḥ śobhanaḥ patiḥ pālakaḥ sūryo yāsāmupasāṃ tādaśīḥ akṛṇot akarot |
ayam sūrye sūryamaṇḍale antaḥ madhye jyotiḥ tejaḥ adhāt nihitavān |
tridhātuṃ savanatrayarūpeṇa triprakāram ayaṃ latārūpaḥ somaḥ divi dyuloke rocaneṣu rocamāneṣu triteṣu tṛtīye sthāne teṣu vistṛteṣu deveṣu nigūḻahaṃ nitarāṃ gūḍhamadṛśyatayā vartamānam amṛtam amṛtatvakāraṇaṃ pīyūṣaṃ vindat avinadat alabhata |

idaṃśadanenendra eva parāmṛśyate |

ayameva sūrye'ntaryoniradadhāt |

ayaṃ dyāvāpṛthivī vi ṣkabhāyadayaṃ rathamayunaksasrimam |

indra jyeṣṭhaṃ na ā bhara ojiṣṭhaṃ parpuri śravaḥ | yeneme citra vajrahasta rodasī obhe suśiprāḥ || 5 || indra | jyeṣṭham | naḥ | ā | bhara | ojiṣṭham | parpuri | śravaḥ | yene | ime iti | citra | vajra'hasta | rodasī | ine | ā | ubhe iti | su'śiprāḥ | prāḥ ||5||
he indra jyeṣṭhaṃ praśastatamam ojiṣṭham ojasvitamarutiśayena balakaraṃ parpuri pūrakaṃ śravaḥ annaṃ naḥ asmabhyam ā bhara āhara prayaccha |
he citra cāyanīya vajrahasta vajrabāho he suśiprā śobhanahanuka evaṃbhūta he indra yena annena ime paridṛśyamāne ubhe rodasī dyāvāpṛthivyau ā prāḥ āpūryasi tadannamā bharetyanvayaḥ ||
27 ||
tvāmugramarvase carṣaṇīsahaṃ rājaṃ.deveṣu hūmahe | viśvā su nau vidhurā pibdanā vaso'mitrāntsupahānkṛdhi || 6 || tvām | ugram | arvase | carṣaṇi'saham | rājan | deveṣu | hūmahe | viśvā | su | naḥ | vidhurā | pibdanā | vaso iti | āmrtrān | su'sahān | kṛdhi || 6 ||
he rājan rājamānendra deveṣu devānāṃ madhye ugram udbhaṭabalaṃ carṣaṇīsahaṃ carṣaṇīnāṃ śatrubhūtānāṃ prajānāmabhibhavitāraṃ tvām avase rakṣaṇāya hūmahe āhvayāmaḥ |
tvaṃ viśvā sarvāṇi pibdanā pibdanāni rakṣāṃsi |
pihitamavyaktaṃ śabdayanta iti pibdanāni |
pṛtanādiḥ |
tāni su suṣṭhu vidhurā vyathitāni bādhitāni kuru |
he vaso vāsukendra naḥ asmadīyān amitrān śatrūn susahān sukhena|
bhibhavituṃ śakyān kṛdhi kuru ||
yadindra nāhūṣaṣvā ojau nṛmṇaṃ ca kṛdhiṣu | yadvā pañca kṣitīnāṃ yumnamā bhara sūtrā viśvāni pausyā || 7 || yat | indra | nāhūṣeṣu | ā | ojaḥ | nṛmṇam | ca | kṛdhiṣu | yat | vā | pañca | kṣitīnām | yumnam | ā | bhara | sūtrā | viśvāni | pausyā || 7 ||
he indra nāhūṣeṣu nahupa iti manuṣyanāma |
tatsambandhinīṣu kṛdhiṣu prajāsu |
ākāraḥ samuccaye |
yat ca ojaḥ balaṃ nṛmṇaṃ dhanaṃ ca vidyate |
yadvā yacca pañca pañcānāṃ kṣitīnām |
niṣāda- pañcamāścatvāro varṇāḥ pañca kṣitayaḥ |
teṣāṃ svaṃbhūtaṃ yumnaṃ dyotamānamannaṃ tat sarvamasmabhyam ā bhara āhara prayaccha |
tathā satrā mahānti viśvāni sarvāṇi pausyā balāni cāsmabhyamāhara ||
1. kha-jha-na-bha-ra-āhvayāmahe |
2. śa-pausyā pausyāni |
3. kha-jha-na-bha-mābhara |
yadvā ttukṣau maghavandṛhāvā jane yatpaurau kacca vṛṣṇyam | asmabhyaṃ tadrirīhi saṃ nṛpāhe'mitrāṃtpṛtsu turvaṇe || 8 || yat | vā | ttukṣau | maghavan | druhau | ā | jane | yat | paurau | kat | ca | vṛṣṇyam | asmabhyam | tat | rirīhi | sam | nṛ'sahau | amitrān | pṛtsu | turvaṇe || 8 || he maghavan dhanavannindra ttukṣau etatsaṃjñe rājana yadvā yacca balaṃ vidyate druhau ā duhau caitatsaṃjñe ca jane yat balamasti | paurau etatsaṃjñe nṛpe ca yat kacca yatkiṃcana vṛṣṇyaṃ vīrya vidyate tat sarvam asmabhyaṃ stotṛbhyaḥ saṃ samyak rirīhi prayaccha | kadā | nṛsahe nṛbhiryoddhṛbhiḥ soḍhavye yuddhe pravṛtte | kimartham | pṛtsu pṛtanāsu saṃgrāmeṣu amitrān śatrūn turvaṇe turvituṃ hiṃsitum || cāturviṃśike'hani nikevalye 'indra tridhātu' iti vairūpasya sāmapragāthaḥ śaṃsanīyaḥ | sūtritaṃ ca—'indra tridhātu śaraṇaṃ tvamindra pratittiṣu' (āśva. śrau. 7. 3) iti ||
indraṃ tridhātuṃ śaraṇaṃ trivarūthaṃ svastimata | chardiryaccha maghavadbhyaśca mahaṃ ca yāvayā dvidhumyaḥ || 9 || indra | tri'dhātu | śaraṇam | tri'varūtham | svasti'mata | chardiḥ | yaccha | maghavat'bhyaḥ | ca | maham | ca | yavaya | dvidhum | ebhyaḥ || 9 || he indra tridhātu triprakāraṃ tribhūmikaṃ trivarūthaṃ trayāṇāṃ śītatāpavarṣāṇāṃ vārakaṃ svastimata avināśayuktaṃ chardiḥ chadiṣmat ācchādanayuktamevaṃguṇaviśiṣṭaṃ śaraṇaṃ gṛhaṃ maghavadbhyaśca | maghaṃ havirlakṣaṇaṃ dhanam | tadbhyaścāsmadīyebhyaḥ mahaṃ bharadvājāya ca yaccha dehi | api ca ebhyaḥ sakāśāt dvidhuṃ śatrupreritaṃ dyotamānāyudhaṃ yavaya pṛthak kuru ||
ye gavyatā manasā śatrumādurabhipraghnanti dhṛṣṇuyā | ardha smā no maghavannindra girvaṇastanūpā antamo bhava || 10 || ye | gavyatā | manasā | śatrūm | ā'duḥ | abhi'praghnanti | dhṛṣṇu'yā | ardha | sma | naḥ | maghavan | indra | girvaṇaḥ | tanū'pāḥ | antam | bhava || 10 || ye janāḥ gavyatā śatrūṇāṃ gā icchatā manasā yuktāḥ santaḥ śatruṃ śātayitāraṃ vairiṇam āduḥ aṃhisiṣuḥ | ye ca dhṛṣṇuyā dhṛṣṇvo dhṛṣṭāḥ santaḥ asmānabhilakṣya praghnanti prakarṣeṇa hiṃsanti he maghavan dhanavan girvaṇaḥ girāṃ saṃbhaktaḥ indra adha sma asmin kāle tebhyaḥ sakāśāt naḥ asmākaṃ tanūpāḥ tanūnāṃ śarīrāṇāṃ rakṣitā san antam antikatamaḥ bhava || 2 ||
1. jha-ta-nṛsahe |
2. kha-jha-na-bha-chardiṣmat |
3. ra-yāvaya |

ṛgveda:
sindhuriva pravaṇa āśuyā yato yadi krośamanu dhvani | ā ye vayo na vartulyāmiṣi gṛbhītā bahogarvivaṃ || 14 || sindhūna'iva | pravaṇe | āśuyā | yataḥ | yadi | krośam | anu | dhvani | ā | ye | vayeḥ | na | vartulati | āmiṣi | gṛbhītāḥ | bāhoḥ | gatri || 14 ||
uktasyaivārthasyātiśayapratipādanārthayam |
ᐁpravaṇe nimnadeśe ᐂsindhūniva syandamānā nadīriva ᐃāśuyā āśu śīghraṃ ᐄyataḥ gacchataḥ'iva he indra ᐅyadi codyāse prerayasi' |
kadā |
ᐆkrośanu |
krośa iti bhayanāma |
bhayamalakṣya ᐇsvani sve ākrośe sati |
kīdṛśāste adhvāḥ |
ᐈbāhoḥ bāhumūlayoḥ ᐉgṛbhītāḥ kakṣyayā baddhāḥ ᐊye aśvāḥ ᐋgavi gorupe dhane jetavye sati ᐌā vartulati punaḥparāvartante |
ᐍāmiṣi āmiṣe māṃse grahītavye sati ᐎvayo ᐏna yathā śyenādayaḥ pakṣiṇaḥ punaḥpurāvartante tadvat |
evaṃvidhānbhayena krośe sati yadā tvaṃ prerayasi tadānīmasmadīyebhyaśchardiḥ prayaccheti saṃbandhaḥ |
yadapi ṛgvede prasaktirnāsti ᐐtathāpi rājapurohitatvena rājakīyānāṃ puruṣāṇāṃ jayaḥ prārthyata iti na kaścidvirodhaḥ ||
23 ||
'svāduḥ' ityevatriṃśacaṃ caturthaṃ sūktaṃ bharadvājaputrasya gargasyārṣam |
'prastoka ikṣu' ityeṣā triṣṭup, 'daśāśvān' ityanuṣṭup, 'daśa rathān' iti gāyatrī, 'mahi rādhaḥ' iti dvipadā dvekā- daśakā, 'yujāno haritā' ityeṣā bṛhatī, 'divaspṛthivyāḥ' iti jagatī śiṣṭāstriṣubhaḥ |
āditaḥ pañcānāṃmṛcāṃ somo devatā |
'agavyūti kṣetram' ityardhacardhaprathamapāde devā uttarasya ṛksidvitīyasya bṛhaspatiścaturthasyendraḥ |
'prastokaḥ' ityādyāścatasraḥ sṛñjayaputrasya rājño dānastutirūpāt tadevatākāḥ |
'vanaspate vīddhaḥ' ityayaṃ tṛco rathadevatyaḥ |
'upa śvāsaya' iti tṛco dundubhidevatyaḥ |
'samaśvaparṇāḥ' ityardharcaṃ aindraḥ |
śiṣṭā indradevatākāḥ |
tathā cānukrāntaṃ—'svāduhrekatriṃśadṛgeḥ pañcādau sāṃmyo'gadyuyardharco liṅgoktadevataḥ prastoka iti triṣṭupnuṣṭupgāyatrī dvipadā sāṃjyasya prastokasya dānastutiḥ parau tṛcau rathadundubhidevatye'nyau'rdharcau yujāno bṛhatī triṣṭumaha dvipadā divaspṛthivyā jagatī' iti |
āgnimāruta ādidaśatrasaḥ śaṃsanīyāḥ |
sūtritaṃ ca—'svāduṣkilāyamiti catasro madhye cāhvānam' (āśva. śrau. 5. 20) iti ||
svāduṣkilāyaṃ madhumā~ utāyaṃ tīvraḥ kilāyaṃ rasā~ utāyam | uto nva'sya paviāṃsamindraṃ na kaścana sahata āhaveṣu || 1 || svāduḥ | kila | ayam | madhumān | uta | ayam | tīvraḥ | kila | ayam | rasavān | uta | ayam | uto iti | nu | asya | paviāṃsam | indram | na | kaḥ | cana | sahate | āhaveṣu || 1 ||
ᐁayam abhipūtaḥ somaḥ ᐂsvāduḥ āsvādanīyaḥ ᐃkila bhavati |
ᐄuta api ca ᐅmadhumān mādhuryāṃśca ayaṃ somo bhavati |
tathā ᐆayaṃ somaḥ ᐇtīvraḥ ᐈkila madotpādane tīkṣṇaḥ khalu bhavati |
ᐉuta api ca ᐊayaṃ somaḥ ᐋramavān sāravāṃśca bhavati |
anena vākyacatuṣṭayena somasya mādhuryātiśayatvaṃ ca pratipāditam ᐌ |
ᐍuto api ca ᐎasya somasya |
dvitīyārthe ṣaṣṭhī |
imaṃ somaṃ ᐏpaviāṃsaṃ pītavantam ᐐindram ᐑāhaveṣu saṃgrāmeṣu ᐒna ᐓkaścana na ko'pi ᐔsahate abhibhavati |
ᐕnu iti pūrakaḥ ||
1. kha-jha-jña-ta-na-bha-ra-prerayeḥ | śa-prerayase | 2. kha-ᐆryudraprasaktiḥ | 3. ta-pratipādyate |
ayaṃ svāduriha madiṣṭha āsa yasyendrau vṛtrahatyai mamāda | purūṇi yaścyautnā śambarasya vi narvārti nava ca dehyo' han || 2 || ayam | svāduḥ | iha | madiṣṭhaḥ | āsa | yasya | indraḥ | vṛtra'hatyai | mamāda | purūṇi | yaḥ | cyautnā | śambarasya | vi | narvārtim | nava | ca | dehyaḥ | han || 2 ||
iha asmin yajñe ayaṃ somaḥ svāduḥ āsvāditaḥ pītaḥ san madiṣṭhaḥ atiśayena madayitā āsa babhūva |
yasya somasya pānena indro vṛtrahatye vṛtrahananakāle mamāda hṛṣṭo babhūva |
yaḥ somaḥ śambarasya asurasya purūṇi bahūni cyautnā cyautnāni dalāni jaghāna |
tathā dehyaḥ digdhā upacitā āsurīḥ purīḥ tadīyān dehān vā narvārti nava ca navottaranavatisaṃkhyākā yaḥ somaḥ vi han vyahan ayaṃ soma iti prathamapādena sambandhaḥ ||
ayaṃ me pīta urdiyati vācamayaṃ manīpāmṛśatīmajīgaḥ | ayaṃ paḻvarīramimīt dhīro na yāmyo bhuvanaṃ kaccunāre || 3 || ayam | me | pītaḥ | ut | iyarti | vācam | ayam | manīṣām | uśatīm | ajīgarti | ayam | paḻvarīḥ | amimīt | dhīraḥ | na | yāmyaḥ | bhuvanam | kat | cana | āre ||3||
atha somaḥ pītaḥ san me mama vācam udiyarti udgamayati |
ayam eva somaḥ uśatīṃ kāntāṃ manīṣāṃ buddhim ut ajīgaḥ udgārayati |
prakāśayati ||
‘gṛ nigaraṇe’ ityasmāṇṇya- ntālluṅi caḍi rūpam ||
api ca ayaṃ dhīraḥ dhīmān somaḥ paḻvarīḥ ‘paṇmāṃvīrṃhasapāntu dyauśca pṛthivī cāśva rātriśvāpaśrauṣadhayaśca ’ iti pratipāditā dyupṛthivyādyāḥ pad amimīta nirmitavān |
yāmyaḥ urvībhyaḥ āre dūre viprakṛte deśe kaccana kiṃcana bhuvanaṃ bhūtajātaṃ na bhavati api tu āstveva sarvaṃ bhūtajātaṃ vartate tādṛśīrvīrnirmitavān ||
ayaṃ sa yo varimārṇaṃ pṛthivyā vṛṣmāṇaṃ divo akṛṇodyaṃ saḥ | ayaṃ pīyūṣaṃ tisṛṣu pravatsu somo dādhārorvantarikṣam || 4 || ayam | saḥ | yaḥ | varimārṇam | pṛthivyāḥ | vṛṣmāṇam | divaḥ | akṛṇot | ayam | maḥ | ayam | pīyūṣam | tisṛṣu | pravatsu | somaḥ | dādhāra | uru | antarikṣam || 4 ||
saḥ khalu ayaṃ somaḥ yaḥ pṛthivyāḥ varimārṇam urutvaṃ vistṛtatvam akṛṇot akarot |
tathāyaṃ somaḥ divaḥ dyulokasya vṛṣmāṇaṃ saṃhatatvaṃ dṛḍhatvamakṛṇot akarot |
ayaṃ somaḥ maḥ hi bhavati |
api ca ayaṃ somaḥ tisṛṣu oṣadhīṣvapsu goṣu ca pravatsu prakṛṣṭāsvetāsu pīyūṣaṃ rasaṃ dādhāra dhārayati |
tathā uru vistīrṇam antarikṣaṃ ca dhārayati |
tathā ca mantrāntaraṃ—‘svāmmā oṣadhīḥ soma viśvāstvamapo ajanayastvaṃ gāḥ |
tvamā tatanyānvantarikṣam ’ (ṛ. saṃ. 1. 91. 22) iti ||
1. gha-śa-pratipādanāt |
2. kha-jha-bha-ra-sahatam; na-mahattvam |
ayaṃ viṃdaccitradṛśīkaṃmarṇaḥ śukramedhanāmuṣasāmanīke | ayaṃ mahānmahatā skambhanenadyāmastabhād vṛṣabho marutvān || 5 || ayam | viṃdat | citra'dṛśīkam | arṇaḥ | śukra'sadhanām | upasām | anīke | ayam | mahān | mahatā | sva'mbhanena | ut | dyām | astabhāt | vṛṣabhaḥ | marutvān || 5 ||
ayaṃ somaḥ citradṛśīkaṃ vicitradarśanaṃ yadvā āścaryeṇa darśanīyam arṇaḥ sarvasya prerakaṃ sauraṃ jyotiḥ vidan labhayati |
kasmin kāle |
śukrasadhanāṃ śukraṃ nirmalamantarikṣaṃ sadha sadanaṃ yāsāṃ tāsām upasām anīke pramukhe |
upaḥ kāla ityarthaḥ |
api ca mahān pravṛddhaḥ ayaṃ somaḥ mahatā sthaviṣṭhena skambhanena stambhasādhanena madhyavartināntarikṣeṇa dyāṃ dyulokam ut astabhāt ūrdhvamasthāpayat |
yathādyāṃ na patati tathāntarikṣaṃ tasyādhāratvena kṛtavānityarthaḥ |
kīdṛśaḥ somaḥ |
vṛṣabhaḥ vṛṣṭerjanayitā |
somāhutyā hi vṛṣṭirjanyate |
mahatvān marudbhiryuktaḥ ||
30 ||
dhṛṣatpiba kalaśe sormamindra vṛtrahā śūra samare vasūnām | mādhyaṃdine savana ā vṛṣasva rayisthānāṃ rayimasmāsu dhehi || 6 || dhṛṣat | piba | kalaśe | somam | indra | vṛtra'hā | śūra | sama'are | vasūnām | mādhyaṃdine | savane | ā | vṛṣasva | rayi'sthānaḥ | rayim | asmāsu | dhehi || 6 ||
he śūra śauryavan indra vṛtrahā vṛtrāṇāmāvarakāṇāṃ śatrūṇāṃ hantā tvaṃ vasūnāṃ dhanānāṃ samare saṃgrāme nimittabhūte sani kalaśe droṇakalaśe 'vasthitaṃ somaṃ dhṛṣat dhṛṣṭaṃ yathā bhavati tathā piba |

jaṭharaṃ somena pūraya |
sarvaindraṃ hi mādhyaṃdinaṃ savanam |
kiṃca rayisthānaḥ rayīṇāṃ dhanānāṃ sthānabhūtastvaṃ rayiṃ dhanam asmāsu dhehi dhāraya ||
indra pra ṇaḥ puraetevaṃ paśya pra nau naya prataraṃ vasyo accha | bhavā supāro atipārayo no bhavā sunītiruta vāmanītiḥ || 7 || indre | pra | naḥ | puraetā'iva | paśya | pra | naḥ | naya | pra'taram | vasyaḥ | accha | bhava | su'pāraḥ | ati'pārayaḥ | naḥ | bhava | su'nītiḥ | uta | vāma'nītiḥ || 7 ||
he indra tvaṃ puraeteva purato gantaiva naḥ asmān pra paśya prakarṣeṇaikṣasva |
yathā mārgarakṣakaḥ svayaṃ purato gacchannugacchato rakṣaṇīyān pathikān paśyati tathā paśyetyarthaḥ |
tathā vasyaḥ vasīyaḥ śreṣṭhaṃ dhanam accha abhimukhyena prataraṃ prakṛṣṭataramatiśayena pra ṇaya asmān prāpaya |
tathā supāraḥ suṣṭhu pārayitā duḥkhebhyastārayitā bhava |

sunītiḥ śobhanayananaścāsmākaṃ bhava |

asmadarthaṃ śreṣṭhaprāpaṇo bhava ||
1. gha-śa- nimitte |
aindre paśau 'uruṃ na:' ityeṣā vapāyā yājyā|
sūtritaṃ ca—'uruṃ no lokamanu neṣi vidvān pra sasāhiṣe puruhūta śatrūn' (āśva. śrau. 3. 7) iti|
hotrakai: sada:prasarpaṇasamaye'pyeṣā japya|
sūtritaṃ ca—'uruṃ no lokamanu neṣi vidvāniti japantaḥ' (āśva. śrau. 5. 3) iti|
cāturvaṃśike'- hanyahargaṇeṣu ca dvitīyādiṣvaha:su mādhyaṃdinasavane'cchāvākasyaiṣā cārambhaṇīyā'|
sūtritaṃ ca—'uruṃ no lokamanu neṣi vidvāniti kadrudraya ārambhaṇīyā:' (āśva. śrau. 7. 4) iti ||
uruṃ nau lokamanu neṣi vidvāntsarvvajjyotirbhayaṃ svasti| ṛṣvā te indra sthavirasya bāhū upa stheyāma śaraṇā bṛhantā || 8 || urum | na: | lokam | anu | neṣi | vidvān | sarvva: | svat | jyoti: | abhayam | svasti | ṛṣvā | te | indra | sthavirasya | bāhū iti | upa | stheyāma | śaraṇā | bṛhantā || 8 ||
he indra vidvān jānaṃstvam uruṃ vistīrṇaṃ lokaṃ sthānaṃ svargasthaṃ na: asmān anu neṣi anugamaya|
tathā sarvvata sukhavat abhayaṃ bhayarahitaṃ jyoti: ca svasti kṣemeṇānu neṣi asmāna- nugamaya|
he indra sthavirasya sthūlasya vṛddhasya te tava saṃbandhinau ṛṣvā darśanīyau bṛhantā mahantau bāhū tvadīyau hastau śaraṇā śaraṇau rakṣakatayā upa stheyāma upatiṣṭhema |
sevemahi ||

‘vanaspate’ ityeṣā ratārohaṇe viniyuktā |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘vanaspate vīḍvaṅgo hi bhūyā ityetayā- nyānpapi vānaspatyāni’ (āśva. gṛ. 2. 6. 5) iti ||
vanaspate vīḍvaṅgo hi bhūyā asmatsakhā prataraṇaḥ suvīraḥ | gobhiḥ saṃnaddho asi vīḍvyasvasthātā tai jayatu jetvāni || 26 || vanaspate | vīḍu'aṅgaḥ | hi | bhūyāḥ | asmat'sakhā | prataraṇaḥ | suvīraḥ | gobhiḥ | sam'naddhaḥ | asi | vīḍvyasva | ā'sthātā | te | jayatu | jetvāni || 26 ||
he vanaspate vanaspativikāra ratha tvaṃ vīḍvaṅgaḥ dṛḍhāvayavaḥ bhūyāḥ bhava |
asmatsakhā vayaṃ sakhāyo yasya sa tādṛśaśca bhava |
prataraṇaḥ pravardhayitā suvīraḥ śobhanairvīraiḥ śūrabhaṭaiḥ putrādibhirvā yuktaśca bhava |
api ca tvaṃ gobhiḥ govikāraiścarmabhiḥ saṃnaddhaḥ asi samyak baddho bhavasi |
tādṛśastvaṃ vīḍvyasva asmān dṛḍhīkuru |
te tava āsthātā tvayi avasthito rathī ca jetvāni jetavyāni śatrusainyāni jayatu ||
divaspṛthivyāḥ paryoja uddhṛtaṃ vanaspatibhyaḥ paryābhṛtaṃ sahaḥ | apā mojamānaṃ pari gobhirāvṛtamindrasya vajraṃ haviṣā ratha yaja || 27 || divaḥ | pṛthivyāḥ | pari | ojaḥ | ut'dhṛtam | vanaspati'bhyaḥ | pari | ā'bhṛtam | sahaḥ | apāṃ | ojamānam | pari | gobhiḥ | ā'vṛtam | indrasya | vajram | haviṣā | ratham | yaja ||27||
1. kha-abhyapūjayat; jha-na-bhaya-abhyapūjayat; tara-pūjayat; śa-pūjayata |
2. ya-āsthātā te |
ᐁdiva: dyulokāt ᐁpṛthivyā: bhūmeśca ᐁudbhūtam udbhūtaṃ sāravenopāttaṃ yat ᐁoja: balamasti tadrūpam |
vanaspativikāratvādrathasya |
vanaspatayo hi pṛthivyā: sārabhūtā: te ca ᐁsusaṃbandhibhirudai: pravṛddhā: |
ato rathasya lokadvayasāratvamupapannam |
ᐁpari ᐁpañcamyarthānuvādī |
api ca ᐁvanaspatibhya: vṛkṣebhya: |
ᐁpari pūrvavan |
ᐁābhūtam āhataṃ ᐁsaha: balarūpam ᐁapām udakānām ᐁojamānaṃ prerakam |
yadvā |
ojmā vega: |
apāṃ vega iva vegavantaṃ ᐁgobhi: govikāraiścarmabhi: ᐁpari parita: ᐁāvṛtam āve- ṣṭitam ᐁindrasya ᐁvajraṃ vajraikadeśam |
śrūyate hi—' indro vṛtrāya vajramudayacchat sa tredhā vyabhavat spyastṛtīyaṃ rathastṛtīyaṃ yūpastṛtīyaṃ ' iti |

semāṃ nau havyadātiṃ juṣāṇo deva ratha prati havyā gṛbhāya |

indrasya | vajra: | marutām | anīkam | mitrasya | garbha: | varuṇasya | nābhi: | sa: | imām | na: | havya'dātim | juṣāṇa: | deva | ratha | prati | havyā | gṛbhāya || 28 ||
yo ratha: ᐁindrasya ᐁvajra: uktaprakāreṇa vajraikadeśa: ᐁmarutām etatsaṃjñānāṃ devagaṇānām ᐁanīkam agrabhūtaṃ tadgacchīdagragāmī ᐁmitrasya aharbhimānino devasya ᐁgarbha: garbhavadantarvartamāna: |
ahani hi ratha: saṃcarati |
ᐁvaruṇasya rājyabhimānidevasya ᐁnābhi: nābhisthānīya: |
nābhiyathā dehamadhye naśvalyenātiṣṭhate tadvat rātrau kvacidavasthita: |
he ᐁdeva dyotamāna ᐁratha ya uktaguṇa: ᐁsa: tādṛśastvam ᐁimāṃ ᐁna: asmadīyāṃ ᐁhavyadātiṃ havirdānavartīṃ yāgakriyāṃ ᐁjuṣāṇa: sevamāna: san ᐁhavyā havyāntyasmadīyāni havīṃṣi ᐁprati ᐁgṛbhāya pratigṛhāṇa |
pareṇa tṛcena saṃgrāme dundubhirabhimarśanīya: |
sūtritaṃ ca—' upa śvāsaya pṛthivīmuta dyāmiti tṛcena dundubhimabhimṛśet ' (āśva. gṛ. 3. 12. 17) iti |
upa śvāsaya pṛthivīmuta dyāṃ purutrā tai manutāṃ viṣṭhitaṃ jagat |
sa dundubhe sañjarindreṇa devairdūrahāvīyo arpa sedha śatrūn |
29 |
upa | śvāsaya | pṛthivīm | uta | dyām | puru'trā | tai | manutām | vi'sthitam | jagat | sa: | dundubhe | sa'j | indreṇa | devai: | durāta | davīya: | arpa | sedha | śatrūn || 29 ||
he dundubhe ᐁpṛthivīmuta api ca ᐁdyāṃ divam ᐁupa ᐁśvāsaya ātmīyena jayaghoṣeṇāśvāsaya |
yathā lokadvayaṃ tvadīyena śabdenāpūritaṃ bhavati tādṛśaṃ śabdaṃ kurvityartha: |
api ca ᐁvisthitaṃ viśeṣeṇa sthitaṃ ᐁjagat sthāvaraṃ jaṅgamaṃ cobhayavidhaṃ prāṇijātaṃ ᐁte tvadīyaṃ ᐁśabdaṃ ᐁpurutrā bahudhā ᐁmanutāṃ manyatām |
jānātu |
he ᐁdundubhe ᐁsa: tvam ᐁindreṇa anyai: ᐁdevai: ca ᐁsaj: saha ᐁdūrāhavīya: dūrādapi dūrataraṃ ᐁśatrūn asmadīyān ᐁapa ᐁsedha apagamaya |
atra niruktaṃ-' dundubhiriti śabdānukaraṇaṃ dumo bhinna iti vā dundubhyatervā syādvakarmaṇa: ' (niru. 9. 12) ityādi |
1. kha-jha-ta-na-bha-pari: | 2. kha-jha-na-bha-viṣṭhitam; ta-sthitam | 3. kha-jha-na-bha-ya- dūrataramatiśayena dūrataram | 4. jha-syācchabdakarmaṇa: |
ā krandaya balamojo na ā dhā niḥ stanini duritā bādhamānaḥ |
apa protha dundubhe ducchunā ita indrasya muṣṭirasi vīḻayasva ||
30 ||
ā | krandaya | balam | ojaḥ | na | ā | dhāḥ | niḥ | stanini | duḥ'itā | bādhamānaḥ | apa | protha | dundubhe | ducchunāḥ | itaḥ | indrasya | muṣṭiḥ | asi | vīḻayasva || 30 ||
he dundubhe ᵥā ᵥkrandaya asmacchatrūn rodaya |

prayaccha |

hiṃsaṃsvaṃ ᵥniḥ ᵥstanihi nitarāṃ śabdaṃ kuru |
‘ stana śabde ’ iti dhātuḥ |

athāṣṭamo'dhyāya ārabhyate |
ṣaṣṭhamaṇḍalasya caturthe'nuvāke catvāri sūktāni vyākhyātāni |
'yajñayajñā' iti dvāviṃśatyṛcaṃ pañcamaṃ sūktaṃ bṛhaspatiputrasya śaṃyorārṣam |
atrānukramyate—'yajñayajñā dvyadhikā śaṃyustṛṇapāṇikaṃ pṛbhisūktaṃ pragāthau bṛhatī mahāsatòbṛhatī mahābāhatabāhaṃtī pragāthāvityāmedhyaḥ: kākubhaḥ pragāthaḥ purauṣṇigbṛhatyatijagatīti mārutyo'nyāstisro vāsāṃ liṅgoktadevatāḥ kākubhaḥ pragāthaḥ: purauṣṇigbṛhatīti pauṣṇyo bṛhatī mahābṛhatī yavamadhyāvanuṣṭup2 dyāvābhūmīrvā pṛbhervā' iti |
'yajñayajñā vaḥ' ityeṣā bṛhatī |
'tṛtīyo dvādaśakaḥ3' iti tallakṣaṇasadbhāvāt |
dvitīyā satobṛhatī |
'ayujau jāgatau satobṛhatī' ityuktalakṣaṇopetatvāt |
tṛtīyāccaturthyau bṛhatīsatobṛhatyau pañcamī bṛhatī ṣaṣṭhī mahāsatobṛhatī |
aṣṭākṣarādyaḥ pādā dvādaśākṣarau dvau pādau sā mahāsatobṛhatī |
saptamī mahābṛhatī |
aṣṭākṣarāścatvāraḥ pādā eko dvādaśākṣaraḥ sā mahābṛhatī |
aṣṭamī mahāsatobṛhatī |
aṣṭinakhyo dvau jāgatau sā mahāsatobṛhatī |
navamī bṛhatī daśamī satobṛhatī |
etā daśa agnidevatyāḥ |
ekādaśī kakup |
madhyamo yadi dvādaśākṣara ubhayato'ṣṭakau dvau pādau sā kakup |
dvādaśī satobṛhatī trayodaśī purauṣṇip |
ādyo jāgato dvitīyatṛtīyau gāyatrau sā purauṣṇip |
caturdaśī bṛhatī pañcadaśī atijagatī dvāpañcādakṣarā |
ekādaśyāḥ pañca maruddevatākāḥ |
trayodaśyādīnāṃ tisṛṇāṃ liṅgoktā indrādayo vā devatāḥ |
ṣoḍaśī kakup saptadaśī satobṛhatī aṣṭādaśī purauṣṇip ekonaviṃśī bṛhatī |
ṣoḍaśyādīnāṃ catasṛṇāṃ pūṣā devatā |
viṃśī bṛhatī ekaviṃśī yavamadhyā mahābṛhatī |
ādyau dvāvaṣṭau tṛtīyo dvādaśakṣaracaturthapañcamāvaṣṭau sā yavamadhyā mahābṛhatī |
viṃśyekaviṃśyoḥ 'pṛbhisūktam' ityuktatvāt marutāṃ mātā pṛbhirdevatā |
dvāviṃśī anuṣṭup |
sā cāpi pṛbhirdevatyā dyāvābhūmīdevatyā vā |
sūktaviniyogo laiṅgikaḥ |
ābhimāste 'yajñayajñā vaḥ' ityayaṃ stotriyapragāthaḥ |
sutritaṃ ca— 'yajñayajñā vo agnaye devo vo draviṇodā iti pragāthau stotriyānurūpau' (āśva. śrau. 5. 20) iti ||
yajñayajñā vo agnaye girāgirā ca dakṣase |

1. kha-jha-bha-°vivakṣayāṃ bahumatamamā°; ya-°vivakṣayāṃ tadmanamamā°|

1. jha-dyotanena |
na-vājapeye abhimāṣate tvaṃ naścitra iti pragāthaḥ stotriyaḥ |

dadātu vā |

grāḥ gāyatryādīni chandāṃsi |
‘chandāṃsi vai grāḥ’ (tai. saṃ. 5. 1. 7. 2) iti śruteḥ |
taiḥ saha prīyamāṇā guṇate stuvate mahyam acchidraṃ chidrarahitaṃ durārdharṣaṃ śatrubhiḥ śītavānādibhiśca dharṣitumaśakyaṃ śaraṇaṃ gṛhaṃ śarma sukhaṃ ca yaṃsat prayacchatu ||
ekādaśine pāṃṣṇe paśau ‘pathaspathaḥ’ iti haviyo yājyā |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘pathaspathaḥ paripatiṃ vacasyā bṛhaspate yā paramā parāvaditi dve’ (āśva. śrau. 3. 7) iti ||
1. kha-jha-na-bha-ya-gaṇābhiprāyeṇaika |
2. gha-śa-jagataḥ sthāpaka |

^pathaspathaḥ: sarvasya mārgasya ^paripatim adhipatam ^arkam arcanīyaṃ pūṣaṇaṃ ^kāmena kāmyamānena phalena ^kṛtaḥ vaśīkṛtaḥ stotā ^vacasyā' stutyā ^abhyānaṭ abhyanutām |
prāpnotu |

‘rā dāne’ |
asmāleṭi rūpam ||
tathā ^dhiyaṃdhiyaṃ sarvamasmadīyaṃ karma sa pūṣā ^pra ^sīṣadhāti prasādhayatu ||

sūyitaṃ ca—‘prathamabhājaṃ yaśasaṃ vayodhāṃ somāpūṣaṇā jananā rayīṇāmiti sūktam’ (āśva. śrau. 3.8) iti ||
prathamabhājaṃ yaśasaṃ vayodhāṃ supāṇiṃ devaṃ sugabha̭stimṛvam |
hotā yakṣadyajataṃ pustyānāmabhitvaṣṭāraṃ suhavaṃ vibhāvā ||

tathā he ᵥrodasī dyāvāpṛthivyau ᵥsusuṣṭubhe susukhe yuvāṃ ᵥbṛhat mahat ᵥśaraṇaṃ gṛhaṃ ca karathaḥ kurutam |

abhi tyaṃ vīraṃ girvaṇasamindraṃ brahmaṇā jaritarnavena | śravadidvamuṣa ca stavāno rāsadvājā~ upa maho gṛṇānaḥ || 6 || abhi | tyam | vīram | girvaṇasam | arca | indram | brahmaṇā | jaritaḥ | navena | śravat | it | havam | upa | ca | stavānaḥ | rāsat | vājān | upa | mahaḥ | gṛṇānaḥ ||6||

^hi yasmāt ^yūyaṃ ^mātṛtamāḥ mātṛbhyo'pyadhikāḥ ^bhiṣajaḥ^ ^stha bhavatha tasmāt dhātetyanvayaḥ |
