ā nau devaḥ savitā trāyamāṇo hiraṇyapāṇirajatā jaṅgamyāt | yo datravā~ upaso na pratīkaṃ vyūrṇute dāśuṣe vāryāṇi || 8 || ā | naḥ | devaḥ | savitā | trāyamāṇaḥ | hiraṇyapāṇiḥ | yajataḥ | jaṅgamyāt | yaḥ | datravān | upasaḥ | na | pratīkam | vi'ūrṇute | dāśuṣe | vāryāṇi || 8 ||
^trāyamāṇaḥ rakṣan ^hiraṇyapāṇiḥ suvarṇahasto hitaramaṇīyapāṇirvā ^yajataḥ yaṣṭavyaḥ evaṃbhūtaḥ ^savitā prerakaḥ ^devaḥ ^naḥ asmān ^ā ^jaṅgamyāt āgacchatu |

1. kha-gha-bha-pradadyāt | 2. kha-hiṃsantam; gha-bha-śa-ahiṃsantam | 3. kha-jha-na-bha-tatsutāya | 4. kha- jha-na-bha-pṛthakartavyānāṃ bhayānām | 5. kha-gha-bha-bhiṣajaḥ ārogyakartāraḥ | 6. ta-bhaiṣajyamāsthityata |

upaso na pratīkam uṣasaḥ pramukhamiva dāśuṣe havīṃṣi dattavate yajamānāya vāryāṇi varaṇīyāni dhanāni nyuṇate vivṛṇoti prakāśayati sa savitetyanvayaḥ ||

9 ||
uta | tvam | sūno iti | sahasaḥ | naḥ | adya | ā | devān | asmin | adhvare | vavṛtyāḥ | syām | aham | te | sadam | ita | rātau | tarva | syām | agne | avasā | mudvīraḥ || 9 ||
uta api ca he sahasaḥ sūno balasya putra agne tvam atha asmin kāle naḥ asmadīye asminnadhvare yāge devān yaṣṭavyānindrādīn ā vavṛtyāḥ āvartaya |
āgamaya |

10 ||
uta | tyā | me | havam | ā | jagmyātam | nāsatyā | dhībhiḥ | yuvam | aṅga | viprā | atrim | na | mahaḥ | tamasaḥ | amumuktam | tūrvatam | narā | duḥitāta | abhīke || 10 ||
uta api ca he viprā prājñau nāsatyā satyasvabhāvau satyasya netārau vā he aśvinau tyā tau prasiddhau yuvaṃ yuvāṃ dhībhiḥ paricaraṇakaiḥ 2 karmabhirupetaṃ me madīyaṃ havaṃ stotram aṅga kṣipram ā jagmyātam āgacchatam |

te nau rāyo ghumato vājavato dātārau bhūta nuvataḥ purukṣoḥ |

11 ||
te | naḥ | rāyaḥ | ghu'mantaḥ | vājavataḥ | dātāraḥ | bhūta | nuvataḥ | purukṣoḥ | daśasyantaḥ | divyāḥ | pārthivāsaḥ | go'jātāḥ | apyāḥ | mṛḻata | ca | devāḥ || 11 ||
he devāḥ te yūyaṃ ghumataḥ dīptimataḥ vājavataḥ balayuktasya nuvataḥ nṛbhiḥ putrādibhirupetasya purukṣoḥ bahubhiḥ kīrtanīyasya rāyaḥ dhanasya naḥ asmabhyaṃ dātāro bhūta bhavata |
api ca daśasyantaḥ kāmān prayacchantaḥ divyāḥ divi bhavā ādityāḥ pārthivāsaḥ pṛthivyāṃ bhavā vasavaḥ gojātāḥ |
gauḥ pṛśnirmādhyamikā vāk |

‘udu tyat’ iti ṣoḍaśacaṃ dvitīyaṃ sūktamṛjivana ārṣaṃ vaiśvadevam |

1 ||
ut | ū~ iti | tyat | cakṣuḥ | mahiṃ | mitrayoḥ | ā | eti | priyam | varuṇayoḥ | adabdham | ṛtasya | śuci | darśatama | anīkam | rukmaḥ | na | divaḥ | ut'itā | vi | adyaut || 1 ||

veda yastrīṇi vidathānyeṣāṃ devānāṃ janma sanutarā ca vipraḥ | ṛju marteṣu vṛjinā ca paśyanbhabhi caṣṭe sūro arya evān || 2 || veda | yaḥ | trīṇi | vidathāni | eṣām | devānām | janma | sanutaḥ | ā | ca | vipraḥ | ṛju | marteṣu | vṛjinā | ca | paśyan | abhi | caṣṭe | sūraḥ | aryaḥ | evān || 2 ||

5 ||
dyauḥ | pitariti | pṛthivi | mātar | adhuḥ | agne | bhrātaḥ | vasavaḥ | mṛḻata | naḥ | viśve | ādityāḥ | aditte | sa'joṣāḥ | asbhabhyam | śarma | bahulam | vi | yanta || 5 ||
he pitaḥ pitṛsthānīya dyauḥ dyuloka he mātaḥ mātṛsthānīya adhuḥ adrodhiḥ pṛthivi he bhrātaḥ bhrātṛsthānīya agne he vasavaḥ vāsayitāro devāḥ te sarve yūyaṃ naḥ asmān mṛḻata sukhayata |

mā no vṛkāya vṛkyai samasmā aghāyate riradhatā yajatrāḥ |

vaiśvadeve paśāvevaiṣa yājyā |
sūtritaṃ ca—'viśve devā: śṛṇutemaṃ havaṃ me ye ke ca jmā mahino ahimāyā:' (āśva. śrau. 1. 7) iti ||
viśve devā: śṛṇutemaṃ haveṃ me ye antarikṣe ya upa dyavi cha |
ye agnijihvā uta vā yajatrā āsadyāsminbarhiṣi mādayadhvam ||
13 ||
viśve | devā: | śṛṇuta | imam | havam | me | ye | antarikṣe | ye | upa | dyavi | stha | ye | agni'jihvā: | uta | vā | yajatrā: | ā | sadya | asmin | barhiṣi | mādayadhvam || 13 ||
he 'viśve 'devā: 'me madīyam 'imaṃ 'havam āhvānaṃ 'śṛṇuta |

ye ca 'dyavi dyuloke stha |
'ye ca 'agnijihvā: |
agnijihvāsthānīyo yeṣām |

॰ajāśva |
ajāśvāgā evāśvā aśvakāryāpakṣā yasya tādṛśa pūṣan ॰vasoḥ vasunaḥ dhanasya ॰rāśiḥ saṃghaśca bhavasi |

॰ajāśvaṃ chāgavāhanaṃ
yaḥ stotā yaenaṃ yapūṣaṇaṃ yapoṣakaṃ yakarambhāt karabhāṇāṃ ghṛtamiśrāṇāṃ yavasaktūnām attā' yaiti' yādideśati2 abhiṣṭauti yatena puruṣeṇa anyaḥ3 yadevaḥ yana yādideśa ādeśyaḥ stotavyo na4 bhavati |

uta ghā sa rathītamaḥ sakhyā sartapartiyujā | indro vṛtrāṇi jighnate || 2 || uta | gha | saḥ | rathītamaḥ | sakhyā | sat'patiḥ | yujā | indraḥ | vṛtrāṇi | jighnate ||2||
yauta yagha api ca yasaḥ yaḥ śatrūṇāṃ hantṛtvena prasiddhastādṛśaḥ : yarathītamaḥ atiśayena rathī mahā- rathaḥ : yasatpatiḥ satāṃ pālayitā evaṃguṇaḥ : yaindraḥ : yasakhyā mitrabhūtena pūṣṇā yayujā sahāyabhūtena yuktaḥ san yavṛtrāṇi śatrūn yajighnate hanti |
indrasyāpi ayameva sāhāyyakārītyarthaḥ : ||
utādaḥ paruṣe gavi sūraścakraṃ hiraṇyayam | nyairayadrathītamaḥ || 3 || uta | adaḥ | paruṣe | gavi | sūraḥ | cakram | hiraṇyayam | ni | airayat | rathi'tamaḥ ||3||
yauta api ca yasūraḥ prerakaḥ : yarathītamaḥ atiśayena rathī netṛtamo vā pūṣā yaparuṣe paruṣmati parvavati bhāsvati vā yagavi |
gacchatīti gaurādityaḥ |
tasmin yahiraṇyayaṃ hiraṇmayaṃ suvarṇanirmitam yaadaḥ tat rathasya yacakraṃ yanyairayat nitarāṃ prerayati sma ||

‘śukraṃ te’ iti caturthaṃ navamaṃ sūktaṃ bharadvājasyārṣaṃ pauṣṇam |
dvitīyā jagatī śiṣṭāstriṣṭubhaḥ |

pravargyāmiṣṭave'pyeṣā |

atha yā'yaṃ joṣvākaṃ vadati vijajñapaḥ prājitahoṣiṇau devā7 na7 tasyāśnīthaḥ' (niru. 5. 22) iti ||
indrāgnī ko asya vāṃ devau martyaścetati | viṣpūco aśvānyuyujāna īyate ekaḥ samāna ā rathe || 5 || indrāgnī iti | kaḥ | asya | vām | devau | martyaḥ | ciketati | viṣpūcaḥ | aśvān | yuyujānaḥ | īyate | ekaḥ | samāne | ā | rathe || 5 ||
he 7devau dyotamānau 7indrāgnī 7vāṃ yuvayoḥ 7asya idaṃ karma 7kaḥ 7martaḥ manuṣyaḥ 7ciketati jānīyāt |
na ko'pītyarthaḥ |
kiṃ tatkarma |

‘śrathadvṛtram’ iti pañcadaśarcamekādaśaṃ sūktaṃ bharadvājasyārṣamaindrāgnam|
ādau tisṛbhistisṛbhiḥ caturthyādyā nava gāyatryaḥ|
trayodaśī triṣṭup caturdaśī bṛhatī pañcadaśyanuṣṭup|
tathā cānukrāntam— ‘śrathatpañconā gāyatraṃ tu tritriṣṭubhādi triṣṭubbahatyanuṣṭubantam’ iti|
gataḥ sūktaviniyogaḥ|
varuṇapraghāseṣu aindrāgnasya haviṣaḥ ‘śrathan’ ityeṣā yājyā|
sūtritaṃ ca—‘indrāgnī avasā gataṃ śrathadvṛtramuta sanoti vājam’ (āśva. śrau. 2. 17) iti||
śrathadvṛtramuta sanoti vājamindrā yo agnī sahurī saparyāta |
irajyantā vasavyasya bhūreḥ sahastamā sahasā vājayantā ||
1 ||

he ᵛvṛtrahaṇā vṛtrasyāsurasya hantārau he ᵛindra he ᵛagne ᵛvṛtrahabhiḥ vṛtrasya hantubhiḥ vṛtrahanana- nimittaiḥ ᵛśumbhaiḥ balaiḥ ᵛnamobhiḥ asmabhyaṃ dātavyairathaiśca sārdham ᵛarvāk asmadabhimukham ᵛā ᵛyātam āgacchatam |
he ᵛindra he ᵛagne ᵛyuvaṃ yuvām ᵛakavebhiḥ akutsitaiḥ mahadbhiḥ ᵛuttamaibhiḥ utkṛṣṭatamaiḥ ᵛrādhobhiḥ dhanaiḥ saha ᵛasme asmāsu ᵛbhavatam āvirbhavatam ||
cāturviṃśike'hani prātaḥsavane'cchāvākaśāstre ‘tā huve’ iti padahastotriyastṛcaḥ |
sūtritaṃ ca- ‘tā huve yayoridamimaṃ vāmasya manmanaḥ ’ ( āśva. śrau. 7. 2 ) iti |

indrāgnī na mardhataḥ ||

‘iyamadadāt’ iti caturdaśacaṃ dvādaśa sūkaṃ bharadvājasyārṣaṃ sarasvatīdevatākam |
āditastasro jagatyaḥ trayodaśyapi jagatī caturdaśī triṣṭup śiṣṭā gāyatryaḥ |
tathā cānukrāntam—‘iyaṃ padunā sārasvataṃ trijagatyādi jagatītriṣṭubantam’ iti |
atra śaunakaḥ—‘iyamityetadādyaṃ tu sūkaṃ sārasvataṃ japet |
dvijaḥ prātaḥ śucirbhūtvā vāgmī bhavati buddhimān’ (ṛgvi. 2. 295) iti |
pāṣṭhike paṣṭhe'hani prauge ‘iyamadadāt’ iti sārasvatasṛcaḥ |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘iyamadadādrabhasamacyutamiti praugam’ (āśva. śrau. 8. 1) iti ||
iyamadadādrabhasamacyutaṃ divodāsaṃ vadhryaśvārya dāśuṣe | yā śaśvantamākhvādāvasaṃ pṛṇi tā tai dātrāṇi taviṣā sarakhati || 1 || iyam | adadāt | rabhasam | ṛṇa'cyutam | divaḥ'dāmam | vadhri'aśvārya | dāśuṣe | yā | śaśvantam | ā'khvād | avasam | pṛṇam | tā | tai | dātrāṇi | taviṣā | marakhati || 1 ||
iyaṃ sarasvatī rabhasaṃ vegavantam ṛṇacyutaṃ vaidikasya devarṣipitrisambandhino laukikasya ca ṛṇasya cyāvayitāraṃ divodāsam etatsaṃjñaṃ putraṃ dāśuṣe havīṃṣi dattavate vadhryaśvāya etatsaṃjñāya ṛṣaye adadāt dattavatī |
yā sarasvatī śaśvantaṃ bahulaṃ paṇaṃ paṇanaśīlaṃ vaṇijamādatjanam avasaṃ kevalaṃ svātmana eva tarpakam ākhvād ājaghāna seyamadadādityanvayaḥ |
atha pratyakṣakṛtya stauti |
he sarasvati devi tā tāni putradānādīni te tvadīyāni dātrāṇi dānāni taviṣā taviṣāṇi mahānti bhavanti ||
iyaṃ śuṣmebhirbisakhāvārujatsānu girīṇāṃ taviṣebhirūrmibhiḥ | pārāvatnīmavase suvṛktibhiḥ sarasvatīmā vivāsema dhītibhiḥ || 2 || iyam | śuṣmebhiḥ | bisakhāḥ'iva | arujat | sānu | girīṇām | taviṣebhiḥ | ūrmi'bhiḥ | pārāvat'ghnīm | avase | suvṛkti'bhiḥ | sarasvatīm | ā | vivāsema | dhīti'bhiḥ ||2||
sarasvatī devatārūpeṇa nadīrūpeṇa ca vartate |
devatārūpā stutā |
adhunānyā nadīrūpāṃ sarasvatīṃ stauti |
iyaṃ nadīrūpā sarasvatī śuṣmaiḥ' śoṣakairātmabalaiḥ taviṣebhiḥ mahadbhiḥ ūrmibhiḥ taraṅgaiḥ 1. gha-śa-śuṣmebhiḥ śuṣmaiḥ |

ᵥbisakhāiva|
bisaṃ khanatīti bisakhāḥ|
sa yathā bisārthaṃ paṅkaṃ rujati tadvat|

yadvā|

tayorghātitīm|

kiṃcāyaṃ paricārakaḥ ᵥgambhīrāya mahābalāya ᵥrakṣase rākṣasāya ᵥhetiṃ ghātakamāyudham ᵥasya asyati |
kṣipati |
prathamapuruṣasya madhyamapuruṣeṇa vyatyayaḥ |
ᵥdroghāya abhidrohāramakāya ᵥānavāya manuṣyasambandhini ᵥvacase ᵥcit vacanāyaiva hetimasyati |
manuṣyāṇāmabhidrohāya ᵥenam ᵥāskandatīti |
antaraiścakrastanayāya vṛtirdhumatā yāta nuvata rathena |
sarnutyena tyajasā martyasya vanuṣyatāmapi śīrṣā vavṛktam |
10 |
antaraiḥ | cakraiḥ | tanayāya | vṛtiḥ | dhu'matā | ā | yātam | nu'vatā | rathena | sarnutyena | tyajasā | martyasya | vanuṣyatām | api | śīrṣā | vavṛktam || 10 ||
he aśvinau ᵥantaraiḥ anikṛṣṭaiḥ ᵥcakraiḥ yuktena ᵥdhumatā dīptimanā ᵥnuvatā netrā sārathinā yuktena |
yadvā |
aśvayuktena |
'pataṅgāḥ naraḥ' ( ni 1. 14. 22 ) ityaśvanāmasu pāṭhāt |
ᵥrathena ᵥvartiḥ asmākaṃ gṛhaṃ ᵥtanayāya tanayaṃ dātum ᵥā ᵥyātam |
api ca ᵥsarnutyena tirohitena ᵥtyajasā krodhena |
'haṇiḥ tyajaḥ' iti krodhanāmasu pāṭhāt |
ᵥmartyasya manuṣyasya ᵥvanuṣyatāṃ bādhamānā- nām |
'vanuṣyatihantikarmā' iti yāskenoktatvāt |
ᵥśīrṣā śirāṃsi ᵥapi ᵥvavṛktaṃ pravaktam |
ā paramābhirṛta madhyamābhirniyudbhiryātamavamābhirvāk |
dṛḻahasya cidromato vi vrajasya duro varta guṇate citrarātī |
11 |
ā | paramābhiḥ | uta | madhyamābhiḥ | niyut'bhiḥ | yātam | avamābhiḥ | arvāk | dṛḻahasya | cit | go'maṃtaḥ | vi | vrajasya | duraḥ | vartam | guṇate | citrarātī iti nitra'rātī || 11 ||
1. gha-jña-ta-na-śa-bhūma | 2. kha-a-na-bha-ahantu | 3. kha-aghamahanta; jña-ta-na-śa-aghamahantu | 4. kha-jña-na-bha-paricaran; gha-paricaran | 5. tha-mitrāvaruṇa0 | 6. kha-bha-śara-na tu manuṣyāṇāmabhi0; jña-ta-na-nanu manuṣyāṇāmabhi0 | 7. ta-evam | 8. kha-jña-na-bha-śara-āskandatīti | 9. kha-jña-bha- ya-śa-pravaktam; gha-chinatam |
he aśvinau ^paramābhiḥ ^utkṛṣṭābhiḥ ^uta api ca ^madhyamābhiḥ ^anutkṛṣṭābhiḥ ^avamābhiḥ ^nikṛṣṭābhiśca ^niyudbhiḥ ^vāhaiḥ ^arvāk ^asmadabhimukham ^ā ^yātam |
^dṛḍhasya ^cit ^dṛḍhāpi- dhānasyāpi ^gomataḥ ^goyuktasya ^vrajasya ^goṣṭhasya ^duraḥ ^dvārāṇi ^vi ^varta ^vivṛṇutam |
kiṃca ^guṇate stuvate mahyaṃ ^citrarātī ^vicitradānau ^bhavatam |
2 |
‘ka tyā’ ityekādaśarcaṃ dvitīyaṃ sūktaṃ bharadvājasyārpam |
atreyamanukramaṇikā—‘ka tyekapadāntaṃ traiṣṭubham’ iti |
‘ā vāṃ sumne’ ityekādaśī ekapadā triṣṭup śiṣṭāstriṣṭubhaḥ |
‘āśvinaṃ tu’ ityu- ktvā'dasyāpi sūktasyāśvinau devatā |
prātaranuvākāśvinaśastrayorviṣṭubhe chandasyukto viniyogaḥ |
ka tyā valgū puruhūta dūto na stoma
vvāṃ yuvayorarthe vandhasa: somasya vgharīman vistṛte'bhivadau yat kṛtyamasti tat vakāri kṛtam|
vsuprāyaṇatamaṃ mṛdutamaṃ vbarhi: vastāri stīrṇam|
vuttānahasta: kṛtāñjali: vyuvayu: yuvāṃ kāmayamāno hotā vvavanda stauti|
vvāṃ yuvāṃ vnakṣantaḥ vyāpyuvantaḥ|
'tṛkṣa pṛkṣa nakṣa gatau' iti dhātu:||
vbhadraya: grāvāṇa: vā vājñan somarasam ā' samantāt vyaktikṛtavantaḥ||
ūrdhvo vāṃmiradhvareṣvasthāt ratireti jūrṇinīṃ ghṛtācīṃ|
pra hotā gurtamanā urāṇo'yuṃkta yo nāsatyā havīrman||
4||
ūrdhva:| vām| agni:| adhvareṣu| asthāt| pra| rati:| eti| jūrṇinī| ghṛtācī| pra| hotā| gurta'manā:| urāṇa:| ayuṃkta| ya:| nāsatyā| havīrman|| 4||
vagni: vvāṃ yuvayoryajñārtham vūrdhva: vamthāt udatiṣṭhat|
vadhvareṣu yajñeṣu vpra veti samiddha āhavanīyaṃ gacchati ca|
vrati: dhanaṃ havi: vjūrṇinī pragāminī|
'jūrṇirjavatervā' (niru. 6. 4) iti yāskenoktatvāt|
vghṛtācī ghṛtenāktā! upastīrṇābhighāritatyartha:|
kiṃca vhonā vurāṇa: uru kurvāṇa: vpra vgūtaṃmanā: prakarṣeṇoyuktamanā: bhavati vya: hotā vnāsatyā aśvinau vhavīrman āhlāne stotre vā vayukta yojayati||
adhi śriye duhitā sūryasya rathaṃ tasthau purubhujā śatotim|
pra māyābhirmāyinā bhūtamātra narā nṛtu janinyajñiyānām||
5||
adhi| śriye| duhitā| sūryasya| ratham| tasthau| puru'bhujā| śata'ūtim| pra| māyābhi:| māyinā| bhūtam| atra| narā| nṛtū iti| janinam| yajñiyānām|| 5||
vpurubhujā he pūrṇabhujau bahūnāṃ rakṣakau vāśvinau yuvayo: vśatotiṃ bahurakṣakaṃ bahugamanaṃ vā vrathaṃ vsūryasya vduhitā sūryā vśriye śrayitum vadhi vtasthau adhyatiṣṭhat|
api ca vyajñiyānāṃ devānām vatra vjaninam asmin janmani prādurbhāve vmāyābhi: prajñānai:|
'śacī māyā' iti prajñānāmasu pāṭhāt|
māyinau prajñāvantau vnarā netārī vnṛtū nṛtyantau vbhṛtaṃ bhavatm|
yadvā|
he māyinau prājñau narau netārī nṛtū nṛtyantāśvinau yajñiyānāṃ devānāmātra janinam janmani prādurbhāve prabhūtaṃ prājñatamamanyādikaṃ devaṃ māyābhi: prajñānai: kauśalairvā jitavantau stha ityartha:|
ayamartha: 'prajāpatiyeṃ somāya rājñe duhitaraṃ prāyacchatsūryāṃ sāvitrīma' (ai. brā. 4.7) ityādinā spaṣṭīkṛta:||
3||
yuvaṃ śrībhirdṛśatābhirbhābhi: śubhe puṣṭimūhathu: sūryāyā:|
pra vāṃ vayo vapuṣe'nu patnakṣadrāṇī suṣṭutā dhiṣṇyā vām||
6||
yuvam| śrībhi:| dṛśatābhi:| ābhi:| śubhe| puṣṭima| ūhathu:| sūryāyā:| pra| vām| vaya:| vapuṣe| anu| patnan| akṣadrāṇī| suṣṭutā| dhiṣṇyā| vām|| 6||
1. kha-gha-jha-na-bha-'ā' nāsti|
2. kha-jha-bha-vyanakti kṛtavantaḥ; ta-hyākkāṃkurvata:; na-vyanaktī- tthanavantaḥ; śa-vyaktīkurvata:|
3. kha-siddha āhavanīyam; ta-śa-samiddhamāhavanīyam|
4. kha-jha- bha-rātivardhanam; na-rātivaddhanam|
5. kha-jha-na-bha-śryartham; gha-śreyārtham|

he aśvinau ^yuvaṃ yuvāṃ ^darśatābhi: darśanīyābhi: ^ābhi: śrīmatāṃ paridṛśyamānābhi: ^śrībhi: lakṣmībhi: kāntibhirvā ^sūryāyā: yuvayo: patnyā: ^śubhe śobhanārtha ^puṣṭiṃ pūrtim ^ūhathu: prāpayatha: |
kiṃca ^vāṃ yuvāṃ ^vaya: aśvā: ^vapuṣe śobhāyai ^pra ^anu ^pasan prakarṣeṇānvapatan |
api ca he ^dhiṣṇyā stutyarhāvaśvinau ^vāṃ yuvāṃ ^suṣṭutā ^vāṇī ṛṣīṇāṃ stuti: ^nakṣatra vyāpnoti ||
ā vāṃ vayo'śvāso vahiṣṭhā abhi prayo nāsatyā vahantu |
pra vāṃ ratho manojavā asarjarṣa: pakṣa iṣidho anu pūrvī: ||
7 ||
ā | vām | vaya: | aśvāsa: | vahiṣṭhā: | abhi | praya: | nāsatyā | vahantu | pra | vām | ratha: | mana:'javā: | asarji | iṣa: | pakṣa: | iṣidha: | anu | pūrvī: || 7 ||
he nāsatyau ^vāṃ yuvāṃ ^vaya: gantāra: ^vahiṣṭhā: voḍhṛtamā: ^aśvāsa: aśvā: ^praya: somarūpa- mannam ^abhi ^ā ^vahantu prāpayantu |
^vāṃ yuvayo: ^manojavā: manovega: ^ratha: ^pakṣa: saṃparcanīyā: ^iṣidha: eṣaṇīyā: ^pūrvī: bahvī: ^iṣa: annāni somān ^anu ^pra ^asarji visṛṣṭa: ||
puru hi vāṃ purubhujā deṣṇaṃ dhenūṃ na iṣaṃ pinvatamasrakrām |
stutaśca vāṃ mādhvī suṣṭutiśca rasāśca ye vāmanu rātimagman ||
8 ||
puru | hi | vām | puru'bhujā | deṣṇam | dhenūm | na: | iṣam | pinvatama | asrakrām | stuta: | ca | vām | mādhvī iti | su'stuti: | ca | rasā: | ca | ye | vām | anu | rātim | agman || 8 ||
^purubhujā he pūrṇabhujau bahupālakau vā ^vāṃ yuvayo: ^deṣṇaṃ dātavyaṃ dhanaṃ ^puru bahu ^hi bhavati |
ataḥ kāraṇāt ^na: asmabhyaṃ ^dhenuṃ prīṇayatrīm ^asrakāṃ mattonyatrāsaṃkramaṇīm |
‘ asrakāmasaṃkra- maṇīm ’ ( niru. 6. 29 ) iti yāskenoktatvāt |
^iṣam annaṃ ^pinvataṃ prayacchatam |
yadvā |
iṣameṣaṇīyāṃ dhenumityanvaya: |
kiṃca ^vāṃ ^stuta: |
stuvantīti stuta: stotāra: |
santi |
^mādhvī madyitārāvaśvinau ^suṣṭuti: śobhanā stuti: ^ca stotṛṣu bhavati |
^rasā: somarasā: ^ca santi |
^ye rasā: ^vāṃ yuvayo: ^rātiṃ dānam ^anu uddiśya ^agman devayajanaṃ prāsā: |
ayaṃ bhāva: |
suṣṭutiṃ śrutvā devayajanaṃ prāsān somarasān pītvā stotṛbhya: kāmān prayacchatamiti śeṣa: ||
uta ma ṛjñe purayasya radhvī sumīḻhe śataṃ pairuke ca pukā |
ṣāṇḍo dādviraṇi: sphiṣṭindarśa vaśāsau abhisācaṃ ṛṣvān ||
9 ||
uta | me | ṛjñe iti | purayasya | radhvī iti | su'mīḻhe | śatam | pairuke | ca | pukā | ṣāṇḍa: | dāt | hiraṇina: | smat'diṣṭin | darśa | vaśāsa: | abhi'sāca: | ṛṣvān ||9||
7uta' api ca 7purasya puranāma kasya svabhūte 7agre ṛjugamane 7raccī kṣipragāminyau vaḍave 7me āśvino: stutiṃ kurvato mama abhūtām |
7surmaḻhe rāśi vidyamānā: santaḥ 7śaṃtaṃ gāvo mamābhū- van |
7peruke rājñi vidyamānāni 7pakkā pakkānyajñāni mamāsan |
7śāṇḍa: ca rājā 7hiraṇina: hira- ṇyavata: 7smadṛṣṭīn praśastadarśanān rathān 7aśvān vā 7daśa daśasaṃkhyākān 7vaśāsa: anuguṇān4 7abhiṣāca: śatrūṇāmabhibhāvukān 7ṛṣvān darśanīyān purupān aśvino: stotṛbhya: 7dāt dadātu ||

10 ||
sam | vām | śatā | nāmatyā | sahasrā | aśvānam | puru'panthā: | gire | dāt | bharat'dvājāyā | vīra | nu | gire | dāt | hatā | rakṣā
sumārgāṇi ⱽsugāni sukhena gamyāni ⱽkṛṇoti karoti |
api ca ⱽmaghonī dhanavatyuṣā: ⱽvasvī praśastā ⱽdakṣiṇā samarthayitrī ca ⱽabhūt ||
bhadrā dadṛkṣa urviyā vi bhāsyutai śocirbhāṃnavo ghārmapasan |
āvirvakṣa: kṛṇuṣe śubhramānoṣau devi rocamānā mahaubhi: ||
2 ||
bhadrā | dadṛkṣe | urviyā | vi | bhāsi | ut | te | śoci: | bhānava: | ghām | apasan | āvi: | vakṣa: | kṛṇuṣe | śubhramānā | urdha: | devi | rocamānā | mahe: | 'bhi: || 2 ||
he uṣo devi ⱽbhadrā kalyāṇī ⱽdadṛkṣe dṛśyase |
ⱽurviyā vistīrṇāṃ ca ⱽvi ⱽbhāsi |
ⱽte tava ⱽśoci: śociṣa: ||
jaso luk ||
dīpyamānā: ⱽbhānava: raśmaya: ⱽghām antarikṣam ⱽut ⱽapasan utpatanti |
kiṃca he ⱽupo ⱽdevi ⱽmahaubhi: tejobhi: ⱽśubhramānā śobhamānā ⱽrocamānā dīpyamānā ⱽvakṣa: tvadīyaṃ rūpam ⱽāvi: ⱽkṛṇuṣe prakaṭīkaroṣi ||
vahanti sīmaruṇāso ruśanto gārva: subhargāmurviyā prathānām |
apejate śūro astaiva śatrūnbādhate tamau ajiro na volhā ||
3 ||
vahanti | sīm | aruṇāsa: | ruśanta: | gārva: | su'bhargām | urviyā | prathānām | apa | ījate | śūra: | asta'iva | śatrūn | bādhate | tama: | ajira: | na | volhā || 3 ||
ⱽaruṇāsa: lohitavarṇā: ⱽruśantaḥ dīpyamānā: ⱽgārva: raśmaya: |
'kiraṇā gāva:' iti raśmināmasu pāṭhāt |
ⱽsubhargām ⱽurviyā urvī vistīrṇāṃ ⱽprathānāṃ prathamānāṃ ⱽsīm enāmuṣodevatāṃ ⱽvahanti |
seyamuṣodevatā ⱽśūro ⱽastaiva vīra: kṣeteva ⱽśatrūn ⱽtama: ⱽapejate apagamayati |
ⱽvolhā senāyā: ⱽajiro ⱽna kṣipragāmī śatrūn yathā ⱽbādhate tathā ⱽtamāṃsi ⱽbādhate ca ||
sugota tai supathā parvateṣvāte apastarsi svabhāno |
sā na ā vaha pṛthuyāmaṛṣve rayiṃ divo duhitariṣayadhyai ||
4 ||
su'gā | uta | te | su'pathā | parvateṣu | avāte | apa: | tarasi | svabhāno iti sva'bhāno | sā | na: | ā | vaha | pṛthu'yāman | ṛṣve | rayim | diva: | duhita: | iṣayadhyai || 4 ||
he uṣo devi ⱽparvateṣu ⱽuta durgeṣvapi ⱽavāte gamanasādhanarahite deśe ⱽsupathā supathāni mārgāṇi ⱽsugā sugāni sukhena gamyāni ⱽte tava bhavanti |
ⱽsvabhāno he svaprakāśe ⱽapa: antarikṣamākāśam |
‘āpa:’ ityantarikṣanāmasu pāṭhāt |
sāmudrikīrapo vā ⱽtarasi |
ⱽpṛthuyāman pṛthurathe ⱽṛṣve darśanīye ⱽdivo ⱽduhita: antarikṣasute he uṣo devi ⱽsā tvam ⱽiṣayadhyai eṣaṇīyaṃ ⱽrayiṃ dhanaṃ ⱽna: asmabhyam ⱽā ⱽvaha ||
1. kha-jha-ta-śa-dṛśyate |
2. kha-gha-jha-ta-na-bha-śa- 'śatrūn' nāsti |
3. kha-gha-jha-ta- na-bha-śa-iva |
sā vaha ukṣabhiravātopo varaṃ vahasi joṣamanu |
tvaṃ divo duhitaryā he devī pūrvahūtau mahanā darśitā bhūḥ ||
5 ||
sā | ā | vaha | yā | ukṣa'bhiḥ | avātā | uṣaḥ | varam | vahasi | joṣam | anu | tvam | divaḥ | duhitaḥ | yā | ha | devī | pūrva'hūtau | mahanā | darśitā | bhūḥ || 5 ||
he uṣaḥ sā tvaṃ varaṃ dhanaṃ mahyam ā vaha |
yā tvam avātā apratigatā joṣam prītim anu ukṣabhiḥ anaḍudbhiraśvairvā stotṛbhyo varaṃ vahasi |
kiṃca he divo duhitaḥ antarikṣasute yā tvaṃ devī dyotamānā pūrvahūtau prathamāhvāne prātaranuvāke mahanā pūjanīyā bhavasi sā tvaṃ darśitā darśanīyā bhūḥ iti yojanā |
yā darśitā bhū rityanvaye yadvṛttena sāmānādhikaraṇyāt bhūrityasya nighālo na syāt ||
utte vayaścidapaptannaraśca ye pitṛbhājau vyuṣṭau |
amā sate vahasi bhūri vāmamūrṣo devi dāśuṣe martyāya ||
6 ||
ut | te | vayaḥ | cit | vasateḥ | apaptan | naraḥ | ca | ye | pitṛ'bhājaḥ | vi'uṣṭau | amā | sate | vahasi | bhūri | vāmam | uṣaḥ | devi | dāśuṣe | martyāya || 6 ||
he uṣo devi te tava vyuṣṭau satyāṃ vayaścit pakṣiṇo'pi vasateḥ nivāsasthānāt ut apaptan utpatanti |
ye ca pitṛbhājaḥ haviṣāṃ saṃbhaktāraḥ annasyopārjakā vā |
'pituḥ' ityannanāmasu pāṭhāt |
naraḥ tai'pi ca utpatanti |
kiṃ ca he uṣo devi tvam amā samīpe sate vartamānāya dāśuṣe haviṣāṃ pradātre martyāya bhūri prabhūtaṃ vāmaṃ dhanaṃ vahasi ||
5 ||
'eṣā syā' iti pañca caturthaṃ sūktaṃ bharadvājasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubham |
anukramyate ca—'eṣā syā' iti |
'upasthaṃ tu' iti pūrvasūkte uktatvāt idamapi upadevatākam |
prātaranuvākāśvina- śastrayorukto viniyogaḥ ||
eṣā syā nau duhitā divojāḥ kṣitīrucchantī mānuṣīrajīgaḥ |
yā bhānunā ruśatā rāmyāskhāyā tirastamasaścidaktūn ||
1 ||
eṣā | syā | naḥ | duhitā | divaḥ'jāḥ | kṣitīḥ | ucchantī | mānuṣīḥ | ajīgaḥ | yā | bhānunā | ruśatā | rāmyāsu | ajñāyi | tiraḥ | tamasaḥ | cit | aktūn || 1 ||
eṣā syā sā divojāḥ divo jātā ata eva duhitā divaḥ putrī uṣāḥ naḥ asmadartham ucchantī tamāṃsi varjayantī kṣitīḥ prajāḥ mānuṣīḥ ajīgaḥ udgirati |
'jigarti- giratikarmā vā' (niru. 6. 8) iti yāskaḥ |
prakāśayatīti yāvat |
yā ruśatā dīpyamānena
1. kha-jha-ta-na-bha-śa-prati |
2. kha-gha-jha-ta-na-bha-śa-vahasi |
ᐁbhānunā yuktā ᐁrāmyāsu yāmyāsu rātriṣu |

vviśveṣāṃ vsatāṃ madhye vjyeṣṭhatamā jyeṣṭhatamau mitrāvaruṇau vvaḥ vāṃ vgīrbhiḥ stutibhiḥ vvavudhadhyai vardhayituṃ pravṛtto'smi |
vyā yau vdvā dvau sahitau vasamā viṣamau |
' tririndra hanti caturaścidrathaḥ ' ( ṛ. saṃ. 1. 152. 2 ) iti nigamāntareṇāsāmyaṃ darśitam |
' yamiṣṭhā yannatamau vhvaiḥ ātmīyai vbāhubhiḥ vraśmeva raśminevāśvān vjanān vsaṃ vyamatuḥ saṃyacchataḥ3 |
bāhubhiriti bahuvacanaṃ pūjārtham ||
iyaṃ maddhāṃ pra stṛṇīte manīṣopa priyā narmasā barhiraccha |
yantaṃ nau mitrāvaruṇāvadhṛṣṭaṃ chardiyardāṃ varudhyaṃ sudānū ||
2 ||
iyam | maddhā | vām | pra | stṛṇīte | manīṣā | upa | priyā | narmasā | barhiḥ | accha | yantam | naḥ | mitrāvaruṇau | ardhem | chardiḥ | yat | vām | varudhyam | sudānū iti su'dānū ||2||
1. gha-jha-ta-na-śa - aspardhante; jha-ya-spardhante |
2. kha-viśveṣāṃ devānām |
3. kha-gha-jha-ta-na-bha-śa-saṃgacchataḥ |
he mitrāvaruṇau ᵥvāṃ yuvāṃ ᵥmat mama ᵥiyaṃ ᵥmanīṣā stutiḥ ᵥpra ᵥstuṇīte pracchādayati |
ᵥpriyā he priyau yuvāṃ ᵥnamasā agnena haviṣā saha ᵥupa gacchati ca |
yuvayoḥ ᵥbarhiḥ yajñam ᵥaccha abhigacchati ca |
he ᵥmitrāvaruṇau ᵥnaḥ asmabhyam ᵥaṣṭam anabhibhūtaṃ ᵥchardiḥ gṛhaṃ ᵥyantaṃ prayacchatam |
he ᵥsudānū śobhanadānau ᵥvāṃ yuvayoḥ saṃbandha yuvabhyāṃ deyaṃ ᵥyat gṛhaṃ ᵥvarūthyaṃ śītavātāpānāṃ vārayitṛ bhavati ||
ā yātaṃ mitrāvaruṇā suśastyūpa priyā narmasā hūyamānā |
saṃ yāvamaḥstho apasaiva janāñchudhīyatatho mahitvā ||
3 ||
ā | ᵥyātam | mitrāvaruṇā | suᵥśasti | upa | priyā | narmasā | hūyamānā | sam | yau | apraḥsthaḥ | apasāᵥiva | janān | śrudhiᵥyataḥ | cit | yataḥ | mahiᵥtvā ||3||
he mitrāvaruṇau ᵥsuśasti śobhanaśaṃsanaṃ ᵥnamasā agnena haviṣā stotreṇa vā ᵥhūyamānā’ stūyamānau vā ᵥpriyā viśvasya priyau ᵥupa ᵥā ᵥyātam upāgacchatam |
yau ᵥmahitvā mahattvena yuktau yuvāṃ ᵥśrudhīyataḥ śrudhimaṃsaṃ yaśo vā''tmana icchato ᵥjanān ᵥamraḥsthaḥ karmaṇyadhikṛtaḥ puruṣaḥ |
‘apaḥ amaḥ ’ iti karmanāmasu pāṭhāt |
ᵥapaseva karmeṇaiva ᵥsaṃ ᵥyataḥḥ saṃyacchathaḥ ||
aśvā na yā vājinā pūtabandhū ṛtā yadgarbhamaditirbharadhyai |
pra yā mahiṃ mahāntā jāryamānā ghorā martīya ripave ni dīdhaḥ ||
4 ||
aśvā | na | yā | vājinā | pūtabandhū iti pūtaᵥbandhū | ṛtā | yat | garbham | aditiḥ | bharadhyai | pra | yā | mahiṃ | mahāntā | jāryamānā | ghorā | matauya | ripave | ni | dīdhariti dīdhaḥ ||4||
ᵥyā yau ᵥaśvā ᵥna aśvāviva ᵥvājinā vājinau balinau ᵥpūtabandhū pūtastautrau ᵥṛtā satyau ca |
ᵥyat yau ᵥgarbhaṃ garbhabhūtau ᵥaditiḥ devī ᵥbharadhyai babhāra |
kiṃca ᵥyā yau ᵥjāyamānā jāyamānāveva ᵥmahi mahataḥ ᵥmahāntā mahattarau prabhūtau mitrāvaruṇau ᵥmatauya martyāya ᵥripave hiṃsakāya ᵥghorā ghorau dhātakau ᵥni ᵥdīdhaḥ nidharāditiḥ svodare ||
viśve yadvoṃ maṃhunā mandamānāḥ kṣatraṃ devāso adadhuḥ sajoṣaḥ |
pari yadbhūtho rodasī cidurvī santi spaśo adadhāso amūrāḥ ||
5 ||
viśve | yat | vām | maṃhunā | mandamānāḥ | kṣatram | devāsaḥ | adadhuḥ | suᵥjoṣaḥ | pari | yat | bhūthaḥ | rodasī iti | cit | urvī iti | santi | spaśaḥ | adadhvāpaḥ | amūrāḥ ||5||
ᵥviśve sarve ᵥdevāsaḥ devāḥ ᵥsajoṣaḥ parasparaṃ prītiyuktāḥ ᵥyat yau ᵥvāṃ yuvāṃ ᵥmaṃhanā mahattvena ᵥmandamānāḥ stuvantaḥ ᵥkṣatraṃ balam ᵥadadhuḥ dhattavantaḥ |
kiṃca ᵥyat yau yuvām ᵥurvī vistīrṇe ᵥrodasī dyāvāpṛthivyau ᵥpari ᵥbhūthaḥ paribhavathaḥ tayoryuvayoḥ ᵥadabdhāsaḥ ahiṃsitāḥ ᵥamūrāḥ amūḍhāḥ ᵥspaśaḥ raśmayaśca vā ᵥsanti ||
5 ||
1. jha-hūyamānā āhūyamānau |
tā hi kṣatraṃ dhārayethe anu dyūndhethe sānumupamādivaṃ dyoḥ | dṛḻho nakṣatra uta viśvadevo bhūmimātāndhāṃ dhāsināyoḥ || 6 || tā | hi | kṣatram | dhārayethe iti | anu | dyūn | dṛhethe iti | sānum | upamāt'iva | dyoḥ | dṛḻhaḥ | nakṣatraḥ | uta | viśvadevaḥ | bhūmim | ā | atān | dyām | dhāsinā | āyoḥ ||6||
^tā tau mitrāvaruṇau yuvām ^anu ^dyūn divase divase ^kṣatraṃ balaṃ ^dhārayethe ^hi |
^dyoḥ antarikṣasya ^sānuṃ samucchritapradeśaṃ meghaṃ sūryaṃ vā ^dṛhethe dṛḍhīkurutam |
^upamādiva |
upa- mīyate prakṣipyate iti upamāt sthūṇā |
sā yathā vatsaṃ dhārayati tadvadityarthaḥ |
^dṛḻhaḥ yuvābhyāṃ dṛḍhīkṛtaśca meghaḥ ^nakṣatraḥ antarikṣaṃ vyāpnuvānaḥ ^uta api ca ^viśvadevaḥ sūryaḥ ^bhūmiṃ ^yāṃ divaṃ ca ^āyoḥ manuṣyasya |
'duhavaḥ āyavaḥ' iti manuṣyanāmasu pāṭhāt |
^dhāsinā annena haviṣā |
'dhūna dhāsiḥ' ityannanāmasu pāṭhāt |
āpyāyitaḥ san ^ātān ātanoti yuvābhyāṃ dṛḍhīkṛta eva ||
tā vigraṃ dhaithe jaṭharaṃ puṇadhyā ā yatsabra sabhṛtayaḥ puṇanti | na mṛṣyante yuvatayo'vātā vi yatpayo viśvajinvā bharante || 7 || tā | vigram | dhaithe iti | jaṭharam | puṇadhyai | ā | yat | sabra | sa'bhṛtayaḥ | puṇanti | na | mṛṣyante | yuvatayaḥ | avātā | vi | yat | payaḥ | viśva'jinvā | bharante || 7 ||
^tā tau mitrāvaruṇau yuvāṃ ^vigraṃ prājñaṃ ^yajamānam |
'vipraḥ vigraḥ' iti medhāvināmasu pāṭhāt |
^dhaithe dhārayathaḥ ^jaṭharam udaraṃ ^puṇadhyai somena pūrayitum |
^yat yadā ^sabhṛtayaḥ saha śriyamāṇā ṛtvijaḥ ^sanna yajñasadanam ^ā ^puṇanti āpūrayanti |
^viśvajinvā he viśvajinvānau ^yat yadā ^payaḥ jalaṃ bhavanmaghaṃ prahitaṃ tadā ^yuvatayaḥ nadyaḥ diśo vā ^na ^mṛṣyante rajasā nābhibhūyante |
^avātāḥ aśukvāśva ^tri ^bharante vibhūtiṃ dhārayanti ||
tā jihvayā sadamedaṃ sumedhā ā yadvān satyo aratirṛte bhūta | tadvāṃ mahitvaṃ ghṛtānnāvastu yuvaṃ dāśuṣe vi cayiṣṭamahaḥ || 8 || tā | jihvayā | sadam | idam | sumedhāḥ | ā | yat | vām | satyaḥ | aratiḥ | ṛte | bhūta | tat | vām | mahitvam | ghṛta'annau | astu | yuvam | dāśuṣe | vi | cayiṣṭam | ahaḥ || 8 ||
^tā tau mitrāvaruṇau yuvāṃ ^sumedhāḥ prājñaḥ ^jihvayā vācā ^sadaṃ sarvadā ^idaṃ jalam ^ā yācate |
ā ityupasargadarśanāt yogyakriyādhyāhārāt yācata iṣyadhyāhiyate |
pūrvasyāmṛci jalasya prakṛtatvāt buddhau viparivartamānaṃ jalamidaṃśabdena parāmṛśyate |
^yat yadā ^vāṃ yuvayoḥ ^aratiḥ abhigantā ^ṛte yajñe ^satyaḥ māyārahitaḥ ^ā ^bhūta ābhavati he ^ghṛtānnau ^tat ^mahitvam apekṣitapradātṛtvalakṣaṇaṃ ^vāṃ yuvayoḥ ^astu |
kiṃca ^yuvaṃ yuvāṃ ^dāśuṣe dāśuṣo yajamānasya ||
ṣaṣṭhyarthe caturthī ||
^ahaḥ pāpaṃ ^vi ^cayiṣṭaṃ vināśayatamityarthaḥ ||
1. kha-bha-medhāvinam |
pra yadvāṃ mitrāvaruṇā spūrdhan priyā dhāma yuvadhitā minanti | na ye devāsa ohasā na martā aryajñasāco apyo na putrāḥ || 9 || pra | yat | vām | mitrāvaruṇā | spūrdhan | priyā | dhāma | yuva'dhitā | minanti | na | ye | devāsaḥ | ohasā | na | martāḥ | aryajña'sācaḥ | apyaḥ | na | putrāḥ || 9 ||
he mitrāvaruṇau vāṃ yuvayoḥ priyā priyāṇi dhāma dhāmāni karmāṇi yuvadhitā yuvābhyāṃ vihitāni yat ye ayajanto janāḥ spūrdhan spardhamānāḥ pra minanti hiṃsanti |
ye ca devāsaḥ devāḥ ohasā vahanasādhanena stotreṇa na yujyante |
martāḥ manuṣyāśca ohasā na yujyante |
ye ca apyaḥ apaḥ karma |
tadahantaḥ aryajñasācaḥ na r yajñayuktāḥ vṛthākarmāṇi kurvanta ityarthaḥ |
ye ca na putrāḥ |
pṛṇantīti putrāḥ |
na putrā apṛṇanta ityarthaḥ |
tān sarvān vi cayiṣṭamityanupajyate ||
vi yadvācaṃ kīptāso bharante śaṃsanti ke cinnividoṃ manānāḥ | ādvāṃ bravāma satyānyukthā narkirdevebhiryatatho mahitvā || 10 || vi | yat | vācam | kīptāsaḥ | bharante | śaṃsanti | ke | cit | ni'vidaḥ | manānāḥ | āt | vām | bravāma | satyāni | ukthā | narkiḥ | devebhiḥ | yatathaḥ | mahitvā || 10 ||
yat kīptāsaḥ medhāvina udgātāraḥ |
uśijaḥ kīptāsaḥ iti medhāvināmasu pāṭhāt |
vācaṃ stutiṃ vi bharante pṛthak uccārayanti tadā ke cit bahucāḥ manānāḥ adyādīn stuvantaḥ nividaḥ śaṃsanti |
āt tathā sati vāṃ yuvābhyāṃ satyāni ukthāni stotrāṇi vayaṃ bravāma |
mahiravā mahasvena devebhiḥ anyairdevaiḥ saha nakiḥ yatathaḥ na gacchathaḥ ||
avoritthā vāṃ chardiṣo abhiṣṭau yuvomitrāvaruṇāvaskṛdhoyu | anu yadvāvaḥ spurānjipyaṃ dhṛṣṇuṃ yadraṇe vṛṣaṇaṃ yunarjana || 11 || avaḥ | itthā | vām | chardiṣaḥ | abhiṣṭau | yuvaḥ | mitrāvaruṇau | askṛdhoyu | anu | yat | gāvaḥ | spurān | ṛjipyam | dhṛṣṇum | yat | raṇe | vṛṣaṇam | yunarjana || 11 ||
he mitrāvaruṇau avoḥ avataḥ vāṃ yuvayoḥ abhiṣṭau abhigamane chardiṣaḥ gṛhāya sukhāya vā |
caturthyarthe ṣaṣṭhī |
yuvāmabhigateṣu stotṛṣu yuvoḥ yuvayoḥ saṃbandhaṃ yuvābhyāṃ deyaṃ taddvādikam askṛdhoyu avicchinnaṃ bhavati |
tathā ca yāskaḥ— askṛdhoyurṛkudhāyuḥ |
kṛdhivati hrasvanāma nikṛttaṃ bhavati ( niru. 6. 3 ) iti |
itthā satyameva |
kadā mitrāvaruṇayorabhigamanamityata āha |
yat yadā gāvaḥ stutayaḥ anu spurān anusphureyuḥ |
yadā ca ṛjipyam ṛjugāminaṃ dhṛṣṇuṃ śatrūṇāṃ dharṣakaṃ vṛṣaṇaṃ kāmānāṃ varṣitāraṃ somaṃ raṇe ramaṇe yajñe yunarjana yajamānā yuñjyuḥ ||
1. kha-jha-ta-bha-ye yajaḥ; na-ye yajñāḥ; śa-ye yajamānāḥ |
2. kha-jha-ta-na-bha-śa-yajñayuktā na |
3. kha-ta-na-śa-kurvantītyarthaḥ; jha-bha-kurvatītyarthaḥ |
4. kha-jha-na-bha-saṃbandhi nāsti |
5. kha-jha-na-bha-tadā |
‘ śruṣṭī vām ’ ityekādaśarcaṃ saptamaṃ sūktaṃ bharadvājasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamaindrāvaruṇam |
tathā cānukramyate - ‘ śruṣṭī vāmaindrāvaruṇamupāntye jagatyau ’ iti |
‘ pra samnāje, indrāvaruṇā sutapau ’ ityete jagatyau śiṣṭā- striṣṭubhaḥ |
indrāvaruṇau devatā |
ābhilvikepūktheṣu tṛtīyasavane hotrakaśastreṣu stomavṛddhau maitrā- varuṇasyedamāvāpārthaṃ sūktam |
sūtritaṃ ca - ‘ śruṣṭī vāṃ yajño yuvāṃ narā ’ ( āśva. śrau. 7. 9 ) iti |
tṛtīyasavane ‘ indrāvaruṇā sutapāvimaṃ sutam ’ iti praśāstuḥ prasthitayājyā |
sūtritaṃ ca - ‘ indrāvaruṇā sutapāvimaṃ sutamindraśca somaṃ pibata bṛhaspate ’ ( āśva. śrau. 5. 5 ) iti |
ukthye tṛtīyasavane ‘ indrāvaruṇā madhumattamasya ’ iti maitrāvaruṇasya śastrāyājyā |
sūtritaṃ ca - ‘ indrāvaruṇā madhumatta- masyeti yājyā ’ ( āśva. śrau. 6. 1 ) iti ||
śruṣṭī vāṃ yajña udyataḥ sajoṣo manuṣvadvṛktabarhiṣo yajadhyai | ā ya indrāvaruṇāviṣe adya mahe sumnāya mahe āvivartat || 1 || śruṣṭī | vām | yajñaḥ | uta'dyataḥ | sa'joṣaḥ | manuṣvat | vṛkta'barhiṣaḥ | yajadhyai | ā | yaḥ | indrāvaruṇau | iṣe | adya | mahe | sumnāya | mahe | ā'vivartat || 1 ||
v mahe maṃhanīyau he v indrāvaruṇau v vāṃ yuvayorthe v śruṣṭī kṣipraḥ |
tathā ca yāskaḥ - ‘ śruṣṭīti kṣipranāmāṣṭau aṣṭīti ’ ( niru. 6. 12 ) |
v yajñaḥ somaḥ |
v sajoṣaḥ ṛtvigbhiḥ sahitaḥ |
v adya v ā samantāt v udyataḥ pravṛttaḥ |
v yaḥ somaḥ v manuṣvat manoriva v vṛktabarhiṣaḥ stīrṇabarhiṣaḥ yajamānasya v iṣe annāya v mahe mahate v sumnāya sukhāya ca v yajadhyai yaṣṭuṃ vām v āvavartat āvartayati ||
tā hi śreṣṭhe devatātā tujā śūrāṇāṃ śaviṣṭhā tā hi bhūtam | maghonāṃ maṃhiṣṭhā tuviśuṣma ṛtena vṛtraturā sarvasenā || 2 || tā | hi | śreṣṭhe | deva'tātā | tujā | śūrāṇām | śaviṣṭhā | tā | hi | bhūtam | maghonām | maṃhiṣṭhā | tuvi'śuṣmā | ṛtena | vṛtra'turā | sarva'senā || 2 ||
he indrāvaruṇau v tā tau prasiddhau yuvāṃ v śreṣṭha śreṣṭhau v devatātā devatāto yajñe |
‘ iṣṭeḥ devatātā ’ iti yajñanāmasu pāṭhāt |
v tujā dhanānāṃ prerakau v hi v bhūtam bhavatam |
kiṃca v tā tau v hi yuvāṃ v śūrāṇāṃ madhye v śaviṣṭhā atiśayena balavantau bhūtam |
kiṃca v maghonāṃ dānavatāṃ v maṃhiṣṭhā dātṛtamau v tuviśuṣmā bahubalau v ṛtena satyena v vṛtraturā śatrūṇāṃ hiṃsakau v sarvasenā kṛtsnasainau ca bhūtamiti ||
tā gṛṇīhi namasyebhiḥ śubhaiḥ sumnebhirindrāvaruṇā cakānā | vajreṇānyaḥ śavasā hanti vṛtraṃ sirpattyanyo vṛjaneṣu vipraḥ || 3 || tā | gṛṇīhi | namasyebhiḥ | śubhaiḥ | sumnebhiḥ | indrāvaruṇā | cakānā | vajreṇa | anyaḥ | śavasā | hanti | vṛtram | sirpat | anyaḥ | vṛjaneṣu | vipraḥ || 3 ||
2. kha-jña-na-bha-maghonāṃ devānām |
he bharadvāja ᵛnamasyebhiḥ stutyaiḥ ᵛśūyaiḥ balaiḥ |
‘śūyam’ iti balanāmasu pāṭhāt |
ᵛsuṣṭubhiḥ suṣṭaiśca sarvaiḥ ᵛcakānā stutau|
kāyatiḥ śabdakarmā |
tasya liṭaḥ kānaci rūpamidam |
ᵛtā tāvindrā- varuṇau ᵛgṛṇīhi stuhi |
ᵛanyaḥ tayoranya indraḥ ᵛvṛtraṃ vṛtranāmakamasuraṃ ᵛvajreṇa ᵛhanti |
ᵛanyaḥ varuṇaḥ ᵛvipraḥ prājñaḥ ᵛvrajaneṣu stotṛṇāmudraveṣu rakṣituṃ ᵛśavasā balena ᵛsipakti saṃgacchate |
āśca yanaraśca vāvṛdhanta viśve devāso narāṃ svargūrtāḥ |
praibhya indrāvaruṇā mahitvā dyauśca pṛthivi bhūtamurvī |
4 |
āḥ | ca | yat | naraḥ | ca | vṛtravantaḥ | viśve | devāsaḥ | narām | svadūrtāḥ | pra | ebhyaḥ | indrāvaruṇā | mahi'tvā | dyauḥ | ca | pṛthivi | bhūtam | urvī iti || 4 ||
ᵛnarāṃ nṛṇāṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ madhye ᵛāśca striyaśca |
‘menā ā iti strīgām’ (niha. 3.21) iti yāskavacanāt |
ᵛnaraśca puruṣāśca ᵛviśve sarve ᵛdevāsaḥ devāḥ stotāraḥ |
devāḥ ityatra ‘pumān striyā’ ityekaśeṣāt devyaśca devāśca devāsa iti nirdeśaḥ puṃvannirdeśaḥ |
ᵛsvagūrtāḥ svayamucyatāḥ ᵛyat yadā ᵛvāvṛdhanta stutibhirvardhayante’ tadā he indrāvaruṇau ᵛmahitvā mahaccayuktī ᵛebhyaḥ stotṛbhyaḥ ᵛpra ᵛbhūtaṃ prabhavatam |
he ᵛdyauḥ ᵛpṛthivi ᵛca ubhe ᵛurvī vistīrṇe yuvāmebhyaḥ pra bhūtamityanupaṅgaḥ |
sa itsudānuḥ svavā~ ṛtāvendrā yo vāṃ varuṇa dāśati tman |
iṣā sa dviṣastaredāsvānvaṃsadrayi rayivataśca janān |
5 |
saḥ | it | sudānuḥ | sva'vān | ṛta'vā | indrā | yaḥ | vām | varuṇa | dāśati | tman | iṣā | saḥ | dviṣaḥ | taret | dāsvān | vaṃsat | rayim | rayi'vataḥ | ca | janān || 5 ||
he ᵛindrā indrau ᵛvaruṇā he varuṇau |
itaretarayogādubhayatra dvivacanaprayogaḥ |
varuṇa ityatra saṃhitāyāṃ hasvaśchāndasaḥ |
ᵛvāṃ yuvābhyāṃ ᵛyaḥ yajamānaḥ ᵛtman ātmanaiva ᵛdāśati havīṃṣi dadāti ᵛsa ᵛit sa eva ᵛsudānuḥ śobhanadāno bhavati |
ᵛsvavān dhanavāṃśca ᵛṛtāvā yajñavāṃśca bhavati |
kiṃca ᵛsaḥ ᵛdāsvān dānavān ᵛdviṣaḥ dveṣṭuḥ ᵛiṣā annena jayalabdhena saha ᵛtaret prāpyeta |
api ca ᵛrayiṃ dhanaṃ ᵛrayivataḥ dhanavataḥ ᵛjanān putrān ᵛca ᵛvaṃsat saṃbhajet’ |
11 |
yaṃ yuvaṃ dāśvadharāya devā rayiṃ dhattho vasumataṃ purukṣum |
asme sa indrāvaruṇāpi dhyātra yo bhanakti vṛtramaśastīḥ |
6 |
yam | yuvam | dāśu'adharāya | devā | rayim | dhatthaḥ | vasu'mantam | puru'kṣum | asme iti | saḥ | indrāvaruṇau | api | syāt | pra | yaḥ | bhanakti | vṛtram | aśastīḥ || 6 ||
ᵛdevā devau stūyamānau he ᵛindrāvaruṇau ᵛyuvaṃ yuvāṃ ᵛdāśvadharāya dattahaviṣkāya ᵛvasumataṃ dhanānubandhinaṃ ᵛpurukṣuṃ bahulaṃ pūrṇayaśaskaṃ vā ᵛyaṃ yādṛśaṃ ᵛrayiṃ dhanaṃ ᵛdhatthaḥ prayacchathaḥ |
ᵛyaḥ ᵛapi ca ᵛvadhuṣāṃ hisakānām ᵛaśastīḥ akīrtīḥ |
śatrukṛtāntyayaśaṃsītyarthaḥ |
ᵛpra ᵛbhanakti prahajati ᵛsaḥ tādṛśo rayiḥ ᵛasme asmāsu ᵛsyāt bhūyāt |
1. ya-stulā vardhante vardhayanti |
2. kha-jha-bha-saṃbhajate; na-śa-saṃbhajeta |
ṛ. 3-16
uta naḥ sutrātro devagaupā: sūribhya indrāvaruṇā rayi: syāt | yeṣāṃ śubhma: pṛrtanāsu sāhānta sadyo yumnā tirate taturi: || 7 || uta | na: | su'trātra: | deva'gaupā: | sūri'bhya: | indrāvaruṇā | rayi: | syāt | yeṣām | śubhma: | pṛrtanāsu | sāhān | pra | sadya: | yumnā | tiratai | taturi: || 7 || vuta api ca vindrāvaruṇā he indrāvaruṇau vsūribhya: stotṛbhya: vna: asmabhyaṃ vsutrātra: surakṣa: vdevagopā: | devā gopā goptāro yasya sa tathāvidha: | vrayi: dhanaṃ vsyāt bhūyāt | kiṃca vyeṣām asmākaṃ vśubhma: balaṃ vpṛrtanāsu yuddheṣu vsāhān śatrūṇāmabhibhavitā vtaturi: hiṃsakaśca san vyumnā yaśāṃsi vsadya: eva vpra vtirate samyak tiraskuryāt ||
nū na indrāvaruṇā gṛṇānā pṛktaṃ rayiṃ sauśravasāya devā | itthā gṛṇantau mahinasya śardho'po na nāvā duritā tarema || 8 || nu | na: | indrāvaruṇā | gṛṇānā | pṛktam | rayim | sauśravasāya | devā | itthā | gṛṇanta: | mahinasya | śardha: | apa: | na | nāvā | du:'itā | tarema || 8 || haṃ vindrāvaruṇā indrāvaruṇau vgṛṇānā stūyamānau yuvāṃ vna: asmabhyaṃ vsauśravasāya | suśra- vaso bhāva: sauśravasam | tasmai suśravastvāya vrayiṃ dhanaṃ vnu kṣipraṃ vpṛktaṃ prayacchatam | kiṃca vdevā he devau vmahinasya mahato'vayavo: vśardha: balam vitthā itthaṃ vgṛṇanta: stuvanto vayaṃ vduritā duritāni vnāvā vapo vna udakānīva vtarema ||
pra samrājai bṛhate manma nu priyamarca devāya varuṇāya supratha: | ayaṃ ya urvī mahinā mahivrata: kratvā vibhātyajaro na śociṣā || 9 || pra | sam'rājai | bṛhate | manma | nu | priyam | arca | devāya | varuṇāya | su'pratha: | ayam | ya: | urvī iti | mahinā | mahi'vrata: | kratvā | vi'bhāti | ajare: | na | śociṣā ||9|| he stota: vsamrāje ājñayā rājāṃ śāsakāya vbṛhate parivṛḍhāya vdevāya dyotamānāya vvaruṇāya vpriyaṃ manoharaṃ vmapratha: sarvataḥ pṛthu | tathā ca yāska:—‘saprathā: sarvataḥ pṛthu:’ ( nigha. 6. 7 ) iti | vmanma stotraṃ vnu adya vpra varca proccāraya | vayaṃ vya: yo'yaṃ varuṇa: vmahinā mahimnā yukta: vmahivrata: mahākarmā vkratvā prajñayā ca yukta: vajaro vna jarārahitaśca vurvī vistīrṇe dyāvāpṛthivyau vśociṣā dīpyā vvibhāti vibhāsayati ||
indrāvaruṇā sutapātrimaṃ sutaṃ somaṃ pibataṃ madye dhṛtavratā | yuvo rathau adhvaraṃ devavītaye prati svasaramupa yāti pītaye || 10 || indrāvaruṇā | suta'pau | imam | sutam | somam | pibatam | madyam | dhṛta'vratā | yuva: | ratha: | adhvaram | deva'vītaye | prati | svasaram | upa | yāti | pītaye || 10 || indrāvaruṇā | suta'pau | imam | sutam | somam | pibatam | madyam | dhṛta'vratā | yuva: | ratha: | adhvaram | deva'vītaye | prati | svasaram | upa | yāti | pītaye || 10 || 1. kha-bha-sauśravasāya; jha-na-suyaasvasthāya; śa-suyaśastvāya | 2. kha-jha-na-bha-mahivrato mahāvrata: |
vindrāvaruṇā he indrāvaruṇau vsutapau sutasya somasya pātārī yuvāṃ vmathaṃ madakaraṃ vsutam abhiputam vimaṃ vsomaṃ vpiyatama |
kiṃca vsutayatā he sutakarmāṇau vyuvoḥ yuvayoḥ vrathaḥ vadhvaram uddiśya vdevavītaye devānāṃ pānārthaṃ vpītaye yuvayorapi somapānārthaṃ vsvasaraṃ mārgaṃ vprati vudha vyāti ||
indrāvaruṇā madhumattamasya vṛṣṇaḥ somasya vṛṣaṇā vṛṣethām |
idaṃ vāmandhaḥ pariṣiktamusme āsadyāsminbarhiṣi mādayethām ||
11 ||
indrāvaruṇā | madhumata'tamasya | vṛṣṇaḥ | somasya | vṛṣaṇā | ā | vṛṣethām | idam | vām | andhaḥ | pari'siktam | asme iti | ā'sadya | asmin | barhiṣi | mādayethām || 11 ||
vindrāvaruṇā he indrāvaruṇau vvṛṣaṇā kāmānāṃ varṣitārau yuvāṃ vmadhumattamasya atiśayena madhumantaṃ ramavantaṃ vvṛṣṇa
yā viśvāsāṃ janitārā matīnāmindrāviṣṇū kalaśā somadhānāṃ | pra vāṃ giraḥ śasyamānā avantu pra stomāso gīyamānāso arkaiḥ || 2 || yā | viśvāsāṃ | janitārā | matīnām | indrāviṣṇū iti | kalaśā | soma'dhānāṃ | pra | vām | giraḥ | śasyamānāḥ | avantu | pra | stomāsaḥ | gīyamānāsaḥ | arkaiḥ || 2 || 7yā yau prasiddhāṃ 7indrāviṣṇū 7vidhāmāṃ sarvāsāṃ 7matīnāṃ stutīnāṃ 7janitārā janitārau || ‘jana janane’ ityasmān dhātoḥ tṛci idaṃ rūpam | yadvā | ‘janī prādurbhāve’ ityasmāt dhātoreva tṛci ‘janitā mantre’ iti nipānāt ṇilopaḥ || 7kalaśā kalaśāṃ 7somadhānā somasya nidānabhūtāṃ ca syānām | atha pratyakṣastutiḥ | he indrāviṣṇū 7vāṃ yuvāṃ 7śasyamānāḥ uccāryamāṇāḥ śaśvarūpāḥ 7giraḥ vācaḥ 7pra 7avantu prāpnuvantu | 7arkaiḥ stotṛbhiḥ 7gīyamānāsaḥ gīyamānāḥ 7stomāsaḥ stomāḥ stotrāṇi ca 7pra avantu prāpnuvantu ||
indrāviṣṇū madapatī madānāṃ somaṃ yātaṃ draviṇo dadhānā | saṃ vāmañjantvaktubhirmatīnāṃ saṃ stomāsaḥ śasyamānāsa ukthaiḥ || 3 || indrāviṣṇū iti | madapatī iti mada'patī | madānām | ā | somam | yātam | draviṇo iti | dadhānā | mam | vām | añjantu | aknu'bhiḥ | matīnām | sam | stomāsaḥ | śasyamānāsaḥ | ukthaiḥ ||3|| he 7indrāviṣṇū 7madānāṃ somānāṃ 7madapatī svāminau | madapatī ityatra madaśabdaḥ anuvādaḥ | 7draviṇo draviṇāni dhanānyapi || draviṇā u iti padadvayamekībhūya draviṇo iti niṣpannaṃ padam || 7dadhānā dadānau yuvāṃ 7somam abhi2 7ā 7yātam | 7matīnāṃ stotṛṇāṃ 7stomāsaḥ stotrāṇi 7ukthaiḥ śastraiḥ sārdhaṃ 7śasyamānāsaḥ uccāryamāṇāḥ 7vāṃ yuvām 7aktubhiḥ tejobhiḥ 7sam 7añjantu samarthayantu | saṃ samiti dviruktirādarārthā ||
ā vāmaśvāso abhimātipāha indrāviṣṇū sadhamādau vahantu | juṣethāṃ viśvā havanā matīnāmupa brahmāṇi śṛṇutaṃ giro me || 4 || ā | vām | aśvāsaḥ | abhimāti'sāhaḥ | indrāviṣṇū iti | sadha'mādau | vahantu | juṣethām | viśvā | havanā | matīnām | upa | brahmāṇi | śṛṇutam | giraḥ | me || 4 || he 7indrāviṣṇū 7vāṃ yuvām 7abhimātipāhaḥ abhimātīnāṃ hiṃsakānāmibhavitāraḥ 7sadhamādaḥ saha mādyantaḥ 7aśvāsaḥ aśvāḥ 7ā 7vahantu | 7matīnāṃ stotṛṇāṃ 7viśvā viśvāni sarvāṇi 7havanā havanāni stotrāṇi 7juṣethāṃ sevethām | tadartha 7me madīyāni 7brahmāṇi stotrāṇi 7giraḥ śaśvarūpā vācaśca 7upa 7śṛṇutam ||
1. jha-na-śa-dremadaśabdaḥ |
2. kha-bha-prati |
3. jha-ta-na-ya-śa-samajantu |
indrāviṣṇū tatpanyāyyaṃ vāṃ somasya madaṃ uru cakramāthe | akṛṇutamantarikṣaṃ varīyo'prathataṃ jīvaseṃ no rajāṃsi || 5 || indrāviṣṇū iti | tat | panyāyyam | vām | somasya | madam | uru | cakramāthe iti | akṛṇutam | antarikṣam | varīyaḥ | aprathatam | jīvaseṃ | naḥ | rajāṃsi || 5 || he indrāviṣṇū vāṃ yuvayoḥ tat karma panyāyyaṃ stutyam | kiṃ tat karmetyata āha | somasya made sati yat uru vistīrṇaṃ cakramāthe vicakramāthe | yadyapi viṣṇoreva vikramaḥ tathāpi ekārthatvādubhayostviṣyucyate | yacca antarikṣaṃ varīyaḥ uhataram akṛṇutam akurūtam | yadapi ca rajāṃsi lokān sarvān | 'lokā rajāṃsyucyante' iti yāskaḥ | naḥ asmākaṃ jīvase jīvanāya aprathatam aprathayatam ||
indrāviṣṇū haviṣā vāvṛdhānāgrādvānā narmasā rātahavyā | ghṛtāsutī draviṇaṃ dhatta masme samudraḥ sthaḥ kalaśaḥ somadhānaḥ || 6 || indrāviṣṇū iti | haviṣā | vāvṛdhānā | agra'advānā | narmasā | rāta'havyā | ghṛtāsutī iti ghṛta'āsutī | draviṇam | dhattam | asme iti | samudraḥ | sthaḥ | kalaśaḥ | soma'dhānaḥ || 6 || ghṛtāsutī ghṛtākṣau indrāviṣṇū yuvāṃ haviṣā somena vāvṛdhānā vardhamānau agradvānā somāgrasya śukrasya attārau namasā namaskāreṇa rātahavyā dattahaviṣko yajamānaiḥ asme asmabhyaṃ draviṇaṃ dhanaṃ dhattaṃ prayacchatam | kiṃca yuvāṃ samudraḥ udadhiriva sthaḥ bhavataḥ | yataḥ somadhānaḥ somanidhānaḥ kalaśaḥ bhavataḥ ||
indrāviṣṇū pibartaṃ madhvo asya somasya dastrā jaṭharaṃ pṛṇethām | ā vāmandhāṃsi madirāṇyagman brahmāṇi śṛṇutaṃ havaṃ me || 7 || indrāviṣṇū iti | pibartam | madhvaḥ | asya | somasya | dastrā | jaṭharam | pṛṇethām | ā | vām | andhāṃsi | madirāṇi | agman | upa | brahmāṇi | śṛṇutam | havam | me || 7 || dastrā he darśanīyau indrāviṣṇū yuvāṃ madhvaḥ asya madakaramimaṃ somasya somaṃ pibartam | kiṃca jaṭharam udaraṃ pṛṇethāṃ somena pūrayethām | he indrāviṣṇū vāṃ yuvāṃ madirāṇi madakarāṇi andhāṃsi somātmakānyannāni ā agman abhigacchantu | me madīyāni brahmāṇi stotrāṇi havaṃ madīyamāhvānaṃ ca upa śṛṇutam ||
1. kha-gha-jha-na-bha-śa- 'vicakramāthe' nāsti |
2. kha-gha-jha-na-bha-somasya |
3. kha-gha-jha-na-bha-śa- 'madhvo...yuvāṃ' nāsti; gha-madhvaḥ madhurasya madhuramasya somasya imaṃ somaṃ pibaṃtaṃ jaṭharaṃ pṛṇethāṃ pūrayethām |
vāṃ yuvām; ta-asya somasya imaṃ somaṃ madhvaḥ madhuraṃ pibaṃtam |
tena ca jaṭharamudaraṃ somena pṛṇethāṃ pūraye- thām |
he indrāviṣṇū vāṃ yuvām |
ubhā jigyathurna parā jayethe na parā jigye kataraścanainoḥ |
indraśca viṣṇo yadapaspṛdhethāṃ tredhā sahasraṃ vi tadaireyām ||
8 ||
ubhā | jigyathuḥ | na | parā | jayethe iti | na | parā | jigye | kataraḥ | cana | enoḥ | indraḥ | ca | viṣṇo iti | yat | apaspṛdhethām | tredhā | sahasram | vi | tat | aireyām || 8 ||
he indrāviṣṇū ˅ubhā ubhau yuvāṃ ˅jigyathuḥ śatrūnajaiṣma |
˅na ˅parā ˅jayethe na punaḥ kadācana parājayethe |
˅enoḥ enayoryuvayormadhye ˅kataraścana ekataro'pi ˅na ˅parā ˅jigye na parājaiṣṭa |
he ˅viṣṇo ˅indraśca ˅tvaṃ ca yuvāṃ ˅yat yadvastu prati ˅apaspṛdhethām asuraiḥ sahāspṛdhethāṃ ˅tredhā lokavedavāgātmanā tridhā sthitaṃ ˅sahasram amitaṃ ca ˅vi ˅tat ˅aireyāṃ vyakramethāmityarthaḥ |
tathā ca brāhmaṇam—‘ubhā jigyathurityācchāvākasyāmau hi tau jigyaturna parā jayethe na parā jigye indraśca viṣṇo yadapaspṛdhethāṃ tredhā sahasraṃ vi tadaireyāmitīndraśca vai viṣṇuścasurairyuyudhāte tān ha sma jitvācatuḥ kalpāmahā iti te ha tathaiyasurā ūcuḥ so'bravīdindro yāvadevāyaṃ viṣṇūścavikramate tāvadasmākamatha yuṣmākamitaraditi sa imā~llokānvicakrame'tho vedānatho vācaṃ tadāhuḥ kiṃ tatsahasra- mitīme lokā ime vedā atho vāgiti brūyādaireyāmaireyām’ ( ai. brā. 6-15 ) iti |
yadvā |
tat sahasraṃ gavāṃ sahasraṃ tredhā vyarayethāmityarthaḥ |
13 ||
‘ghṛtavatī bhuvanānām’ iti ṣaḍṛcaṃ navamaṃ sūktaṃ bharadvājasyārṣaṃ jāgataṃ dyāvāpṛthivīdevatākam |
tathā cānukramyate—‘ghṛtavatī ṣad dyāvāpṛthivīyaṃ 2 jāgatam’ iti |
ābhiplavike pañcame'hani vaiśvadeva- śastra ādyastṛco dyāvāpṛthivyanividvānārthaḥ |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘ghṛtavatī bhuvanānāmabhiśriyendra ṛbhubhi- rvājavanniti tṛcau’ ( āśva. śrau. 7 . 7 ) iti |
bṛhaspatisave'pi vaiśvadevaśastra eṣa tṛcaḥ |
sūtritaṃ ca ( āśva. śrau. 9 . 5 ) |
ābhiplavike tṛtīye'hani vaiśvadevaśastre ‘ghṛtena dyāvāpṛthivī’ iti tisro dyāvāpṛthivyanividvānārthāḥ |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘ghṛtena dyāvāpṛthivī iti tisra'naśvo jātaḥ’ ( āśva. śrau. 7 . 7 ) iti ||
ghṛtavatī bhuvanānāmabhiśriyaurvī pṛthvī madhudughe supeśāḥ |
dyāvāpṛthivī varuṇasya dharmaṇā viskaṃbhite ajare bhūriretasā ||
1 ||
ghṛtavatī iti ghṛta'vatī | bhuvanānām | abhi'śriyāṃ | urvī iti | pṛthvī iti | madhudughe iti madhu'dughe | su'peśasā | dyāvāpṛthivī iti | varuṇasya | dharmaṇā | viskaṃbhite iti vi'skaṃbhite | ajare iti | bhūri'retasā || 1 ||
1. kha-jha-ta-śa-indra; gha-indraḥ |
2. kha-jha-na-bha-dyāvāpṛthivyam |
^dyāvāpṛthivī dyāvāpṛthivyau ^ghṛtavatī dīptimatyau udakavatyau vā |
‘vanaṃ ghṛtam’ ityudaka- nāmasu pāṭhāt |
bhavata iti śeṣaḥ |
^bhuvanānāṃ bhūtānām ^abhiśriyā abhiśrayaṇīye^ bhavata iti sarvatrānusaṃdheyam |
^urvī vistīrṇe ^pṛthivī bahukāryarūpeṇa prathite ca ^madhudughe madhuna udakasya ^dogdhryau ^supeśasā surūpe ^varuṇasya sarvasya niyāmakasya ^dharmaṇā dhāraṇe^ ^viṣkabhite pṛthagdhārite ^ajare nitye ^bhūriratasā bahuretaske bahukāryai^ vā bhavataḥ^|
atra sākṣāt dyāvāpṛthivyoḥ stutiḥ prasaṅgāt varuṇasyeti draṣṭavyam ||
asaṃścantī bhūridhāre paryasvatī ghṛtaṃ duhāte sukṛte śucivrate |
rājantī asya bhuvanasya rodasī asme retaḥ siñcataṃ yanmanurhitam ||
2 ||
asaṃścantī iti | bhūridhāre iti bhūri'dhāre | paryasvatī iti | ghṛtam | duhāte iti | sukṛte | śucivrate iti śuci'vrate | rājantī iti | asya | bhuvanasya | rodasī iti | asme iti | retaḥ | siñcatam | yat | manuḥ'hitam || 2 ||
^asaṃścantī amajyamāne vyudasyantyau vā ^bhūridhāre bahudhāre |
divo vṛṣṭidhārāḥ pṛthivyāścāhvayu- torasadhārāḥ |
evamubhayorapi bahudhāratvam^ |
^payasvatī udakavatyau |
tathā ca yāskaḥ - ‘asajya- māne iti vā vyudasyantyāviti vā bahudhāre udakavatyau’ (niru. 5.2) iti |
^śucivrate śucikriye dyāvāpṛthivyau ^sukṛte śobhanakāriṇe yajamānāya ^ghṛtaṃ sasyādisamṛddhihetumudakaṃ ^duhāte |
atha pratyakṣastutiḥ |
^rodasī he dyāvāpṛthivyau ^asya ^bhuvanasya bhūtajātasya ^rājantī īśāne yuvām ^asme ^retaḥ prajananasamarthaṃ vīryaṃ ^siñcatam |
^yat retaḥ ^manuhitaṃ manuṣyebhyo hitam ||
yo vāmṛjave karmaṇāya rodasī matoṃ dudarśa dhiṣaṇe sa sādhati |
pra prajābhirjāyate dharmaṇaspari yuvoḥ siktā viṣurūpāṇi sarvatā ||
3 ||
yaḥ | vām | ṛjave | karmaṇāya | rodasī iti | matau | dudarśa | dhiṣaṇe iti | saḥ | sādhati | pra | prajābhiḥ | jāyate | dharmaṇaḥ | pari | yuvoḥ | siktā | viṣurūpāṇi | sa'vratā ||3||
^dhiṣaṇe sṛṣṭe sarvasya bhuvanasya nivāsabhūte vā he ^rodasī ^vāṃ yuvābhyāṃ ^yaḥ ^martaḥ martyaḥ ^ṛjave ^karmaṇāya yuvayoḥ sukhagamanāya ^dadāśa havīṃṣi dadāti ^saḥ martaḥ ^sādhati^ kāmān sādhayati |
kiṃca ^prajābhiḥ putrapautrādibhiḥ ^pra ^jāyate pravṛddho bhavati |
^dharmaṇaḥ karmaṇaḥ ^pari upari ^yuvoḥ yuvayoḥ ^siktā siktāni retāṃsi ^viṣurūpāṇi nānāvarṇāni ^savratā samāna- karmāṇi bhūtāni jāyante ||
1. kha-gha-jha-na-bha-śa-abhiśrayaṇāyena | 2. ya-dhārakaṃ | 3. ya-bahukāryaiti bhāvaḥ | 4. kha-jha-na bha-bahudhāram; śa-bahudhāraṃ tvam | 5. kha-jha-na-bha-sa sādhati |
ghṛtena dyāvāpṛthivī abhīvṛte ghṛtaśriyāṃ ghṛtapṛcā ghṛtāvṛdhā | urvī pṛthivī hotṛvūryai purohite te idimrā īḻate summiṣṭaye || 4 || ghṛtena | dyāvāpṛthivī iti | abhīvṛte ityabhi'vṛte | ghṛta'śriyāṃ | ghṛta'pṛcā | ghṛta'vṛdhā | urvī iti | pṛthivī iti | hotṛvūryai | purohite iti puraḥ'hite | te iti | it | viprāḥ | īḻate | sumnam | iṣṭaye || 4 || ᵥdyāvāpṛthivī dyāvāpṛthivyau ᵥghṛtena udakena ᵥabhīvṛte āvṛte bhavataḥ | ᵥghṛtaśriyāṃ udakaṃ śrayantyau ca ᵥghṛtapṛcā ghṛtasaṃpṛkte ca ᵥghṛtāvṛdhā ghṛtaṃ vardhayantyau ca ᵥurvī vistīrṇe ᵥpṛthivī prathite ca bhavataḥ | ᵥhotṛvūryai yajñe ᵥpurohite yajamānaiḥ puraskṛte ca bhavataḥ | ᵥviprāḥ prājñāḥ ᵥstotāraḥ ᵥte ᵥit dyāvāpṛthivyāveva ᵥiṣṭaye yajñāya | 'hotrā iṣṭiḥ' iti yajñanāmasu pāṭhāt | ᵥsumnaṃ sukham ᵥīḻate yācante | sukhe sati hi yāgāḥ pravartante ||
madhu no dyāvāpṛthivī mimikṣatāṃ madhuśrutā madhudughe madhuvrate | dadhāne yajñaṃ draviṇaṃ ca devatā mahi śravo vājamasme suvīryam || 5 || madhu | naḥ | dyāvāpṛthivī iti | mimikṣatām | madhu'śrutā | madhudughe iti madhu'dughe | madhuvrate iti madhu'vrate | dadhāne iti | yajñam | draviṇam | ca | devatā | mahi | śravaḥ | vājam | asme iti | suvīryam || 5 || ᵥmadhuśrutā udakasya kṣārayitryau ᵥmadhudughe udakadogdhyau ᵥmadhuvrate udakāryakarmaṇau ᵥdevatā devatābhūte ᵥasme asmāsu ᵥyajñaṃ ᵥdraviṇaṃ dhanaṃ ca ᵥmahi mahat ᵥśravaḥ yaśaḥ ᵥvājam annaṃ ᵥsuvīryaṃ suvīryatvaṃ ca ᵥdadhāne dadadhyau ᵥdyāvāpṛthivī dyāvāpṛthivyau ᵥnaḥ asmān ᵥmadhu madhunā || tṛtīye- kavacanasya luk || ᵥmimikṣatāṃ siñcatām ||
ūrjaṃ no dyauśca pṛthivī ca pinvatāṃ pitā mātā viśvavidā sudaṃsasā | saṃrarāṇe rodasī viśvaśaṃbhuvā saniṃ vājaṃ rayimasme sarmanvatām || 6 || ūrjam | naḥ | dyauḥ | ca | pṛthivī | ca | pinvatām | pitā | mātā | viśvavidā | sudaṃsasā | saṃrarāṇe iti sam'rarāṇe | rodasī iti | viśvaśaṃbhuvā | sanim | vājam | rayim | asme iti | sam | invatām || 6 ||
1. kha-gha-jña-na-bha-śa-apavṛte | 2. kha-jha-ta-na-bha-śa-ghṛtadhrā | 3. jha-hotṛvūryai hotābhirvarṇanīye | 4. kha-jha-na-bha-prajāḥ |
^pitā varṣasya ^sektṛtvena pitṛbhūtā ^dyauśca ^mātā dhārakatvena mātṛbhūtā ^pṛthivī ^ca ubhe ^na: asmabhyam ^ūrjam annaṃ ^pinvatāṃ yacchatām|

14 ||
‘udu pya deva:’ iti ṣaṭkaṃ daśamaṃ sūktaṃ bharadvājasyārṣaṃ savitṛdevatākam|
ādyāstisro jagattyaścaturthādyāstisrastriṣṭubha:|
tathā cānukrāntam—‘udu pya sāvitraṃ tristriṣṭudantam’ iti|
sūkta- viniyogo laiṅgika:|
cāturviṃśike'hani vaiśvadevaśastre ādyastṛca: sāvitrasūktasthāna āvapanīya:|
sūtritaṃ ca—‘udu pya deva: savitā hiraṇyayeti tisraste hi dyāvāpṛthivī’ (āśva. śrau. 7. 4) iti|
bṛhaspatisave'pi vaiśvadevaśastre sāvitrasūktasthāna eṣa tṛca:|
sūtritaṃ ca—‘udu pya deva: savitā hiraṇyayetyanuttiṣṭheta’ (āśva. śrau. 9. 5) iti|
nūḻahe pañcame'hani vaiśvadevaśastre ‘udu pya deva: savitā damūnā:’ iti tṛca: sāvitranividdānārtha:|
sūtritaṃ ca—‘udu pya deva: savitā damūnā iti tisra:’ (āśva. śrau. 8. 8) iti|
vaiśvadevaparvaṇi sāvitradvādaśakapālasya ‘vāmamadya’ iti yājyā|
sūtritaṃ ca—‘vāmamadya savitarvāmasu śva: pūpantava vate vayam’ (āśva. śrau. 2. 16) iti||
udu pya deva: savitā hiraṇyayī bāhū ayaṃsata sarvanāya sukratu: |
ghṛtena pāṇī abhi pṛṇuṣte mukho yuvā sudakṣo rajaso vidharmaṇi ||
1 ||
uta | oṃ iti | sya: | deva: | savitā | hiraṇyayī | bāhū iti | ayaṃsata | sarvanāya | mu'kratu: | ghṛtena | pāṇī iti | abhi | pṛṇuṣte | mukha: | yuvā | su'dakṣa: | rajasa: | vi'dharmaṇi ||1|| ^deva: dyotamāna: ^sukratu: sukarmā ^sya: sa prasiddha: ^savitā ^hiraṇyayā hiraṇmayau ātmīyau ^bāhū ^savanāya suvanāya dānāya vā ^ut ^ayaṃsata udayacchati| kiṃca ^makha: maṃhanīya: ^yuvā nityataruṇa: ^sudakṣa: suprajña: ^rajasa: lokarayodhakasya vā ^vidharmaṇi vidharaṇe sthita: ^ghṛtena udakena pūrṇau svau ^pāṇī ^abhi ^pṛṇuṣte abhiprerayati ||
devasya vayaṃ saṃvitu: savīmani śreṣṭhe syām vasunnaśca dāvanai |
yo viśvasya dvipado yaścatuṣpado niveśane prasave cāsi bhūmana: ||

1. kha-jha-na-bha-śa-varṣasya |
2. kha-jha-ta-na-bha-śa- ‘viśvabhuvā...bhāvayitrī’ nāsti |
3. kha-jha- na-bha-śa-triṣṭubantamiti |
adabdhebhiḥ savitaḥ pāyulibhiḥ śivebhiradya pari pāhi no gayam | hiraṇyajihvaḥ suvitāya navyase rakṣā mākino aghaśaṃsa īśata || 3 || adabdhebhiḥ | suvitariti | pāyulibhiḥ | tvam | śivebhiḥ | adya | pari | pāhi | naḥ | gayam | hiraṇya'jihvaḥ | suvitāya | navyase | rakṣa | mākinaḥ | agha'śaṃsaḥ | īśata || 3 ||
he savitaḥ adabdhebhiḥ ahiṃsitaiḥ pāyulibhiḥ tejobhiḥ rakṣaṇasādhanairvā śivebhiḥ sukha- karaiḥ naḥ asmākaṃ gayaṃ gṛhaṃ dhanaṃ vā |
' gayaḥ kudaraḥ ' iti gṛhanāmasu ' mīḻahaṃ gayaḥ ' iti dhananāmasu pāṭhāt |
adya pari pāhi rakṣa |
hiraṇyajihvaḥ hitaramaṇīyavāk svam |
tathā ca yāskaḥ—' hiraṇyaṃ kasmāt ' ityupakramya ' hitaramaṇaṃ bhavatīti vā ' ( niru. 2.10 ) iti |
suvitāya sukhāya navyase navatarāya bhava |
rakṣa asmān pālaya ca |
naḥ asmākam aghaśaṃsaḥ anarthamāśaṃsamānaḥ śatruḥ mākiḥ īśata medhata ||
udu ṣya devaḥ savitā damūnā hiraṇyapāṇiḥ pratidoṣamasthāt | ayohanuryajato mandrajihva ā dāśuṣe suvati bhūri vāmam || 4 || ut | ṣya | devaḥ | savitā | damūnāḥ | hiraṇya'pāṇiḥ | prati'doṣam | asthāt | ayaḥ'hanuḥ | yajataḥ | mandra'jihvaḥ | ā | dāśuṣe | suvati | bhūri | vāmam || 4 ||
devaḥ dyotamānaḥ damūnāḥ damamanāḥ dānamanā vā |

hiraṇyapāṇiḥ hiraṇmayapāṇiḥ ayohanuḥ hiraṇmayahanuḥ |
' rukmam ayaḥ ' iti hiraṇyanāmasu pāṭhāt |

patayat gacchat kaccit yatkiṃcit avaṃ mahat sarvaṃ vastujātaṃ tirohitam arīramata ramayati ca ||

‘indrāsomā mahi’ iti pañcamekādaśaṃ sūktaṃ bharadvājasyārṣaṃ ṛṣṭubhamindrāsomadevatākam |
anukramyate ca- ‘indrāsomā pañcandrāsomam’ iti |
sūktaviniyogaḥ laiṅgikaḥ ||

yuvaṃ sūryyaṃ vividathuryyuvaṃ svarvviśvā tamāṃsyahataṃ nidaśca ||
1 ||
indrāsomā | mahi | tat | vām | mahitvam | yuvam | mahāni | prathamāni | cakrathuḥ | yuvam | sūryyam | vividathuḥ | yuvam | svaḥ | viśvā | tamāṃsi | ahatam | nidaḥ | ca || 1 ||

kiṃ tadityata āha |
yuvaṃ yuvāṃ mahāni mahānti prathamāni prathamāni mukhyāni vā bhūtāni |
‘prathama iti mukhyanāma pratamau³’ bhavati ’ ( niru. 2.22 ) iti yāskaḥ |
cakrathuḥ akārṣṭam |

2 ||

api ca dyāṃ

ābhilavikeṣūpatyeṣu tṛtīyasavane brāhmaṇācchaṃsinaḥ stomāti- śaṃsanārthamidaṃ sūktam |
sūyate hi—‘yo adribhittre diva iti sūkte’ (āśva. śrau. 7.9) iti |
vājapeye bārhaspatye caro ‘bṛhaspatiḥ samajayadvasūnīti - - - tvāmīḻyate ajiraṃ dūḻyāya’ (āśva. śrau. 9.9) iti ||
yo adribhitprathamajā ṛtāvā bṛhaspatirāṅgiraso haviṣmān |
dvibarhajmā prāgharmasatpitā na ā rodasī vṛṣabho roravīti ||
1 ||
yaḥ | adri'bhit | prathama'jāḥ | ṛta'vā | bṛhaspatiḥ | āṅgirasaḥ | haviṣmān | dvibarha'jmā | prāgharma'sat | pitā | naḥ | ā | rodasī iti | vṛṣabhaḥ | roravīti || 1 ||
yaḥ |
bṛhaspatiḥ |
adribhiḥ paṇibhirviracitānāṃ śiloccayānāṃ bhettā meghānāṃ vidārako vā |
‘adriḥ prāvaḥ’ iti meghanāmasu pāṭhāt |
prathamajāḥ prajāpatervīryāt prathamaṃ jātaḥ |
ṛtāvā satyavān udakavān vā |
āṅgirasaḥ paścājjātaḥ |
tathā ca nigamaḥ—‘ye'ṅgirasa āsaste- 'ṅgiraso'bhavandyaṅgirasaḥ punarvaśāntā udadīvyanta tat bṛhaspatirabhavat’ (ai. brā. 3. 34) iti |
haviṣmān yajñabhāgavān |
dvibarhajmā dvayorlokayorvṛṣṭihitagamanaḥ |
prāgharmasat prakarṣeṇa dīrasthāne vartamānaḥ |
naḥ asmākaṃ pitā pālayitā bhavati sa bṛhaspatiḥ |
vṛṣabhaḥ varṣakaḥ san |
rodasī dyāvāpṛthivyau ā roravīti abhijarjeti ||
janāya cidya īrvata u lokaṃ bṛhaspatirdevahūtī cakāra |
anṛtrāṇi vi puro dardarīti jayanchatrū~ramitrampṛtsu sāhen ||
2 ||
janāya | cit | yaḥ | īrvate | ūṃ iti | lokam | bṛhaspatiḥ | deva'hūtau | cakāra | ghnan | vṛtrāṇi | vi | puraḥ | dardarīti | jayan | śatrūn | amitrān | pṛtsu | sahan || 2 ||
api ca yaḥ |
bṛhaspatiḥ |
devahūtau yajñe vartamānāya īrvate upagacchate stotre janāya lokaṃ sthānaṃ cakāra karoti |
cit iti pūraṇaḥ |
saḥ vṛtrāṇi āvarakāṇi tamāṃsi ghnan nivārayan |
pṛtsu yuddheṣu śatrūn jayan amitrān sahan abhibhavan puraḥ āsurīḥ purīḥ |
vi dardarīti bhṛśaṃ vidārayati ||
bṛhaspatiḥ samajayadvasūni maho vrajān gomato deva eṣaḥ |
apaḥ siṣāsantsarpatītau bṛhaspatirhantyamitramarkaiḥ ||
3 ||
bṛhaspatiḥ | sam | ajayat | vasūni | mahaḥ | vrajān | go'mataḥ | devaḥ | eṣaḥ | apaḥ | siṣāsan | svaḥ | aprati'itaḥ | bṛhaspatiḥ | hanti | amitram | arkaiḥ || 3 ||
devaḥ dyotamānaḥ | eṣaḥ prasiddhaḥ | bṛhaspatiḥ | vasūni paṇīnāmasurāṇāṃ valasya vā dhanāni mahaḥ mahataḥ | gomataḥ gosahitān vrajān ca samajayat | tathā ca nigamau—‘bṛhaspatirgovapuṣo
balasya nirmaṃjñānaṃ na parvaṇo jabhāra ' (ṛ. saṃ. 10. 68. 9) iti, ' dūramita vaṇayo varīya uddāvau yantu minanīrṛtena |
bṛhaspatiryā avindajjigṛhvāḥ somo grāvāṇa ṛpayaśca viprāḥ ' (ṛ. saṃ. 10. 108. 11) iti |
kiṃca bṛhaspatiḥ apratīnaḥ kenāpyapratigataḥ sipāsan saṃbhtukāmaḥ svaḥ svargasya amitram asuram akeṃḥ arcanasādhanairmantraiḥ hanti |
' arko mantro bhavati yadenenārcanti ' (niru. 5. 4) iti yāskaḥ ||
17 ||
' somārudrā ' iti caturṛcaṃ trayodaśaṃ sūktaṃ bharadvājasyārṣaṃ somarudradevatākam |
anukramyate ca— ' somārudrā caturṛcaṃ somāraudram ' iti |
viniyogo laiṅgikaḥ ||
somārudrā dhārayethāmasuryaṃ pra vāṃ miṣṭayo'raṃmaśruvantu |
darmedamase sṛta ratnā dadhānā śaṃ nau bhūtaṃ dvipade śaṃ catuṣpade ||
1 ||
somārudrā | dhārayethām | asuryam | pra | vām | iṣṭayaḥ | aram | aśruvantu | darme'dame | sṛta | ratnā | dadhānā | śam | naḥ | bhūtam | dvi'pade | śam | catuḥ'pade ||1||
somārudrā he somārudrā asuryam asurasaṃvandhi balamasmāsu dhārayethām |
kiṃca dame'dame sarvvasmin gṛheṣu iṣṭayaḥ yajñāḥ vāṃ yuvām aram paryāptaṃ pra aśruvantu prakarṣeṇa vyāpnuvantu |
api ca yuvāṃ sṛta ratnā ratnāni dadhānā dhārayantau naḥ asmabhyaṃ śaṃ sukhakarau sukharūpau vā bhūtaṃ bhavatam |
dvipade asmadīyāya putrāye catuṣpade paśvādye ca śaṃ bhūtaṃ bhavatam ||
somārudrā vi vṛhataṃ viṣūcīmīmāṃ yā no garyamāviveśa |
āre bādhethāṃ nirṛtiṃ parācairasme bhadrā sauśravasārni santu ||
2 ||
somārudrā | vi | vṛhatam | viṣūcīm | amītrā | yā | naḥ | garyam | ā'viveśa | āre | bādhethām | niḥ'ṛtim | parācaiḥ | asme iti | bhadrā | sauśravasārni | santu ||2||
somārudrā he somārudrā naḥ asmākaṃ garya gṛhaṃ gṛhagataṃ prāṇijātaṃ vā yā amītrā yo rogaḥ āviveśa tāṃ triyūcīṃ viṣvak sarvvato gacchantīmīmāṃ vi vṛhataṃ viyojayatam |
api ca nirṛtim alakṣmīṃ parācaiḥ parāgatā bhavati tathā āre dūre bādhethāṃ nivārayatam |
asme asmākaṃ bhadrā bhadrāṇi sauśravasārni annāni yaśāṃsi ca santu bhavantu |
suśravaso bhāvaḥ sauśravasam |
śrava'iti yaśaśca ||
somārudrā yuvametānyusme viśvā tanūṣu bheṣajāni dhattam |
ava syataṃ muñcataṃ yacco asti tanūṣu baddhaṃ kṛtameno asmāt ||
3 ||
somārudrā | yuvam | etāni | asme iti | viśvā | tanūṣu | bheṣajāni | dhattam | ava | syatam | muñcatam | yat | ca | asti | tanūṣu | baddham | kṛtam | enaḥ | asmāt ||3||
1. kha-jha-na-bha-śa- ' ratnā ' nāsti |
ma. 6 a. 6. sū. 75 ] pañcamo'ṣṭakaḥ 255
he somarudrā somarudrau yuvaṃ yuvām asme asmākaṃ tanūṣu aṅgeṣu etāni prasiddhāni viśvā sarvāṇi bheṣajāni dhatta dhārayatam |
kiṃca naḥ asmākaṃ tanūṣu aṅgeṣu asmābhiḥ kṛtaṃ baddhaṃ ca yat enaḥ pāpam asti vidyate tat pāpam ava syataṃ kṣapayataṃ śrathayatam |
śithilabandhaṃ kurutamityarthaḥ |
asmat asmattaḥ sumnataṃ ca ||
tigmāyudhau tigmahetī suśevau somorudrāviha su mṛḻataṃ naḥ |
pra nau muñcataṃ varuṇasya pāśādropayataṃ naḥ sumanasyamānā ||
4 ||
tigma'āyudhau | tigmahetī iti tigma'hetī | mu'śevau | somorudrau | iha | su | mṛḻatam | naḥ | pra | naḥ | muñcatam | varuṇasya | pāśāt | gopāyatam | naḥ | su'manasyamānā || 4 ||
he somarudrau tigmāyudhau dīptadhanuṣko tigmahetī tīkṣṇaśastrau suśevau śobhanasukhapradau sumanasyamānā śobhanaṃ manaḥ stotramicchantau sumanasyamānau styamānau sumanasau vā santo yuvām iha loke naḥ asmān su atyantaṃ mṛḻataṃ sukhayatam |
naḥ asmān varuṇasya pāśān pra muñcatam |
naḥ asmān gopāyatam āpadyo rakṣataṃ ca ||
16 ||
'jīmūtasya' ityekonaviṃśatyṛcaṃ caturdaśaṃ sūktaṃ bhāradvājasya pāyorārṣam |
anukramyate ca- 'jīmūtasyaivakonā pāyurbhāradvājaḥ saṃgrāmāndyakṣo'bhituṣṭāva varma dhanuryāmārnī iṣudhi jagatyardhe sārathidhardhe raśmīnaśmān rathaṃ rathopān jagatyāṃ liṅgodevatā dvābhyāmiṣuḥ pranodaṃ hastaghnaṃ dvābhyāmiṣuḥ parāḥ paṅktyādayo liṅgodevatāḥ saṃgrāmāśipona'nyānuṣṭubjīna ālākteti ca dve dve' iti |
'rathe tiṣṭhan' 'brāhmaṇāsaḥ' iti ṣaṣṭhīdaśamyau jagatyau antyā 'yo naḥ svaḥ' ityeyānuṣṭup 'arjāne' 'ā jaṅghnanta' 'ālāktā' 'avasṛṣṭā' 'yo naḥ svaḥ' iti pañcānuṣṭubhaḥ 'yatra bāṇāḥ' iti paṅktiḥ śiṣṭāstripubhaḥ |
ādyayā varma stūyate, dvitīyāyā dhanuḥ, tṛtīyāyā jyā, caturthyā ātnī, pañcamyā iṣudhiḥ |
ataḥ pañcānāṃ varmādyā devatāḥ |
ṣaṣṭhyāḥ pūrvārdhena sārathiḥ stūyate |
ataḥ so'rdhastaddevatāḥ |
uttarārdhena raśmayaḥ stūyante |
saptamyā aśvāḥ, aṣṭamyā rathaḥ, navamyā rathagopāḥ |
atamtāstaddevatāḥ |
'brāhmaṇāsaḥ' ityasyāṃ brāhmaṇapitṛmāyavāvāpṛthivīpūpaṇo liṅgodevatāḥ |
ekādaśīdvādaśyau iṣustutirūpatvāt iṣu- devatāke |
trayodaśyā pratodaḥ |
caturdaśyā hastaghnaḥ |
pañcadaśīṣoḍaśyau iṣudevatāke |
sapta- daśyāḥ yuddhabhūmibrahmaṇaspatyadinirūpā liṅgodevatāḥ |
aṣṭādaśyāḥ kavacasomavaruṇā devatāḥ |
antyāyā devā brahma ca devatāḥ |
yuddhe anena sūktena rājānaṃ saṃnāhayet |
tathā cāśva- lāyanaḥ- 'saṃgrāme samupoḻahe rājānaṃ saṃnāhayedā tvāhārpamantaredhīti paścādathasyāvasthāyā jīmūta- syeva bhavati pratīkamiti kavacaṃ prayacchet uttarayā dhanuruttarāṃ vācayetsvayaṃ caturthīṃ jayetpañca- myepudhiṃ prayacchedabhimravatamane ṣaṣṭhīṃ saptamyāścāṣṭamīmipūnvekṣamāṇaṃ vācayedyahaviriva bhogaiḥ paśyati bāhumiti talaṃ nahyamānam' (āśva. gṛ. 3.12) iti |
tathā ca 'avasṛṣṭā parā patetīpu- nvimarjayedyatra bāṇāḥ saṃpatantīti yudhyamāneṣu japet saṃśipyāddhā' (āśva. gṛ. 3.12) iti |
adhyāyopākaraṇasarjanayormaṇḍalāntahīme 'yo naḥ svaḥ' ityepā (āśva. gṛ. 3.5.7) ||
1 kha-gha-jha-ta-na-bha-śa- 'kṣapayataṃ' nāsti |
2 ya-mṛḻayataṃ; śa-mṛḻataṃ mṛḻayatam |
3 kha- jagatyādyā liṅgoktaḥ; śa-jagatyādyā liṅgoktā devatāḥ |
4 kha-jha-ta-na-bha-śa-stro araṇā |
5 kha-jha- ta-na-bha-so'rdhasta |
6 kha-jha-ta-na-bha-śa-devatā |
jīmūtasyeva bhavati pratīkaṃ yadmī yāti samadāmupasthe |
anāviddhayā tanvā jaya tvaṃ sa tvā varmeṇa mahimā pipartu ||
1 ||
jīmūtasya'iva | bhavati | pratīkam | yat | vṛmī | yāti | mu'madām | upasthe | anāviddhayā | tanvā | jaya | tvam | saḥ | tvā | varmaḥ | mahimā | pipartu || 1 ||
^samadāṃ saṃgrāmānām ^upasthe upasthāne sati ^yat yadā ayaṃ rājā ^varmā kavacī ^yāti tadā lohamayena varmaṇā saṃnaddhasya rājñaḥ ^pratīkaṃ rūpaṃ ^jīmūtasyeva meghasyeva ^bhavati |
meghasya ^yādṛśaṃ rūpaṃ tādṛśasya bhavatītyarthaḥ |
he rājan ^tvam ^anāviddhayā śatrubhirbādhitayā ^tanvā śarīreṇa śatrūn ^jaya |
^varmaṇaḥ ^saḥ tādṛśaḥ ^mahimā ^tvā tvāṃ ^pipartu pālayatu ||
dhanvanā gā dhanvanājiṃ jayema dhanvanā tīvrāḥ samadau jayema |
dhanuḥ śatrorapakāmaṃ kṛṇoti dhanvanā sarvāḥ pradiśo jayema ||
2 ||
dhanvanā | gāḥ | dhanvanā | ājim | jayema | dhanvanā | tīvrāḥ | su'madaḥ | jayema | dhanuḥ | śatroḥ | apa'kāmam | kṛṇoti | dhanvanā | sarvāḥ | pra'diśaḥ | jayema || 2 ||
^dhanvanā cāpena ^gāḥ śatrūṇāṃ ^jayema vayaṃ vaśīkaravāma |
^dhanvanā ^ājiṃ saṃgrāmaṃ ca jayema |
^dhanvanā ^tīvrāḥ uddhatāḥ ^samadaḥ atyantaṃ^2 madavatīḥ ^śatrusenaḥśca ^jayema |
tathā ca yāskaḥ - ‘samadaḥ samado vāttaiḥ saṃmado vā madateḥ ’ (niru. 9.17) iti |
^dhanuḥ ^śatroḥ ^apakāmaṃ kāmasyāpāyaṃ ^kṛṇoti karoti |
kiṃca ^dhanvanā vayaṃ ^sarvāḥ ^pradiśaḥ sarvāsu dikṣu vartamānān śatrūn ^jayema |
pradikśabdo lakṣaṇayā tasyeṣu puruṣeṣu vartate magnāḥ krośantī- tivan |
dhanvañśabdasya jayateścāvṛttirādarārthā ||
vakṣyantīvedā ganīganti karṇe priyaṃ sakhāyaṃ pariṣasvajānā |
yoṣeva śiṅakte vitatādhi dhanvañjyā iyaṃ samane pārayantī ||
3 ||
vakṣyantī'iva | it | ā | ganīganti | karṇe | priyam | sakhāyam | pari'svajānā | yoṣā'iva | śiṅakte | vi'tatā | adhi | dhanvan | jyā | iyam | samane | pārayantī ||3||
pūrvatra ṛgadvayena kavacadhanuṣī stute |
atra jyāstutiḥ |
^iyaṃ ^jyā ^samane saṃgrāme ^dhanvan dhanvani |
^adhi iti saptamyarthānuvādaḥ |
^vitatā vistṛtā ^pārayantī pāraṃ nayantī ^priyaṃ priyakaraṃ vākyaṃ ^vakṣyantīva ^karṇe dhanvino rājñaḥ karṇapradeśam ^ā ^ganīganti āgacchati |
^it iti pūraṇaḥ |
^yoṣā nārī ^sakhāyaṃ patim ^iva ^pariṣasvajānā iṣuṃ pariṣvajamānā ^śiṅakte śabdāyate ca |
tathā ca yāskaḥ - ‘vakṣyantīvāgacchati karṇe priyamiba sakhāyamiṣuṃ pariṣvajamānā yoṣeva śiṅakte śabdaṃ karoti vitatādhi dhanuṣi jyayaṃ samane saṃgrāme pārayantī pāraṃ nayantī ’ (niru. 9.18) iti ||

ye rathe raśmayaḥ aśvagrīvābhyāṃ vyāpya vartamānāḥ pāśāḥ paścāt rathasya pṛṣṭhabhāge vidyamānāḥ aśvān manaḥ sārathermano'nukūlam anu yacchanti niyacchanti |

ṛjiti pari vṛṅdhi no'śmā bhavatu nastanūḥ | somo adhi bravītu no'ditiḥ śarma yacchatu || 12 || ṛjīte | pari | vṛṅdhi | naḥ | aśmā | bhavatu | naḥ | tanūḥ | somaḥ | adhi | bravītu | naḥ | aditiḥ | śarma | yacchatu || 12 || vṛjīte | ṛju gacchatīti ṛjītiḥ iṣuḥ | he iṣo vnaḥ asmān vpari vvṛṅdhi parito vardhaya | vnaḥ asmākaṃ vtanūḥ śarīram vaśmā vbhavatu | aśmavadabhedyā dṛḍhā bhavatu | kiṃca vnaḥ asmabhyaṃ vsomo vadhi vbravītu pakṣapātena bravītu | vaditiḥ api vśarma sukhaṃ vyacchatu ||
ā jaghnanti sānveṣāṃ jaghanā~ upa jighnate | aśvājani pracetaso'śvā ntsamatsu codaya || 13 || ā | jaghnanti | sānu | eṣām | jaghanān | upa | jighnate | aśva'ajani | pracetaḥ | aśvān | samatsu | codaya || 13 || vaśvājani aśvānāṃ kṣetri kaśe yathā tvayā vpracetasaḥ prakṛṣṭajñānāḥ sādinaḥ sārathayo vā veṣām aśvānāṃ vsānu sānūni sakthīni vā vjaghnanti āghnanti vjaghanān jaghanāni ca vupa vjighnate upajighnanti sā tvaṃ vsamatsu saṃgrāmeṣu vaśvān vcodaya preraya | tathā ca yāskaḥ—‘āghnanti sānunyeṣāṃ saraṇāni sakthīni | sakthi sacaterāsakto'smin kāyaḥ | jaghanāni copaghnanti | jaghanaṃ jaghnyate | aśvājani pracetaḥ pravṛddhacetaso'śvān samatsu samaraṇeṣu saṃgrāmeṣu codaya ’ (nigha. 9. 20) iti ||
ahiriva bhogaiḥ paryaiti bāhuṃ jyāyā hetiṃ paribādhamānaḥ | hastaghno viśvā vayunāni vidvānpumānpumā~saṃ pari pātu viśvataḥ || 14 || ahiḥ'iva | bhogaiḥ | pari | eti | bāhum | jyāyāḥ | hetim | pari'bādhamānaḥ | hasta'ghnaḥ | viśvā | vayunāni | vidvān | pumān | pumāṃsam | pari | pātu | viśvataḥ || 14 || atra dhanvanaḥ prakoṣṭhe bandhanīyasya hastaghnasya stutiḥ | vhastaghnaḥ | haste hastasamīpe vartani prakoṣṭhe sthitaḥ san jyā hanyate iti hastaghnaḥ || ‘ghnarthe kavidhānam’ iti kaḥ || vjyāyāḥ vhetiṃ jyāhetukāṃ hiṃsāṃ vparibādhamānaḥ nivārayan vbhogaiḥ śarīreṇa | vacanavyatyayaḥ | vahiriva sarpa iva vbāhuṃ prakoṣṭham | lakṣaṇayā bāhuśabdaḥ prakoṣṭhe vartate | vparyaiti pariveṣṭayati | kiṃca vviśvā viśvāni vvayunāni jñānāni jñātavyāni | ‘māyā vayunam’ iti tannāmasu pāṭhāt | vvidvān vjādan vpumān pauruṣopetaḥ svayaṃ vpumāṃsaṃ dhanvanaṃ vviśvataḥ sarvataḥ vpari vpātu rakṣati | tathā ca yāskaḥ— ‘ahiriva bhogaiḥ pariveṣṭayati bāhuṃ jyāyā vadhātparidhāyamāṇo hastaghnaḥ sarvāṇi prajñānāni prajānan pumāpumān bhavati puṃsatervā ’ (nigha. 9. 15) iti ||
1. kha-no varjya; jha-na-bha-no vṛddhaya |
2. kha-gha-sa-ta-na-ma-śa-vartamānaḥ |
3. kha-jha-sa-na-bha-śa-vidvān jānan pumān pumanā bhavati puṃsatervā iti yāskaḥ |
bahumanaskaḥ pumāṃsaṃ dhanvanaṃ viśvataḥ sarvataḥ paripātu rakṣatu |
ālāktā yā ruruśīrṣṇayo yasyā ayo mukham | idaṃ parjanyaretas iṣvai devyai bṛhannamaḥ || 15 || ālā'aktā | yā | ruru'śīrṣṇā | atho iti | yasyāḥ | ayaḥ | mukham | idam | parjanya'retase | iṣvai | devyai | bṛhat | namaḥ || 15 ||

arvasṛṣṭā parā pata śaranye brahmasaṃśite | gacchāmitrān padhasva māmīṣāṃ kaṃ canocchiṣaḥ || 16 || arva'sṛṣṭā | parā | pata | śaranye | brahma'saṃśite | gaccha | amitrān | pra | padyasva | mā | amīṣām | kam | cana | ut | śiṣaḥ || 16 ||
∇brahmasaṃśite mantreṇa tīkṣṇīkṛte ∇śaranye hiṃsākuśale iṣo tvam ∇avasṛṣṭā kṣiptā ∇parā ∇pata parāgaccha |

yatra bāṇāḥ saṃpatanti kumārā viṃśikhā iva | tatrā no brahmaṇaspatiraditiḥ śarma yacchatu viśvāhā śarma yacchatu || 17 || yatra | bāṇāḥ | sam'patanti | kumārāḥ | viṃśikhāḥ'iva | tatra | naḥ | brahmaṇaḥ | patiḥ | aditiḥ | śarma | yacchatu | viśvāhā | śarma | yacchatu ||17||
∇yatra yuddhe ∇kumārā ∇viṃśikhā iva muṇḍitā iva ∇bāṇāḥ ∇saṃpatanti ∇tatra ∇naḥ asmabhyaṃ ∇brahmaṇaspatiḥ ∇śarma sukhaṃ ∇viśvāhā sarvadā ∇yacchatu |

marmāṇi te varmaṇā chādayāmi somastva| rājāmṛtenānu vastām | urorvarīyo varuṇaste kṛṇotu jayantaṃ tvānu devā madantu || 18 || marmāṇi | te | varmaṇā | chādayāmi | somaḥ | tvā | rājā | amṛtena | anu | vastām | uroḥ | varīyaḥ | varuṇaḥ | te | kṛṇotu | jayantam | tvā | anu | devāḥ | madantu || 18 ||
he rājan ∇te tvadīyāni ∇marmāṇi |
